Existing optimizations rely heavily on packing-specific algorithms, limiting their general applicability.
  Targeting this overhead from a packing-independent perspective, we propose a preprocessing-assisted method for secure attention computation. By decomposing attention into precomputable operations and online interactions, this method reduces online inference-phase key switching without modifying existing packing strategies.
  However, the first method shifting key switching offline introduces additional storage requirements. To address this, we propose storage-communication trade-off techniques that replace large precomputed ciphertexts with modest online communication, enabling flexible deployment under varying resource constraints.
  While ciphertext-ciphertext matrix multiplication is offloaded to the preprocessing phase in hybrid schemes and the first layer of FHE-based schemes, these operations still persist in the offline stage and subsequent FHE layers. To further optimize it, we propose a fused key-switch technique targeting the multiplication-followed-by-rotation pattern, which frequently arises in existing RNS-CKKS matrix multiplication schemes. By combining relinearization and rotation into a single procedure, this technique reduces the associated computation costs.
  Analytical evaluations demonstrate that our proposed techniques significantly reduce online key-switch overhead and provide flexible trade-offs between storage and communication without requiring modifications to existing packing strategies.

</details>


### [135] [Representation Matters: An Empirical Study of Program Representations for LLM Vulnerability Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25356)
*Andrew Stoltman,Johnathan Tang,Haipeng Cai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过RepBench基准测试发现，在LLM漏洞检测中，基于静态分析的结构化表示（特别是AST+PDG组合）相比原始源代码能显著提升推理准确率（83.2% vs 53.5%），并揭示了"上下文稀释效应"——添加原始源代码反而会降低模型对漏洞相关证据的注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的LLM漏洞检测方法大多直接提供原始源代码，但程序结构和语义应该如何表示以利于LLM推理仍不清楚。本文旨在挑战这一隐含假设，探究不同程序表示对LLM漏洞推理能力的影响。

Method: 构建RepBench基准测试，将107个真实C/C++漏洞测试用例（覆盖5个CWE类别）转换为多种表示：原始源代码、AST、CFG、PDG及其组合，以及增强PDG（ePDG）。在固定Chain-of-Thought和结构化输出协议下评估10种表示变体，并额外加入19个ePDG用例。

Result: AST+PDG组合准确率最高达83.2%，原始源代码仅53.5%；纯图提示在家族级别上优于纯源代码和源代码+图提示，且需要更少的提示开销。揭示了"上下文稀释效应"：添加原始源代码到紧凑的结构化图证据中会降低推理性能。

Conclusion: 精心选择的结构化表示比简单提供更多原始输入能带来更好的准确率-开销权衡，表明静态分析可作为面向安全LLM推理的有效提示构建层。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for automated vulnerability detection, but it remains unclear how program structure and semantics should be represented for LLM-based reasoning. Most prompting-based approaches provide raw source code, implicitly assuming that more source-level context gives the model better evidence. This paper challenges that assumption through RepBench, an empirical benchmark comparing raw source code with static-analysis-based program representations. RepBench converts real-world C/C++ vulnerability testcases into multiple representations: raw source, Abstract Syntax Trees (ASTs), Control-Flow Graphs (CFGs), Program Dependence Graphs (PDGs), their combinations, and an auxiliary track of enriched PDGs (ePDGs). Using a curated PrimeVul-derived corpus of 107 Joern-based testcases across five CWE categories, we evaluate ten representation variants under a fixed Chain-of-Thought and structured-output protocol, plus 19 additional ePDG cases generated through VulChecker/Hector. Representation choice substantially affects LLM vulnerability reasoning. The strongest variant, AST+PDG, achieves 83.2% accuracy, compared with 53.5% for raw source. At the family level, graph-only prompts outperform both source-only and source-plus-graph prompts while requiring far less prompt overhead. These results reveal a context dilution effect: adding raw source code to compact structural graph evidence can degrade reasoning by making vulnerability-relevant evidence less salient. Overall, our findings show that carefully selected structural representations offer a better accuracy-overhead tradeoff than simply giving LLMs more raw input, and suggest that static analysis can serve as an effective prompt-construction layer for security-focused LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [136] [Security and Privacy in Retrieval-Augmented Generation: Architectures, Threats, Defenses, and Future Directions for Building Trustworthy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25533)
*Balamurugan Palanisamy,G S S Chalapathi,Vikas Hassija,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该综述全面分析了检索增强生成（RAG）系统在集中式、设备端（Micro-RAG）、联邦和混合部署范式下的隐私与安全挑战，提出了涵盖检索、上下文构建和生成阶段的统一威胁分类法，系统审视了成员推断、索引推断、投毒、梯度泄露和合谋等攻击类别，并综述了架构、算法和密码学防御手段，最后指出了构建可信、安全且具有韧性的RAG系统的开放研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG已成为增强大语言模型外部知识的主流范式，但集成检索管道引入了超越传统语言模型威胁的新安全与隐私风险，现有研究缺乏对这一领域的系统性梳理。

Method: 通过综述方法，构建统一威胁分类体系，系统分析不同部署范式下的攻击类别与防御机制。

Result: 提出了涵盖检索、上下文构建和生成阶段的威胁分类法，分析了成员推断、索引推断、投毒、梯度泄露和合谋等攻击，并评估了多种防御方案及其隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: RAG系统面临多维安全与隐私威胁，现有防御方案存在隐私-效用权衡，仍需进一步研究以构建可信、安全且有韧性的RAG系统。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a dominant paradigm for enhancing large language models with external knowledge. By coupling retrieval mechanisms with generative models, RAG systems improve factual grounding and adaptability across domains. However, integrating retrieval pipelines introduces new security and privacy risks that extend beyond conventional language modeling threats. Sensitive information may be exposed through retrieval indices, query logs, context construction, or federated updates, while adversarial manipulation of knowledge bases can undermine trust in generated outputs. This survey provides a comprehensive examination of privacy and security challenges across RAG systems deployed in centralized, on-device (Micro-RAG), federated, and hybrid paradigms. We present a unified taxonomy of threat surfaces spanning the retrieval, context construction, and generation stages and systematically analyze attack classes, including membership inference, index inference, poisoning, gradient leakage, and collusion. We further review architectural, algorithmic, and cryptographic defenses, highlighting privacy-utility trade-offs and deployment considerations. Finally, we outline open research challenges toward building trustworthy, secure, and resilient RAG systems for real-world applications.

</details>


### [137] [CrypFormBench: Benchmarking Formal Analysis Capability of Large Language Models for Cryptographic Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25561)
*Zhaoxuan Li,Qionglu Zhang,Hengyuan Liu,Xiaoyan Gu,Xianhui Lu,Hongbo Liu,Bingzheng Wang,Haihui Fan,Ziming Zhao,Rui Zhang,Li Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CrypFormBench基准，用于评估大语言模型在密码方案形式化分析中的五项核心能力（解释、生成、补全、转换、修正），涵盖700个实例、677个方案、7种形式化验证语言和160个安全属性。对9个最新LLM的评估表明，模型在解释和补全任务上表现尚可，但在生成、转换和修正方面存在困难，最高分仅48.7/100（Claude-3.5）。文章还提供了少样本提示、Pass@K采样和轻量微调等实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动形式化分析密码方案劳动密集且需要大量专业知识。尽管模型检测工具和计算安全工具提高了安全证明的自动化程度，但仍依赖专家进行方案抽象和编写工具特定的形式化描述。大语言模型是一个有前景的替代方案，但缺乏标准化评估方法，其在该领域的有效性尚未被探索。

Method: 设计了CrypFormBench基准，联合涵盖符号安全与计算安全，评估LLM的五项核心能力：解释、生成、补全、转换和修正。基准包含700个实例，覆盖677个密码方案、7种主流形式化验证语言和160个安全属性。对9个最先进LLM进行了系统评估。

Result: 大多数LLM在解释和补全任务上表现良好，这得益于其代码感知优势，但在生成、转换和修正任务上表现不佳。整体性能有限，Claude-3.5取得最高分48.7/100。

Conclusion: 本文提供的基准和分析为当前进展提供了具体视角，并通过少样本提示、Pass@K采样和轻量微调等策略，为缓解可执行性瓶颈、提高工具可用输出的可靠性指明了具体方向。

Abstract: Manual formal analysis of cryptographic schemes is labor-intensive and requires substantial expertise. While model-checking tools (e.g., Scyther and Tamarin) and computational-security tools (e.g., CryptoVerif and EasyCrypt) improve the automation of security proofs, they still rely on experts to abstract schemes and write tool-specific formal descriptions. Large language models (LLMs) are a promising alternative, but their effectiveness in this domain remains unexplored due to the absence of standardized evaluation methodologies. To fill this gap, we introduce CrypFormBench (C.F.B for short), a comprehensive benchmark jointly covering symbolic and computational security to evaluate five core LLM capabilities: interpretation, generation, completion, transformation, and correction. It comprises 700 instances spanning 677 schemes, 7 mainstream formal verifier languages, and 160 security properties. The evaluation of 9 state-of-the-art LLMs reveals that most of them perform well on interpretation and completion, given their code-awareness advantages, but struggle with generation, transformation, and correction. Overall, their performance remains limited, with Claude-3.5 achieving the highest score at 48.7 out of 100. We further provide practical guidance, e.g., few-shot prompting, Pass@K sampling, and lightweight fine-tuning, to mitigate the executability bottleneck and improve tool-usable outputs. Taken together, our benchmark and analyses offer a grounded view of current progress and concrete directions toward reliable LLM-assisted formal cryptographic analysis.

</details>


### [138] [An Approach for a Supporting Multi-LLM System for Automated Certification Based on the German IT-Grundschutz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25608)
*Lea Roxanne Muth,Marian Margraf*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多大型语言模型系统（MLS）与混合检索增强生成（HybridRAG）的方法，用于实现半自动化的BSI IT-Grundschutz认证，旨在应对NIS2指令带来的专家短缺和高成本挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NIS2指令带来了认证需求激增，同时面临安全专家短缺和实施成本高昂的问题，需要一种高效、低成本且能保证质量的自动化认证方案。

Method: 采用多大型语言模型系统（MLS）架构，结合大型语言模型（LLMs）与知识图谱（KGs），并引入混合检索增强生成（HybridRAG）技术，覆盖认证全流程。

Result: 系统支持保护需求评估、建模、IT-Grundschutz检查、措施整合及后续实现等认证阶段，提高了效率并降低了成本。

Conclusion: 该架构有效应对了NIS2带来的数字安全挑战，为满足日益增长的安防概念需求提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to perform semi-automated BSI IT-Grundschutz certification using a MultiLarge Language Model system (MLS) with Hybrid RetrievalAugmented Generation (HybridRAG). Facing the challenges of the Network and Information Security Directive 2 (NIS2) directive, a shortage of specialists, and high implementation costs, our MLS architecture aims to increase efficiency, reduce costs, and support certifiers in maintaining the quality of security concepts while meeting the increased demand for certifications of newly affected companies. The system combines Large Language Models (LLMs) and Knowledge Graphs (KGs) to support different phases of the certification process, including protection needs assessment, modeling, IT-Grundschutz check, measure consolidation, and subsequent realization. Our architecture addresses the growing demand for security concepts and offers an approach to handle the digital security challenges introduced by NIS2.

</details>


### [139] [Probabilistic Agents in Deterministic Audits: Evaluating Multi-Agent Systems for Automated Audits Based on the German IT-Grundschutz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25622)
*Lea Roxanne Muth,Marian Margraf*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合多智能体系统（MAS）与混合检索增强生成（HybridRAG）的架构，用于部分自动化IT-Grundschutz（IT-GS）认证流程。研究引入假设验证循环和解耦推理管线两项技术贡献，并在BSI案例上进行评估。实验表明，系统在语义任务（结构分析与建模）上表现优异，但逻辑推理阶段（保护需求评估与IT-GS检查）因LLM概率特性难以达到IT-GS所需的确定性严谨要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NIS-2指令要求大量中小企业实施风险管理，但IT-GS认证资源密集、人工投入高，难以规模化推广。现有方法缺乏针对IT-GS认证流程的自动化解决方案。

Method: 采用多智能体系统（MAS）与混合检索增强生成（HybridRAG）架构。提出两项创新：1）结构分析阶段的假设-验证循环，通过知识图谱交叉验证减少幻觉；2）解耦推理管线，分离智能体驱动的语义提取与确定性保护需求继承。使用BSI官方案例进行端到端评估。

Result: 系统在语义任务（结构分析、建模）上高效，显著减少人工工作量；但在逻辑推理任务（保护需求评估、IT-GS检查）上表现有限，LLM的概率特性难以满足IT-GS的确定性要求。

Conclusion: MAS+HybridRAG架构可部分自动化IT-GS认证，但当前LLM在需要严格确定性推理的环节仍存在根本性局限，系统适用于语义密集型环节而非全流程替代。

Abstract: The NIS-2 Directive mandates robust Risk Management from thousands of small and medium enterprises. To ensure compliance, companies rely on established standards such as the German IT-Grundschutz (IT-GS) of the Federal Office for Information Security. However, IT-GS certification is resource-intensive and requires a high level of manual effort for documentation, validation, and revision, making scalable implementation difficult and expensive.
  Building upon our previous conceptual framework, this paper presents the technical implementation and empirical evaluation of a Multi-Agent System (MAS) architecture combined with Hybrid Retrieval Augmented Generation (HybridRAG) for the partial automation of IT-GS certification. We introduce two novel technical contributions to the MAS architecture to enforce the compliance rigor. The Hypothesis-Verification Loop in the Structural Analysis (SA) phase that cross-references agent-inferred dependencies against the Knowledge Graph to reduce hallucinations, and a Decoupled Reasoning Pipeline that separates agent-driven semantic extraction from the deterministic protection need inheritance. We utilize the BSI's "RecPlast GmbH" case study as a human expert-generated reference data set for end-to-end evaluation of the architecture and to quantify Precision, Recall, and F1-scores. The performance of the system is investigated across the phases of SA, Protection Needs Assessment (PNA), Modeling, and IT-GS Check.
  The empirical results reveal noticeable differences throughout the different steps of IT-GS. While the MAS demonstrates high efficacy in semantic tasks (SA and Modeling), significantly reducing manual effort through automated information extraction, quantitative results reveal limitations in logical reasoning phases (PNA and IT-GS Check) as the probabilistic nature of current LLMs struggles to meet the deterministic rigor required by IT-GS.

</details>


### [140] [Taxonomy of Risks on Automated Fact-Checking Systems Considering its Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25645)
*Jun Yajima,Tatsuya Oka,Takao Okubo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对自动化事实核查系统在SNS上应用时可能产生的风险进行了系统分类，识别出32种具体风险，并提出了一套基于三阶段风险传播模型（风险因素→危险情境→危害）的风险评估指南词，通过在DEFAME系统上的评估验证了该方法优于传统的STRIDE安全评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着SNS上虚假新闻泛滥，自动化事实核查系统虽被广泛采用，但其基于AI和LLM的特性可能产生错误判断并导致虚假信息扩散或诽谤等二次危害，目前缺乏对这些系统特有风险的系统性分类与评估方法。

Method: 采用三阶段风险传播模型（风险因素→危险情境→危害）对自动化事实核查系统进行风险分类，识别出32种具体风险；并将分类结果作为指南词，应用于DEFAME系统的风险评估，与STRIDE方法进行对比。

Result: 识别出自动化事实核查系统中存在32种具体风险；将分类风险作为指南词用于DEFAME系统评估，能够识别出传统STRIDE方法无法推导出的风险。

Conclusion: 所提出的风险分类和指南词方法能有效覆盖自动化事实核查系统的特有风险，弥补传统IT安全风险评估方法的不足，为自动化事实核查系统的安全使用奠定了基础。

Abstract: In recent years, the posting of fake news including disinformation and misinformation on social networking services (SNS) has become a social problem. To combat this fake news, fact-checking that is the process of assessing the veracity of posts on SNS has become increasingly important. While fact-checking is currently performed by fact-checking organizations, it is difficult to fact-check all posts on SNS. Therefore, the use of automated fact-checking systems is effective. Recent automated fact-checking systems utilize artificial intelligence and large language models, so there are risks of incorrect judgments and posting incorrect results on social media which can lead to the spread of misinformation or to engage in defamation. In this paper, as a first step toward enabling the safe use of automated fact-checking systems, we categorize the specific risks on automated fact-checking systems. In this categorizing, we consider a three-stage risk propagation: risk factors, hazardous situations, and harm. Our analysis revealed that 32 specific risks exist in automated fact-checking systems. In this paper, we utilize the categorized risks as analytical cues (guide words) to present the risk assessment of the automated fact-checking system DEFAME. This assessment result indicates that risks that cannot be derived using STRIDE, a conventional IT security risk assessment method can be derived using our guide words.

</details>


### [141] [Tracing Target Answers in Poisoned Retrieval Corpora via Token Influence Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25721)
*Yan-Lun Chen,Pin-Yu Chen,Chia-Mu Yu,Ying-Dar Lin,Yu-Sung Wu,Wei-Bin Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出TRACE，一个轻量级框架，通过追踪token影响力归因来检测RAG系统中的语料投毒攻击，无需额外分类器或LLM验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统易受语料投毒攻击，现有检测方法依赖辅助分类器或LLM验证，计算开销大。

Method: 通过token影响力归因追踪与答案相关的token，首先发现检索文档中重复的高影响力关键词，然后进行二次验证确认其对模型预测的影响。

Result: 在三个QA基准和六个LLM上展示了强大的检测性能，同时能揭示攻击者指定的目标答案。

Conclusion: TRACE是一种轻量级且有效的RAG投毒攻击检测方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems are vulnerable to corpus poisoning attacks that manipulate model outputs through malicious retrieved documents. Existing detection methods typically rely on auxiliary classifiers or additional LLM-based verification, introducing substantial computational overhead. We present TRACE, a lightweight detection framework that identifies poisoning attacks by tracing answer-related tokens through token influence attribution. TRACE first discovers recurrent high-influence keywords across retrieved documents and then performs a secondary verification to confirm their influence on model predictions. Experiments on three QA benchmarks and six LLMs demonstrate strong detection performance while simultaneously uncovering attacker-specified target answers.

</details>


### [142] [Shoot the Honey, Cloak the Player: Towards Zero-Runtime-Overhead Proactive Defense and Detection for Visual Game Cheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25734)
*Jianing Wang,Chuqi Zhang,Yuancheng Jiang,Adil Ahmad,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AimTrap是针对FPS游戏中视觉自瞄作弊器的首个端到端防御系统，通过对抗性伪装纹理（ACT）隐藏真实玩家和对抗性蜜罐纹理（AHT）诱骗作弊器锁定假目标，结合实时保护与赛后检测，在实际测试中对SOTA视觉自瞄器实现85.1%（ACT）和96.9%（AHT）的防御成功率，且误报率极低、运行时开销可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反作弊方案缺乏端到端解决方案：事后行为检测器无法实时保护比赛完整性且对模仿人类行为的自瞄器越来越脆弱，而主动运行时防御往往缺乏可问责性、开销大或需要侵入式系统集成。

Method: 提出两种对抗性纹理机制：ACT（对抗性伪装纹理）隐藏真实玩家，AHT（对抗性蜜罐纹理）诱骗自瞄器锁定假目标；结合可微渲染与渲染期望实现鲁棒的3D纹理合成，安全纹理管理，以及蜜罐交互轨迹分析管道实现准确作弊归因。

Result: 针对SOTA视觉自瞄器的实际游戏测试中，ACT实现85.1%防御成功率，AHT实现96.9%；相比基线方法，误报率极低，运行时开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: AimTrap提供了实用且有效的端到端防御方案，能够同时实现实时保护与赛后检测，且具备低误报率和低开销的优势。

Abstract: Visual aimbots have emerged as a serious cheating threat in first-person shooter (FPS) games, as they evade existing anti-cheat defenses by operating only on rendered frames rather than game memory. However, existing defenses fail to provide an end-to-end solution: post-hoc behavior detectors cannot protect match integrity in real time and are increasingly fragile against human-mimicking aimbots, while proactive runtime defenses often lack accountability, incur substantial overhead, or require intrusive system integration.
  We present AimTrap, the first end-to-end defense against visual aimbots that combines real-time protection with post-game detection using two adversarial texture mechanisms. Adversarial Camouflage Textures (ACT) hide real players from aimbots, while Adversarial Honeypot Textures (AHT) lure aimbots into locking onto fake targets, yielding strong evidence of cheating. To make this practical, AimTrap integrates differentiable rendering with Expectation over Renderings for robust 3D texture synthesis, secure texture management, and a novel honeypot-interaction trajectory analysis pipeline for accurate cheating attribution.
  In real-game evaluation against a state-of-the-art visual aimbot, ACT achieves 85.1% defense success, AHT achieves 96.9%. Compared with prior baselines, AimTrap attains extremely low false-positive rates, while incurring negligible runtime overhead. These results show that AimTrap provides a practical and effective end-to-end defense against visual aimbots.

</details>


### [143] [RAS: Measuring LLM Safety Through Refusal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25750)
*Chang-Chieh Huang,Yan-Lun Chen,Chia-Mu Yu,Wei-Bin Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeVec是一种基于内部表示的白盒LLM安全评估方法，通过提取参考模型的拒绝对齐方向并测量目标模型的隐藏状态对齐程度，生成RAS评分（0-100），在多个模型族上有效区分对齐与未对齐模型，且比传统输出级评估更快更便宜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统输出级安全评估存在成本高、对评判模型敏感、依赖固定题库等局限性，需要一种更高效、更鲁棒的安全评估方法。

Method: 从安全对齐的参考模型中逐层提取拒绝方向向量（refusal directions），选择安全与不安全行为可分离的稳定层窗口，然后测量目标模型在越狱提示下隐藏状态与这些拒绝方向的对齐程度，得到RAS分数。

Result: 在Llama、Gemma、Qwen模型家族上，RAS能有效区分对齐模型与未审查/ablated变体，与输出级攻击成功率相关，且速度显著快于基于评判模型的评估方法。

Conclusion: 拒绝对齐提供了紧凑且高效的白盒安全评估信号，SafeVec可作为传统输出级评估的替代方案。

Abstract: Safety evaluation of large language models (LLMs) is commonly performed by querying models with unsafe or jailbreak prompts and judging whether their outputs violate a safety policy. Although useful, output-level evaluation is expensive, sensitive to judge choice, and easily tied to fixed question banks. We propose **SafeVec**, a white-box evaluation procedure that measures safety from internal representations rather than generated answers. **SafeVec** first extracts layer-wise refusal directions from a safety-aligned reference model, then selects stable layer windows where safe and unsafe behaviors are separable, and finally scores a target model by measuring whether its hidden states align with these refusal directions under unsafe and jailbreak prompts. The resulting metric, **RAS** (**R**efusal **A**lignment **S**core), maps representation-level refusal alignment to a calibrated 0-100 safety score. Across `Llama`, `Gemma`, and `Qwen` model families, RAS separates aligned models from uncensored and abliterated variants, tracks output-level attack success rate, and is substantially faster than judge-based evaluation. These results suggest that refusal alignment provides a compact and efficient signal for white-box LLM safety evaluation.

</details>


### [144] [Color Matters: Trigger Color Affects Success in Federated Backdoor Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25858)
*Kavindu Herath,Joshua C. Zhao,Saurabh Bagchi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习中的语义后门攻击，发现即使触发器语义、位置和投毒预算不变，仅改变触发器颜色（黑白）就能显著影响攻击成功率，且该趋势在鲁棒聚合下依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索联邦学习中基于语义（而非简单像素模式）的后门攻击机制，特别是触发器颜色这一先前被忽视的因素对攻击效果的影响。

Method: 使用口罩、墨镜等语义触发器，构造黑白两种颜色变体，在四类CelebA发色分类任务上，分别采用标准投毒目标和更强的SABLE目标（含干净分类损失、触发目标损失、特征分离损失和正则化）进行攻击实验。

Result: 白色触发器对攻击金发类更有效，黑色触发器对攻击黑发类更有效；颜色变化显著改变攻击成功率，且该趋势在鲁棒聚合下依然稳健。

Conclusion: 触发器颜色是语义后门机制中一个有意义且不可忽视的因素，影响攻击的操作、持久性和评估。

Abstract: Federated learning is vulnerable to backdoor attacks in which malicious clients inject poisoned updates while preserving benign-task performance. In this paper, we study a semantics-driven backdoor mechanism in which attackers use natural visual accessories as triggers and manipulate only the trigger color while keeping the attack pipeline fixed. Our framework considers semantic trigger objects such as masks and sunglasses, instantiated in black and white variants, and evaluates their effect in a controlled federated learning setting. Malicious clients construct poisoned samples by applying a trigger to source-class images and relabeling them to an attacker-chosen target class, while benign clients train only on clean data. We analyze this mechanism under both a standard poisoning objective and a stronger SABLE-based objective that combines clean classification loss, triggered target loss, feature-separation loss in the penultimate representation space, and regularization to keep malicious updates close to the global model. This design enables the attack to remain effective while reducing excessive update drift. Experiments on a four-class CelebA hair-color task show that trigger color significantly changes attack success rate even when trigger semantics, placement, and poisoning budget are unchanged. White triggers are more effective for attacks targeting the blond class, whereas black triggers perform better for attacks targeting the black class. The same trend persists under robust aggregation, showing that trigger color is a meaningful factor in the operation, persistence, and evaluation of semantic backdoor mechanisms in federated learning.

</details>


### [145] [A Tattered Cloak of Invisibility: Measuring Anonymity Loss in Railgun on Ethereum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25926)
*Kanan Huseynov,Ali Shahzaib,István András Seres,János Tapolcai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究分析了加密货币混币器Railgun中的行为泄漏，提出了五种启发式方法（时间模式、地址重用、交易图邻近性、金额指纹、背包匹配），发现即使密码学上安全的隐私系统也会因用户行为和交易模式遭受显著匿名性损失。五种启发式方法能唯一关联17.65%的取款交易与存款交易，背包求解器算法导致取款交易中位数匿名损失3.42比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统银行服务与基于区块链的金融系统最显著的区别在于后者交易公开可见，大量密码学方案被提出以增加隐私层，但用户实际获得的隐私不仅取决于底层密码协议的安全性，用户行为、交易金额模式和时机决策会大幅降低匿名性。本文旨在研究加密货币混币器中的行为泄漏。

Method: 以以太坊上的Railgun为研究对象，提出五种启发式方法估计存款与取款交易属于同一用户的概率：1）特征时间模式；2）地址重用；3）先前公开交易诱导的交易图邻近性；4）保留数字模式特征的金额指纹；5）背包类型匹配（交易金额分组呈现出揭示性加和模式）。

Result: 五种启发式方法能唯一关联17.65%的Railgun取款交易与存款交易；背包求解器算法导致取款交易中位数匿名损失3.42比特。

Conclusion: 即使密码学上强大的隐私系统也可能因用户行为和交易模式遭受显著匿名性损失。本文有助于更好地理解混币器和匿名池的实际隐私限制，并为更安全的使用实践和设计原则提供指导。

Abstract: From a user's perspective, perhaps the most significant difference between traditional banking services and widely used blockchain-based financial systems is that, in the latter, transactions and, either directly or indirectly, account balances and transaction histories are publicly observable. Therefore, a growing number of cryptographic solutions have been proposed to add a privacy layer to such systems. However, the privacy that users actually obtain does not depend solely on the security of the underlying cryptographic protocol: user behavior, transaction amount patterns, and timing decisions can substantially reduce anonymity.
  In this work, we study behavioral leakage in cryptocurrency mixers, focusing on Railgun on Ethereum. We aim to heuristically estimate the probability that a given deposit and withdrawal transaction belong to the same user. We consider five sources of leakage: characteristic timing patterns, address reuse, proximity in the transaction graph induced by prior public transactions, amount fingerprints that preserve distinctive digit patterns across transaction values, and knapsack type matches in which groups of transaction amounts add up in revealing ways. Our results show that even cryptographically strong privacy systems may suffer substantial anonymity loss due to user behavior and transaction patterns. Our five heuristics are able to uniquely link 17.65% of Railgun withdraw transactions to deposit transactions. We also applied a knapsack solver algorithm that was able to produce a 3.42 bit median anonymity loss for withdraw transactions. This work contributes to a better understanding of the practical privacy limits of mixers and anonymity pools, and points toward safer usage practices and design principles.

</details>


### [146] [Do (Not) Tell Me About My Insecurities: Assessing the Status Quo of Coordinated Vulnerability Disclosure in Germany Amid New EU Cybersecurity Regulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25950)
*Sebastian Neef,Cenk Schlunke,Anne Hennig*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对德国DAX 40家公司的纵向研究表明，采用协调漏洞披露（CVD）程序的公司比例从2023年的50%显著增长到2025年的90%以上，法律义务（如NIS2和CRA）是主要驱动因素，但缺乏人力和报告质量参差不齐仍是挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在日益互联的世界中，良好的IT安全实践对于预防漏洞和数据泄露至关重要。提供安全联系信息（如通过CVD程序或security.txt文件）是企业促进外部漏洞报告的重要实践，但缺乏对大型企业实际采用情况和经验的研究。

Method: 纵向研究方法，对德国DAX指数40家公司进行分析。一方面监控其CVD程序的公开信息，另一方面在2023年和2025年通过电子邮件和邮寄方式发送问卷，回收率为20%。

Result: 采用率从2023年的50%显著增长到2025年的90%以上；新增10个CVD程序和25个新security.txt文件；法律义务（如NIS2和CRA）驱动采纳，但缺乏人力资源和报告质量参差不齐是主要障碍。

Conclusion: 这是首次对德国DAX 40家上市公司CVD实践进行的大规模调查，结果有助于促进中小企业和其它公司采纳和理解安全程序，并为政策制定者提供实践挑战和行业经验的洞察。

Abstract: In our increasingly interconnected world, good IT security practices are necessary to prevent vulnerabilities and data breaches. Providing security contacts, e.g., via Coordinated Vulnerability Disclosure (CVD) programs or security.txt files, is an important practice for businesses to facilitate vulnerability reporting by external parties. As part of a longitudinal study, we analyzed the adoption of, as well as the challenges and experiences with, CVD programs among the 40 companies listed on Germany's DAX (the country's primary stock market index). In addition to monitoring publicly available information about their CVD programs, we sent out questionnaires via email and postal mail in 2023 and 2025, and received answers from 20\% of the companies. The adoption rates show a significant increase from 50\% (2023) to over 90\% (2025), with ten new CVD programs and 25 new security.txt files now available. The survey answers reveal that, for example, legal obligations (e.g., NIS2 and CRA) drive the adoption of CVD practices, but a lack of (human) resources and varying report quality are considered drawbacks. As the first study to survey 40 German stock market index (DAX) companies on their CVD practices, our results can help foster the adoption and understanding of security programs among SMEs and other companies, and provide policymakers with insights into practical challenges and industry experiences.

</details>


### [147] [BlowLive: Blow-Based Multi-Factor Biometrics with Liveness Detection and Revocability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25998)
*Eyasu Getahun Chekole,Howard Halim,Daniël Reijsbergen,Jianying Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出BlowLive多因素生物认证框架，融合吹气声学信号与面部生物特征，采用模糊提取器派生密钥、多普勒频移活体检测，实现高精度（融合模态100%）、强模板保护与可撤销性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理和行为生物特征存在活体检测不可靠、模板泄露隐私、类内变异导致精度下降、生理特征难以撤销等根本性局限。

Method: 1) 融合吹气声学信号（行为模态）与面部生物特征（生理模态）的多因素框架；2) 基于模糊提取器的认证方案，从含噪生物输入中派生稳定密钥；3) 多普勒频移活体检测机制防御重放、合成与深度伪造攻击。

Result: 50名参与者实验中：吹气声学99.56%、面部及融合模态100%认证精度；活体检测99.42%准确率；模板保护与可撤销性优异。

Conclusion: BlowLive有效解决了传统生物认证的多个核心挑战，兼具高精度、强安全性与良好可用性。

Abstract: Biometric authentication systems are increasingly deployed in security-critical applications, yet existing physiological and behavioral biometrics suffer from fundamental limitations: 1) they are vulnerable to spoofing attacks due to unreliable liveness detection, 2) biometric templates may leak privacy-sensitive information 3) intra-user variability results in accuracy degradation, and 4) it is difficult to revoke physiological biometrics and safeguard them over long-term use. To address these challenges, we propose BlowLive, a robust multi-factor biometric (MFB) framework that integrates blow-acoustic signals and facial biometrics as complementary behavioral and physiological modalities. BlowLive incorporates advanced spectral feature extraction and multimodal fusion techniques, achieving high authentication accuracy even for behavioral modalities. Instead of relying on conventional biometric approaches that utilize raw biometric templates for authentication, the proposed framework adopts a fuzzy-extractor-based biometric authentication scheme, wherein stable cryptographic keys are derived from inherently noisy biometric inputs and subsequently used for authentication. To defend against playback, synthetic, and deepfake attacks, BlowLive further integrates a novel Doppler shift-based liveness detection mechanism. We implement the complete BlowLive framework and experimentally evaluate its effectiveness using biometric data collected from 50 participants. The experimental results demonstrate high authentication accuracy (99.56% for blow-acoustics and 100% for facial and fusion modalities), robust liveness detection (99.42% accuracy), strong template protection and revocability, non-invasiveness, and high usability.

</details>


### [148] [Privacy Vulnerabilities of Attention Layers in Tabular Foundation Models and Protection of High-Risk Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26021)
*Tânia Carvalho,Maxime Cordy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了表格基础模型在上下文学习中的隐私漏洞，提出了一种基于注意力机制的无影子模型成员推理攻击（AMIA），并设计了基于k-匿名原则的推理时防御方法，同时指出微调会进一步放大成员推断风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点认为表格基础模型因使用合成数据预训练而隐私风险有限，但忽略了推理时上下文学习可能包含敏感记录的情况。本文旨在揭示这一被忽视的隐私漏洞。

Method: 提出AMIA攻击，利用Transformer注意力模式的集中性进行成员推理，无需影子模型；防御方面，基于k-匿名原则在推理时降低上下文键表示的独特性，且仅针对AMIA评分的高风险查询。

Result: AMIA攻击平均提升7.7%的攻击成功率，尤其在低假阳性率场景下优势明显；防御使AMIA成员泄漏降低50%，对置信度攻击降低25%，仅造成3.9%的性能损失；微调后置信度上升的样本更易受攻击。

Conclusion: 上下文示例存在隐私泄露风险，注意力机制会暴露成员信号；微调会放大记忆效应，增加隐私风险；所提防御可在不引入随机噪声或重训练的条件下有效缓解泄漏。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models are commonly assumed to present limited privacy concerns as they are often pre-trained on large collections of synthetic data. However, these models leverage in-context learning, where sensitive records may be provided directly at inference time as labelled context examples. In this paper, we demonstrate that predictions generated via the attention mechanism leak sufficient information to enable effective Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs). To highlight this vulnerability, we propose AMIA (Attention-based Membership Inference Attack), a shadow-model-free attack that exploits the concentration of transformer attention patterns. Our results show that attention mechanisms reveal strong membership signals, which exceed classical confidence-based attacks, achieving an average gain of 7.7\%, specially in low false-positive regimes. To mitigate this risk, we introduce an inference-time defence inspired by $k$-anonymity principles. This approach reduces the uniqueness of context-key representations without introducing random noise or retraining the model. By targeting only high-risk queries identified through AMIA scores, the defence substantially reduces membership leakage of this attack by an average of 50\% and 25\% against confidence-based attacks, while preserving predictive utility with only 3.9\% performance degradation. Beyond showing that context examples are vulnerable, we further demonstrate that fine-tuning introduces an additional source of privacy risk. In particular, samples whose prediction confidence increases after fine-tuning become more susceptible to MIAs, indicating that fine-tuning can amplify memorisation and expose sensitive training information through confidence shifts.

</details>


### [149] [Can Trustless Agents Be Trusted? An Empirical Study of the ERC-8004 Decentralized AI Agent Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26028)
*Xihan Xiong,Zelin Li,Wei Wei,Qin Wang,William Knottenbelt,Zhipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对ERC-8004协议进行了首次实证研究，发现其身份注册多数为占位符，声誉系统存在不可比较、缺乏可验证交互基础以及易被低成本操纵等问题，大量评论者表现出协同女巫行为，导致去除女巫标记后大部分被评代理失去有效反馈。研究提出了具体的协议修订建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI代理跨组织交易时面临信任挑战，ERC-8004作为首个无需许可的信任层协议已被广泛采用但缺乏实证研究，不清楚其记录的信息能否为决策提供可靠信任基础。

Method: 对以太坊、BNB Smart Chain和Base三条链上的ERC-8004协议进行实证研究，爬取链上身份和声誉事件、链下文件以及x402支付交易数据，覆盖从协议部署至2026年5月13日。

Result: 身份侧：多数注册为占位符，仅3%、4%、15%（分别对应以太坊、BSC、Base）暴露有效注册文件且至少有一个实时服务端点。声誉侧：注册表无法作为信任信号——值不可通约、反馈记录缺乏可验证交互基础、声誉可低成本操纵；73.6%、59.2%、90.6%的评论者表现出协同女巫行为；去除女巫标记后，15.5%、72.3%、89.4%的被评代理无有效反馈。

Conclusion: 研究结果为ERC-8004未来修订提供了具体建议，得出了可操作的协议设计启示，并为AI代理市场研究建立了实证基线。

Abstract: As autonomous AI agents increasingly transact across organizational boundaries, a fundamental trust challenge emerges: how can an agent assess whether an unknown counterpart is trustworthy? The ERC-8004 protocol addresses this challenge with the first permissionless trust layer for AI agent economies, built around three on-chain registries for Identity, Reputation, and Validation. Despite its rapid adoption, the protocol has not been studied empirically, leaving it unclear whether the information it records provides a trustworthy basis for decision-making. To address this gap, we present the first empirical study of ERC-8004 across three chains: Ethereum, BNB Smart Chain (BSC), and Base, covering the period from protocol deployment through May 13, 2026. We crawl on-chain Identity and Reputation events, off-chain files, and x402 payment transactions.
  On the identity side, we find that most registrations are placeholders rather than active agents, with only a small fraction (3%, 4%, and 15% across Ethereum, BSC, and Base) exposing a valid ERC-8004 registration file with at least one live service endpoint. On the reputation side, we show that the Registry, as currently deployed, cannot function as a trust signal: values are not commensurable, feedback records are rarely grounded in verifiable interactions, and reputation can be manipulated at minimal cost. Consistent with these design weaknesses, we find that a substantial fraction of reviewers (73.6%, 59.2%, and 90.6% across Ethereum, BSC, and Base) exhibit coordinated Sybil behavior. After removing Sybil-flagged feedback, 15.5%, 72.3%, and 89.4% of rated agents, respectively, are left with no valid feedback. We then turn these findings into concrete recommendations for future revisions of ERC-8004. Our study yields actionable protocol-design implications and establishes an empirical baseline for research on AI agent markets.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Minimax PAC Bounds for Learning in Exogenous Contextual MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25170)
*Corentin Pla,Hugo Richard,Marc Abeille,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了带外生i.i.d.上下文的表格型折扣马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）的PAC学习问题，提出了方差缩减算法，在已知奖励/转移且仅需采样上下文分布时，实现了与上下文空间大小|Z|无关的样本复杂度；在完全未知设定下给出了匹配的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MDP学习理论多假设环境固定，但实际应用中常存在不受控但可观测的上下文变量（如用户特征），这些上下文影响奖励和转移但不受策略控制。本文旨在刻画此类外生上下文MDP中策略评估、最优价值估计和最优策略提取的样本复杂度下限，并设计最优算法。

Method: 1) 提出方差缩减算法，利用上下文分布的采样或acles（执行前采样n次，执行中采样m次）；2) 在已知奖励/转移条件下，设计算法分别解决PE、BVE和BPE问题，样本复杂度为(Õ(1/((1-γ)^3ε^2)), 0)，即无需执行中采样；3) 在完全未知设定下，给出PE问题的匹配上下界：(Õ(|X|/((1-γ)^3ε^2)), Õ(1/((1-γ)^2ε^2)))。

Result: 已知设定下：样本复杂度为(Õ(1/((1-γ)^3ε^2)), 0)，与|Z|无关且达到极小极大最优（对数因子内）；完全未知设定下：PE的样本复杂度为(Õ(|X|/((1-γ)^3ε^2)), Õ(1/((1-γ)^2ε^2)))，上下界匹配。作为推论，还改进了一步完美前视（one-step perfect look-ahead）情景的紧致界。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了外生上下文MDP的PAC学习样本复杂度，揭示了上下文分布采样与转移核采样的分离性质，提供了最优算法与匹配下界。

Abstract: We study PAC learning in tabular discounted Markov decision processes with exogenous i.i.d. contexts, with discount factor $γ$, finite state space $\mathcal X$, action space $\mathcal A$, and context space $\mathcal Z$. At each time step, a context is drawn independently from an unknown distribution $μ$ and revealed before the agent acts. This context may affect both rewards and transitions, while remaining uncontrolled by the agent. Depending on the regime, the learner has access either to a sampling oracle for $μ$, to a sampling oracle for the transition kernel conditioned on state-context-action tuples, or to both. Oracles can be accessed before and during policy execution. The sample complexity is measured by a couple $(n,m)$, where $n$ is the number of calls to the sampling oracles before execution and $m$ is the number of calls to the sampling oracles during execution. When rewards and transitions are known and only the context distribution $μ$ is sampled, we give a variance-reduced algorithm that solves policy evaluation (PE), best-value estimation (BVE), and best-policy extraction (BPE) with $\left(\widetilde O\left(1/((1-γ)^3\varepsilon^2)\right), 0 \right) $ sample complexity. The rate is independent of $|\mathcal Z|$ and minimax optimal up to logarithmic factors. As a corollary, we also obtain tight rates in the case of one-step perfect look-ahead, improving upon the existing guarantees. In the fully unknown regime, where both $μ$ and P must be learned, we show that PE remains $|\mathcal Z|$-free, with matching upper and lower bounds $\bigl(\widetilde O(|\mathcal X|/((1-γ)^3\varepsilon^2)),\, \widetilde O(1/((1-γ)^2\varepsilon^2))\bigr)$.

</details>


### [151] [Stabilizing black-box algorithms through task-oriented randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25269)
*Yali Wang,Zhaojun Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向任务的随机化方法，自适应地根据输入数据的生成机制来稳定黑箱模型输出，并建立了稳定性与探索之间权衡的理论分析，扩展至大语言模型中的top-k排序问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱模型在现代研究中广泛应用，但其输出稳定性对于实现可信人工智能至关重要。输入数据的多样性（从结构化高斯分布到未知结构的复杂数据）使得稳定黑箱输出成为挑战，亟需一种能有效利用先验信息并适应数据生成机制的方法。

Method: 提出一种任务导向的随机化方法，该方法能自适应地根据输入数据的生成机制调整策略，尤其针对非结构化复杂性。建立了全面的稳定性保证理论框架，并分析了稳定性与探索之间的内在权衡。受大语言模型架构启发，将框架扩展到top-k排序问题。

Result: 通过广泛的数值模拟和真实数据集应用，验证了所提方法的有效性和正确性。

Conclusion: 本文为黑箱模型的稳定性提供了严格的理论基础，并揭示了稳定性与探索之间的权衡关系，同时成功将方法扩展至大语言模型的top-k排序场景。

Abstract: As black-box models become foundational to modern research, ensuring their stability is paramount for the realization of trustworthy artificial intelligence. The inherent diversity of inputs - ranging from structured Gaussian distributions to complex data with unknown structures - poses a significant challenge: how to stabilize black-box outputs while effectively leveraging available prior information. This paper introduces a task-oriented randomization methodology that adaptively tailors its strategy to the underlying generative mechanisms of the input data, specifically addressing unstructured complexities. A comprehensive suite of stability guarantees is proposed. Beyond establishing rigorous theoretical foundations for stability, the research provides a detailed analysis of the intrinsic trade-off between stability and exploration. Motivated by the architecture of Large Language Models, the framework is further extended to top-k ranking problems. The validity and effectiveness of the proposal are demonstrated through extensive numerical simulations and applications to the real-world dataset.

</details>


### [152] [Statistically Valid Hyperparameter Selection: From Tuning to Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25601)
*Amirmohammad Farzaneh,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"先学习后测试"(LTT)范式的统一统计框架，将超参数选择形式化为多重假设检验问题，能够在有限样本下以显式的误差概率控制，保证所选超参数满足特定的可靠性要求（如平均风险、分位数风险或信息论约束）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数选择在现代AI系统部署中至关重要，但现有方法（如网格搜索、贝叶斯优化）仅凭经验操作，缺乏对可靠性或安全性的正式统计保证。

Method: 采用"先学习后测试"(LTT)范式，将超参数选择形式化为对候选超参数集合的多重假设检验问题，并利用p值、e值及集中不等式等统计工具，实现有限样本下误差概率的显式控制。

Result: 该方法能够选择出在统计上可证明满足应用特定可靠性要求的超参数，包括对平均风险、分位数风险或信息论约束的边界控制，并提供显式的有限样本误差概率保证。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一的统计框架，弥补了超参数选择缺乏正式统计保证的空白，使超参数选择在可靠性关键场景中具备可验证的安全保障。

Abstract: Hyperparameter selection is a critical step in the deployment of modern artificial intelligence systems, given the need to tune degrees of freedom such as inference-time parameters, implementation-level settings, and thresholds driving decision rules. Despite its practical importance, hyperparameter selection is typically performed using best-effort empirical methods such as grid search or Bayesian optimization, which provide no formal statistical guarantees on reliability or safety.
  This monograph presents a unified statistical framework for reliable hyperparameter selection, centered on the learn-then-test (LTT) paradigm, which formulates the problem as multiple hypothesis testing over a candidate set of hyperparameters. The framework enables the selection of hyperparameters that provably satisfy application-specific reliability requirements -- such as bounds on average risk, quantile risk, or information-theoretic constraints -- with explicit, finite-sample control of error probabilities. The supporting statistical machinery, namely p-values, e-values, and concentration inequalities, is developed from first principles in a dedicated appendix.

</details>


### [153] [Gaussian Mean Field Variational Inference can Overestimate Predictive Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25745)
*James Odgers,Ben Riegler,Siddharth Swaroop,Vincent Fortuin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过分析共轭贝叶斯线性回归模型，证明均值场变分推断（MFVI）不仅会低估参数空间的后验方差，还会在某些方向上高估预测方差——尤其是在训练数据集中的方向上。这导致在分布内测试点上，MFVI的预期预测方差甚至超过精确后验，甚至出现MFVI后验无法降低预测方差的病态情况。文章将此与冷后验效应联系起来，认为调整温度可以纠正这种过度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为MFVI总是低估后验方差，本文试图揭示这一理解是不完整的，通过理论分析和实验展示MFVI在预测方差上的过度估计现象，并解释其与冷后验效应的联系。

Method: 以共轭贝叶斯线性回归（BLR）为分析对象，从理论上推导MFVI在参数空间和预测空间中方差的估计偏差，并通过合成数据和真实回归任务进行实验验证。

Result: MFVI在参数空间低估方差，但在预测空间可能高估方差；过度估计发生在训练数据集中方向；分布内测试点上MFVI的预期预测方差超过精确后验；存在MFVI后验无法降低预测方差的病态案例；温度调整可纠正过度估计。

Conclusion: MFVI的方差估计偏差是双面的：既有低估也有高估，且高估方向与数据集中方向一致。冷后验效应可以通过调整温度来修正这种预测方差的高估，使MFVI的预测更接近精确后验。

Abstract: Mean Field Variational Inference (MFVI) is widely understood to underestimate posterior variance. By analysing conjugate Bayesian Linear Regression (BLR), we show that this characterization is incomplete: while MFVI underestimates the variance in parameter space, it can overestimate the predictive variance compared to the exact posterior. We show that if the MFVI posterior underestimates predictive variances in some directions, it necessarily overestimates them in others. Crucially, this overestimation occurs in directions where the training data concentrates. This leads to the surprising result that, for a test point drawn from the training distribution, MFVI's expected predictive variance exceeds that of the exact posterior. We demonstrate a pathological case of this effect, where the MFVI posterior fails to reduce predictive variance compared to the prior on in distribution data. We connect these results to the Cold Posterior Effect, arguing that varying the temperature can correct this overestimation, yielding predictions closer to those of the exact posterior. We validate our theory on synthetic and real-world regression tasks.

</details>


### [154] [FedReLa: Imbalanced Federated Learning via Re-Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26037)
*Guangzheng Hu,Patricia Menéndez,Feng Liu,Mingming Gong,Guanghui Wang,Liuhua Peng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: FedReLa 是一种无需全局类别分布知识的数据级联邦学习方法，通过特征依赖的标签重分配器纠正全局决策边界，有效解决了数据异构性和类别不平衡的共存问题，显著提升了少数类别的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中全局类别不平衡与跨客户端数据异构性自然共存，局部与全局不平衡的失配加剧了聚合模型性能退化，而全局类别分布不可知给数据级方法带来挑战。

Method: 提出 FedReLa 方法，通过特征依赖的标签重分配器（feature-dependent label re-allocator）对样本进行重标定，纠正有偏的全局决策边界，无需全局类别分布知识；该方法模块化、模型无关，可与算法方法集成且不增加额外通信开销。

Result: 在逐步不平衡和长尾数据集上，显著提高了少数类别的准确率和整体准确率，超越了先前的最优方法。

Conclusion: FedReLa 有效应对了联邦学习中数据异构性与类别不平衡共存的核心挑战，为数据级联邦学习提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Federated learning has emerged as the foremost approach for decentralized model training with privacy preservation. The global class imbalance and cross-client data heterogeneity naturally coexist, and the mismatch between local and global imbalances exacerbates the performance degradation of the aggregated model. The agnosticism of global class distribution poses significant challenges for data-level methods, especially under extreme conditions with severe class absence across clients. In this paper, we propose FedReLa, a novel data-level approach that tackles the coexistence of data heterogeneity and class imbalance in federated learning. By re-labeling samples with a feature-dependent label re-allocator, FedReLa corrects biased global decision boundaries without requiring knowledge of the global class distribution. This modular, model-agnostic approach can be integrated with algorithmic methods to deliver consistent improvements without additional communication overhead. Through extensive experiments, our method significantly improves the accuracy of minority classes and the overall accuracy on stepwise-imbalanced and long-tailed datasets, outperforming the previous state of the art.

</details>


### [155] [When Does Synthetic Data Augmentation Improve Score-Based Imbalanced Classification?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26053)
*Zhengchi Ma,Pengfei Lyu,Anru R. Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个理论框架来刻画合成数据增强在基于评分的分类任务中的效果，将其分解为有效类别权重变化和合成分布误差。在模型正确设定下，增强无法带来根本性改进，甚至可能引入偏差；而在模型错误设定下，增强可通过改变类别平衡来修正排序错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据增强被广泛用于缓解类别不平衡，但其在基于评分的分类中的理论效果尚不明确，缺乏对何时有效、何时无效的系统性理论理解。

Method: 将增强效果分解为有效类别权重变化和合成/真实分布差异两部分；分别在模型正确设定和错误设定下进行理论分析；建立极小化最优下界；推导显式改进界。

Result: 正确设定下，原始估计器已实现总体最优，增强仅可能带来有限样本方差缩减，且会引入合成分布偏差；错误设定下，增强可改变有效类别平衡以修正排序错误，但改进非单调。

Conclusion: 合成数据增强在模型正确设定时收益有限且可能有害，在模型错误设定时可带来实质性改进，但效果非单调，需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Synthetic data augmentation is widely used to mitigate class imbalance, but its theoretical effects on score-based classification remain poorly understood. This paper develops a framework for characterizing when synthetic minority augmentation can improve threshold-integrated and threshold-optimized metrics, including AUROC, AUPRC, best-threshold balanced accuracy, and best-threshold \(\F_1\) score. We separate the effect of augmentation into two components: a change in effective class weighting and a discrepancy between the synthetic and true minority distributions. Under well-specified score models, the raw estimator already targets the likelihood-ratio ordering, which is population-optimal for the metrics considered. Consequently, augmentation cannot provide a fundamental population-level improvement beyond possible finite-sample variance reduction, and may introduce additional bias through synthetic distributional error. We further establish minimax lower bounds showing that the raw estimator already achieves the optimal metric-regret rate in the well-specified regime. Under misspecification, however, augmentation can play a qualitatively different role: by changing the effective class balance, it can alter the restricted-class projection and correct ranking errors induced by the raw imbalanced objective. We provide explicit improvement bounds quantifying the roles of approximation error, finite-sample estimation error, and synthetic distributional error. Simulation studies corroborate the theory, demonstrating limited gains under well-specification and nontrivial but nonmonotone improvements under misspecification.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Sample complexity of unbalanced entropic OT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24987)
*Francisco Andrade,Gabriel Peyré,Clarice Poon*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了熵正则化非平衡最优传输（Entropic Unbalanced OT）在最优耦合层面的样本复杂度，通过平移不变对偶形式、紧性与强凸性分析，推导出经验耦合的高概率有限样本界，揭示了正则化在缓解维数灾难和降低样本需求方面的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确平衡OT对数据中质量增减过于刚性，且高维样本复杂度不理想。熵正则化改善统计行为但尚未在耦合层面得到充分分析，非平衡松弛则通过软约束适应含噪声的经验数据。本文旨在从耦合估计的角度理解这两种互补技术为何在机器学习中不可或缺。

Method: 1) 建立平移不变对偶形式；2) 证明内在对偶变量的紧性与强凸性；3) 将几何估计转化为高概率有限样本界，分析经验耦合的收敛行为。

Result: 给出了熵正则化非平衡OT在最优耦合层面的高概率有限样本误差界，定量说明了正则化如何缓解维数灾难、降低对样本数量的需求，并保证估计器与Sinkhorn类可扩展求解器兼容。

Conclusion: 正则化是机器学习实践中处理高维概率测度比较的必要手段：它软化了维数诅咒，减少了稳定传输估计所需的样本量，同时保持了与可扩展Sinkhorn算法的兼容性。

Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) has become a central language for comparing probability measures, but exact balanced OT is often both too rigid for data with missing, created, or destroyed mass and subject to unfavorable high-dimensional sample complexity. Entropic regularization and unbalanced relaxations address these limitations in complementary ways. Entropy smooths the geometry, improves statistical behavior, and enables fast Sinkhorn-type algorithms, while unbalanced marginal penalties replace hard conservation constraints by divergence terms adapted to noisy empirical data. This paper studies the sample complexity of entropic unbalanced OT at the level of the optimal coupling, rather than only the scalar transport value. We develop a translation-invariant dual formulation, prove compactness and strong convexity properties for the intrinsic dual variables, and convert these geometric estimates into high-probability finite-sample bounds for empirical couplings. The results clarify why regularization is a practical necessity in machine learning applications: it softens the curse of dimensionality, reduces the number of samples needed for stable transport estimation, and keeps the resulting estimators compatible with scalable Sinkhorn-type solvers.

</details>


### [157] [Laplace--Fisher Gate Identities for Optimal Matrix-Gated Blended Score Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25169)
*Alois Duston,Tan Bui Tanh*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出Laplace-Fisher Gate Identity (LFGI)，通过矩阵值混合系数实现最优方差分数估计，用于反向Ornstein-Uhlenbeck扩散中的未归一化目标采样，并在贝叶斯逆问题中构建归一化后验密度代理，提升密度校准与采样诊断效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从未归一化目标分布中采样需要估计噪声扰动边缘分布的分数（score），现有标量混合估计方法在奇异或强各向异性目标下过于僵化，方差控制能力不足。

Method: 将混合分数估计建模为关于矩阵值混合系数（门控）的条件风险最小化问题，推导出方差最优门控的解析表达式——Laplace-Fisher Gate Identity (LFGI)，并给出高斯特例、有限参考一致性及稳定性界。

Result: 在PDE约束的贝叶斯逆问题基准上，LFGI相比其他分数估计器类别提升了后验密度校准和采样诊断效果；在已知证据的高斯和非高斯设置中验证了绝对校准性能。

Conclusion: LFGI提供了灵活且方差最优的分数估计框架，有效利用MCMC试点样本和导数信息构建归一化后验密度代理，支持后验能量评估、模型证据估计及基于密度的诊断。

Abstract: Sampling from an unnormalized target by reversing an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck diffusion requires the score of each noise-perturbed marginal. Tweedie's identity and a target-score identity give unbiased finite-reference estimators for this score. Scalar blends can reduce variance, but are too rigid for singular or strongly anisotropic targets. We cast blended score estimation as conditional risk minimization over matrix-valued blending coefficients, or gates, and derive the variance-optimal gate [
\Gstar(y,t)=\alphat^2\bigl(\alphat^2 I_d+\gammat,\E[H_0(X_0)\mid Y_t=y]\bigr)^{-1},\qquad H_0=-\nabla^2\log p_0 . ]
Here (\alphat=e^{-t}) and (\gammat=1-e^{-2t}). We call this formula the \emph{Laplace--Fisher Gate Identity} (\LFGI{}). Since the Tweedie--TSI disagreement has conditional mean zero, the gate changes estimator variance without changing its expected value. We give the Gaussian special case and prove finite-reference consistency and stability bounds for estimating the gate from weighted reference samples.
  We apply the finite-reference LFGI estimator to normalized density evaluation for Bayesian inverse problems. When MCMC pilot samples and derivative information are available, LFGI uses these byproducts to construct a normalized posterior-density surrogate. The surrogate enables posterior-energy evaluation, model-evidence estimation, and density-based diagnostics beyond those available from samples alone. On a PDE-constrained inverse-problem benchmark, LFGI improves posterior-density calibration and sampling diagnostics relative to the other tested score-estimator classes, and known-evidence experiments check absolute calibration in Gaussian and non-Gaussian settings.

</details>


### [158] [Quantitative Wasserstein Propagation of Chaos for Transport Ensemble Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25346)
*Frederic J. N. Jorgensen,Ricardo Baptista,Franca Hoffmann,Youssef Marzouk*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文为传输集合滤波器（TEFs）建立了一个通用概率框架，证明了其交互粒子系统以蒙特卡洛速率在Wasserstein度量下收敛到平均场极限，并首次为EnKF和EnSMF提供了非渐近、高概率的收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传输集合滤波器（包括EnKF和EnSMF）在隐马尔可夫模型的状态分布近似中广泛应用，但其非渐近收敛特性缺乏严格的理论保证。本文旨在建立通用的概率框架来量化这类交互粒子系统的传播混沌行为。

Method: 采用同步耦合构造结合条件分布下的矩和尾部稳定性，以及交互粒子系统动力学传播的定量估计，建立Wasserstein度量下的非渐近、高概率路径收敛性证明。

Result: 识别了TEFs的极限平均场动力学；证明了交互粒子系统以蒙特卡洛速率收敛到平均场极限的独立同分布样本；收敛到平均场定律本身具有通常的维度依赖经验Wasserstein速率。

Conclusion: 首次为EnKF和EnSMF提供了非渐近、高概率的收敛保证，为传输集合滤波器的理论分析提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We develop a general probabilistic framework for analyzing propagation of chaos in transport ensemble filters (TEFs), a broad class of interacting particle systems that are used to approximate the sequence of state distributions in hidden Markov models given a history of observations. This class of transport-based filtering algorithms includes the widely used ensemble Kalman filter (EnKF), based on affine updates at each filtering step, as well as the ensemble stochastic map filter (EnSMF), which employs nonlinear updates. For this class, we identify the limiting mean-field dynamics. We then establish non-asymptotic, high-probability, pathwise Wasserstein convergence of the interacting particle system to an i.i.d. ensemble drawn from this mean-field limit at the Monte Carlo rate. Convergence to the mean-field law itself follows with the usual dimension-dependent empirical Wasserstein rate. The proof combines a synchronous coupling construction with stability of moments and tails under conditioning, together with quantitative estimates for the propagation of the underlying dynamics through the interacting particle system. Applying our theory to both the EnKF and the EnSMF yields the first non-asymptotic, high-probability convergence guarantees for TEFs.

</details>


### [159] [Closed-form solutions to some generalized variational inference problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25492)
*Hien Duy Nguyen,Jacob Westerhout*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了无限制测度层面的广义变分推断问题，针对f-散度、Bregman散度和Rényi散度等不同正则化惩罚，推导了闭式解公式（包括标量逆梯度密度公式、一维对偶恒等式、密度空间解和质量乘子等），并通过有限模型权重公式和共轭贝叶斯模型示例展示了与传统贝叶斯后验的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Donsker-Varadhan公式将普通贝叶斯后验刻画为KL散度正则化变分问题的解。广义变分推断用其他散度替代KL正则化，但现有研究常局限于参数化变分族。本文旨在研究无限制测度层面的广义变分问题，推导各类散度惩罚下的闭式解，填补理论空白。

Method: (1) 对于f-散度惩罚，推导了标量逆梯度密度公式和单变量对偶恒等式；(2) 对于反向f-散度和混合前向/反向KL惩罚，采用可分离积分原理处理；(3) 对于Bregman散度，得到带标量质量乘子的密度空间解；(4) 对于Rényi散度（阶数r>1），推导了归一化截断幂特征和阈值方程。

Result: 给出了KL散度、Cressie-Read散度、平方Hellinger距离、最小二乘、密度幂散度、Burg/Itakura-Saito散度及Rényi散度等各类惩罚下的闭式解形式，并通过有限模型权重公式和共轭贝叶斯示例验证了这些解的实际应用。

Conclusion: 本文为无限制测度层面的广义变分推断提供了系统性的闭式解理论，揭示了不同散度惩罚下最优后验的显式结构，为超越传统贝叶斯后验的推理方法奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: The Donsker--Varadhan formula characterizes the ordinary Bayesian posterior as the solution of an unrestricted $\mathsf{KL}$-regularized variational problem. Generalized variational inference replaces this regularizer by other divergences, but the resulting measure-valued optimization problem is often studied only after restriction to a parametric variational family. This paper studies the unrestricted measure-level problem. Given a measurable space $(\mathcal{Z},\mathfrak{Z})$, a prior probability measure $P$, a measurable loss $\ell:\mathcal{Z}\to(-\infty,\infty]$, a regularization strength $α>0$, and a divergence $\mathsf{D}(Q\Vert P)$, we seek probability measures in \[ \underset{Q\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{Z})}{\mathrm{arg\,min}}\left\{\int_{\mathcal{Z}} \ell\,\mathrm{d}Q+α\mathsf{D}(Q\Vert P)\right\}. \] For $f$-divergence penalties we derive a scalar inverse-gradient density formula and a one-dimensional dual identity; the Kullback--Leibler, Cressie--Read, and squared-Hellinger problems are treated as examples. Reverse $f$-divergences and mixed forward/reverse Kullback--Leibler penalties follow from the same separable integral principle. For Bregman divergences between densities we obtain a density-space solution with a scalar mass multiplier, including least-squares, density-power, and Burg/Itakura--Saito examples. For Rényi penalties of order $r>1$ we derive a normalized truncated-power characterization and a threshold equation for every global optimizer. Finite model-weight formulas and simple conjugate Bayesian model illustrations show how these closed forms are realized in practice and differ from the traditional solutions.

</details>


### [160] [Two-sample test with Wasserstein distance on Gaussian samples based on a log-normal approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25521)
*Johann Clément-Cottuz,Maxime Bérar,Gilles Gasso*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Wasserstein 2-距离的两样本检验方法，其中第一个样本通过对数正态分布近似假设为高斯分布，用于判断两个样本是否来自同一分布。该方法在合成数据上进行了评估，并扩展到多变量情形，同时证明了该检验与Shapiro-Wilk正态性检验密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出一种基于Wasserstein距离的两样本检验方法，特别是针对第一个样本可通过对数正态分布近似为高斯分布的场景，以判断两个样本是否来自同一分布。

Method: 基于Wasserstein 2-距离构建两样本检验统计量，假设第一个样本的潜在分布可由对数正态分布近似为高斯分布；在合成数据上评估方法性能，并扩展到多变量情形；通过理论分析揭示该检验与Shapiro-Wilk正态性检验的内在联系。

Result: 在合成数据上验证了所提方法的有效性；成功将单变量方法扩展到多变量情形；建立了与Shapiro-Wilk正态性检验的理论关联。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于Wasserstein距离的两样本检验方法在特定假设下有效，且与经典正态性检验存在密切联系，为分布比较提供了新的工具。

Abstract: In this article, we propose a two-sample test based on the Wasserstein 2-distance between two 1D samples, where the first sample is assumed to be Gaussian based on an approximation of the underlying distribution by a log-normal distribution. A two-sample test is performed to decide whether two samples are drawn from the same distribution or not. We evaluate the proposed method on synthetic data and extend it to the multivariate case. We also show that this test is closely related to the Shapiro-Wilk normality test.

</details>


### [161] [Pointwise Hurst Estimation via Scale Accumulation: A Noise-Robust Approach for Rough Volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25771)
*Jokubas Petkevicius*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出基于几何累积积分的时变Hölder指数(Hurst参数)逐点估计器，证明一致性、噪声鲁棒性及CLT，可消除微观结构噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有估计器只能从积分方差得到全局H，无法直接获取时变H(t)。

Method: 利用几何累积积分G_Lambda(t)=∫从Lambda到1 |eth_s X(t)| s^{-1} ds，其中eth_s X(t)=(X(t+s)-X(t))/s为尺度导数。

Result: 证明一致性、噪声鲁棒性（显式阈值Lambda*=sigma^{1/H}）及CLT（收敛速率(log Lambda)^{-1/2}）。

Conclusion: 该估计器逐点定义、有限分辨率实现、通过尺度分离消除微观结构噪声，可直接从价格路径得到时变H(t)。

Abstract: We introduce an estimator for the pointwise, time-varying Hölder exponent (Hurst parameter) of a stochastic process, based on the geometry accumulation integral G_Lambda(t) = integral from Lambda to 1 of |eth_s X(t)| s^{-1} ds, where eth_s X(t) = (X(t+s)-X(t))/s is the scale derivative at resolution s. We prove consistency, noise robustness with explicit threshold Lambda* = sigma^{1/H}, and a CLT at rate (log Lambda)^{-1/2}. The estimator is pointwise in time, defined at finite resolution, and eliminates microstructure noise by scale separation. Existing estimators give a global H from integrated variance; this gives a time-varying H(t) directly from the price path.

</details>


### [162] [Group invariance of $f$-divergences and the Fisher--Rao distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25790)
*Frank Nielsen,Kazuki Okamura*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究群作用对称性如何影响分布比较。在变换模型假设下，证明所有f-散度在群作用下保持不变，因此不变散度仅依赖于参数对的最大不变量。当参数空间作用可递时，该最大不变量由双陪集给出。将结果应用于多维位置-尺度族，并证明相同的约简适用于Fisher-Rao距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多统计模型具有由群作用描述的自然对称性，理解这些对称性如何影响分布之间的比较是统计推断中的重要理论问题。

Method: 在变换模型框架下（群同时作用于样本空间和参数空间，密度乘子变换），分析f-散度在群作用下的不变性，利用最大不变量理论进行参数约简。

Result: 所有f-散度在群作用下不变；不变散度仅依赖于参数对的最大不变量；参数空间作用可递时，最大不变量由双陪集给出；该约简适用于Fisher-Rao距离。

Conclusion: 群对称性可大幅简化分布比较问题，将高维参数比较降维到低维不变量空间，为位置-尺度族等常见模型提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Many statistical models have natural symmetries described by a group action. We study how such symmetries affect the comparison of two distributions. We work with a transformation model in which a group acts on both the sample space and the parameter space, and the densities transform with a multiplier. Under this assumption, we show that every $f$-divergence is invariant under the group action. As a consequence, an invariant divergence depends only on a maximal invariant of the pair of parameters. When the action on the parameter space is transitive, this maximal invariant is given by a double coset. We apply this result to multidimensional location-scale families, and we show that the same reduction applies to the Fisher--Rao distance.

</details>


### [163] [Finite-sample bounds for regularized optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25947)
*Alberto González-Sanz,Marcel Nutz,Austin J. Stromme*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了正则化最优传输的样本复杂度，针对一般凸正则化（包括KL散度和L^p惩罚），给出了经验代价的非渐近偏差和方差界，并显式依赖于正则化参数和边缘分布的内在维度。结果同时改进、统一和扩展了现有有限样本界，特别改进了熵正则化最优传输的最新结果，并首次获得L^p正则化（1<p<∞）的完全定量结果。对于二次传输代价，发现L^2正则化估计未正则化最优传输代价的速率为n^{-2/(d+4)}，这是目前基于正则化最优传输的估计器可达到的最快非渐近速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为一般凸正则化下的最优传输问题建立统一的非渐近样本复杂度理论，填补L^p正则化缺乏定量有限样本结果的空白，并提升熵正则化最优传输的现有界。

Method: 通过理论分析分别推导偏差界和方差界，显式刻画正则化参数λ与边缘分布内在维度对样本复杂度的影响。对KL散度（熵正则化）和L^p惩罚（1<p<∞）两种主要正则化类型分别给出非渐近界，并特别关注L^2正则化在二次传输代价下的表现。

Result: （1）改进并统一了熵正则化最优传输的现有有限样本界；（2）首次给出L^p正则化（1<p<∞）的完整定量非渐近结果；（3）对于二次传输代价，L^2正则化估计未正则化最优传输代价的速率为n^{-2/(d+4)}，该速率是目前基于正则化最优传输的所有估计器中最快的非渐近速率。

Conclusion: 本文建立的正则化最优传输样本复杂度理论，在统一框架下同时改进了熵正则化结果并开创了L^p正则化的定量分析，其中L^2正则化在二次代价下达到的最优速率具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: We study the sample complexity of regularized optimal transport for general convex regularizations including the Kullback--Leibler divergence and $L^p$ penalties. Our main results are non-asymptotic bias and variance bounds for the empirical cost, with explicit dependence on the regularization parameter and on the intrinsic dimension of the marginals. Our approach simultaneously improves, unifies, and extends existing finite-sample bounds. In particular, we improve the state of the art for entropic optimal transport, and we obtain the first fully quantitative results for $L^p$ regularization with $1<p<\infty$. For the quadratic transport cost, we deduce that quadratically regularized optimal transport (i.e., $L^2$ regularization) estimates the unregularized optimal transport cost at rate $n^{-2/(d+4)}$, the fastest non-asymptotic rate currently available for any estimator based on regularized optimal transport.

</details>


### [164] [Bayesian Nonparametric Privacy-Preserving Synthetic Data Generation: I. Discrete Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26073)
*Maria Chiara Menicucci,Mario Beraha,Stefano Favaro,Riccardo Lazzarini*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Pitman-Yor过程先验的贝叶斯非参数框架，用于离散数据的隐私保护合成数据生成，并研究了其在不同折扣参数区间下的差分隐私保证和统计效用，刻画了隐私-效用的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据生成需要在隐私保证和统计效用之间取得平衡。现有的方法对离散数据（特别是类别数量未知或增长的场景）处理不足，缺乏一个既能提供严格隐私保证又能保持良好统计效用的统一框架。

Method: 将机密数据建模为来自未知离散分布的随机样本，该分布赋予Pitman-Yor过程先验；合成数据从对应的后验预测分布中生成。分析了折扣参数σ∈(-∞,1)三种区间下的差分隐私保证，并利用期望1-Wasserstein距离评估统计效用。

Result: 当σ∈(0,1)时，建立了实例级(ε,δ)-差分隐私保证；σ=0（Dirichlet过程先验）和σ<0（参数Dirichlet-Multinomial模型）在适当条件下获得更强的隐私保证。对于σ<0和σ=0，证明了经验分布在1-Wasserstein度量下的一致性并推导出显式收敛速率。

Conclusion: 该框架自然适用于含重复值及类别数量未知/增长的数据。隐私-效用权衡关系明确：更强的隐私保证需要对发布样本量施加更严格的限制，从而减缓向真实数据生成分布的收敛速度。

Abstract: Synthetic data generation is a powerful approach to privacy-preserving statistical analysis, where data-release mechanisms are governed by a privacy-utility tradeoff: they should provide privacy guarantees while preserving the statistical utility of confidential data. We develop a Bayesian nonparametric framework for private synthetic data generation tailored to discrete data. Specifically, the confidential data are modeled as a random sample from an unknown discrete distribution endowed with a Pitman-Yor process prior, and synthetic data are generated from the corresponding posterior-predictive distribution. Since the Pitman-Yor process defines an almost surely discrete random probability measure, the resulting mechanism is naturally suited to data with ties and settings involving a potentially large, unknown, or growing number of categories. We study differential privacy guarantees of the Pitman-Yor posterior-predictive mechanism across the three regimes of the discount parameter $σ\in(-\infty,1)$. For $σ\in(0,1)$, we establish an instance-level $(\varepsilon,δ)$-differential privacy guarantee. For $σ=0$ and $σ<0$, corresponding respectively to the Dirichlet process prior and to a parametric Dirichlet-Multinomial model, stronger guarantees are obtained, under suitable conditions on the released sample size. We also investigate statistical utility, or informativity, of the released data via the expected $1$-Wasserstein distance between the empirical distribution of the synthetic data and the "true" data-generating distribution. For $σ<0$ and $σ=0$, we prove consistency of the empirical distribution in this metric and derive explicit convergence rates, making precise the privacy-utility tradeoff: stronger privacy guarantees impose more restrictive choices of the released sample size, slowing down convergence to the "true" data-generating distribution.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [A Single Stepsize Suffices for Unprojected Linear TD(0): Simultaneous Robust and Fast Rates via Polyak--Ruppert Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24981)
*Wei-Cheng Lee,Francesco Orabona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究马尔可夫采样下线性TD(0)算法，提出一种依赖于混合时间的步长调度，在不使用投影和曲率先验知识的情况下，实现了PR平均的鲁棒高概率收敛保证，同时获得无曲率依赖和有曲率依赖两种速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TD(0)算法通常需要投影或基于曲率参数的稳定性论证来保证有界性，且高概率收敛分析较为缺乏。本文旨在消除这些限制，提供无需投影、无需曲率先验知识的鲁棒收敛保证。

Method: 采用依赖于混合时间τ_mix的步长调度η_t ∝ 1/(τ_mix log(t)√t)；利用泊松方程工具包分解几何混合马尔可夫链的噪声为鞅项和可控余项；提出基于路径稳定的自举归纳论证。

Result: PR平均同时获得两种速率：无曲率依赖的Õ(τ_mix/√T)和曲率依赖的Õ(τ_mix^2/(ωT))，最终取两者最小值。迭代序列自动一致有界，无需投影。

Conclusion: 提出的步长调度能在无投影、无曲率先验条件下实现TD(0)算法的鲁棒高概率收敛，泊松方程工具包为马尔可夫噪声分析提供了有效框架。

Abstract: We study linear TD(0) under Markovian sampling, where data are generated along a single trajectory. We provide high-probability guarantees for a plain unprojected TD(0) algorithm with Polyak-Ruppert (PR) averaging, using a single stepsize schedule $η_t \propto \frac{1}{τ_{\mathrm{mix}}\log(t)\sqrt{t}}$ that depends on the mixing time but requires no prior knowledge of the curvature parameter $ω$. Our first result shows that such a choice of the stepsize guarantees that the TD(0) iterates are automatically and uniformly bounded with high probability, without projections and without any stability argument based on $ω$. Building on this result, we establish a simultaneous high-probability convergence guarantee for the PR average: the same stepsize yields both a robust curvature-free $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\!\left(\frac{τ_{\mathrm{mix}}}{\sqrt{T}}\right)$ rate and a fast curvature-dependent $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\!\left(\frac{τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^2}{ωT}\right)$rate, with the bound taking the minimum of the two. The core technical ingredient is a Poisson-equation toolkit for geometrically mixing Markov chains, which decomposes Markov noise into a martingale term plus a controlled remainder and enables a new self-bounding inductive argument for pathwise stability.

</details>


### [166] [Latent Block-Diffusion Temporal Point Processes: A Semi-Autoregressive Framework for Asynchronous Event Sequence Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24982)
*Shuai Zhang,Yancheng Chen,Chuan Zhou,Yang Liu,Xixun Lin,Xiangyu Zhao,Jun Zhu,Zhi-Ming Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LBDTPP，一种半自回归时间点过程框架，通过潜在空间中的块级扩散机制实现高质量、变长事件序列生成，在理论上推导出Wasserstein误差界证明块级生成可减少误差累积，实验结果表明该方法在六个真实世界数据集上优于现有基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归时间点过程方法在多步生成中存在误差累积问题，而非自回归扩散方法通常只能生成固定长度序列，缺乏长度灵活性。为同时解决这两个问题，本文提出潜在块扩散机制。

Method: 提出LBDTPP框架，在潜在空间中定义事件块上的自回归概率分布，并在每个块内执行高斯扩散。通过顺序生成块的同时并行采样块内事件，结合自回归的长度灵活性和扩散模型的并行高质量生成能力。

Result: 在六个真实世界基准数据集上，LBDTPP在无条件和条件生成任务中均优于最先进的时间点过程基线方法。经验分析验证了潜在空间扩散和块级生成的优势，并揭示了生成质量与块大小之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 理论推导了Wasserstein误差界，表明在合适的局部逼近和前缀稳定性假设下，块级生成相比逐事件自回归生成可减少误差累积。

Abstract: Modeling and sampling from the underlying distribution of asynchronous event sequences are crucial in various real-world applications, including social networks, medical diagnosis, and financial transactions. Existing autoregressive methods suffer from error accumulation during multi-step generation, while non-autoregressive diffusion methods are typically limited to fixed-length output sequences. In this paper, we propose Latent Block-Diffusion Temporal Point Processes (LBDTPP), a novel semi-autoregressive TPP framework that introduces a latent block diffusion mechanism for high-quality and variable-length event sequence generation. The core idea is to define an autoregressive probability distribution over event blocks in latent space and perform Gaussian diffusion within each block. By sequentially generating blocks while simultaneously sampling events in each block, LBDTPP preserves the length flexibility of autoregressive TPPs and inherits the parallel high-quality generation capability of diffusion models. Theoretically, we derive Wasserstein error bounds showing that, under suitable local approximation and prefix-stability assumptions, block-wise generation can reduce error accumulation compared with event-wise autoregressive generation. Extensive experiments on six real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate that LBDTPP outperforms state-of-the-art TPP baselines in both unconditional and conditional generation tasks. Further empirical analyses verify the benefits of latent-space diffusion and block-wise generation, and reveal the trade-off between generation quality and block size. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zh-Shuai/LBDTPP.

</details>


### [167] [Training for the Model You Return: Improving Optimization for Iterate-Averaged Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25086)
*Kwok Chun Au,Adam Block*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE方法，通过将优化器设计形式化为最优控制问题，在迭代平均估计器场景下改进AdamW，在二次模型上可任意倍数降低极限平方误差，并在1-2B参数LM微调和GPT-2预训练中取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LM pipeline常返回平均模型（如EMA）而非最终迭代，但现有训练流程并未针对"返回平均模型"这一场景进行优化，作者希望回答：既然要返回迭代平均，如何调整训练以提升该平均的性能？

Method: 将针对迭代平均估计器的优化器设计形式化为连续时间随机二次模型下的最优控制问题，求解最优控制策略，随后近似得到PACE——一个轻量级AdamW包装器，将实时权重用裁剪后的逐坐标控制强度拉向EMA。

Result: 证明风格化PACE在标准随机凸优化率下收敛（仅依赖平均规则的常数因子）；在二次设置中可严格降低迭代平均估计器的极限平方误差，且某些实例上可任意倍数降低；在1-2B参数LM监督微调和GPT-2预训练中，PACE在不同学习率、衰减调度等超参数下一致优于AdamW及EMA评估的AdamW。

Conclusion: 针对返回迭代平均的训练场景，通过控制理论视角设计的PACE能有效提升平均模型性能，理论与实验均验证其有效性。

Abstract: Many modern Language Model (LM) pipelines return an averaged model, such as an exponential moving average of the training iterates, rather than the final iterate itself. This raises a fundamental question: given that we will return an iterate average, how should we change training to improve the performance of this average? We study this question by formulating optimizer design for the iterate-average estimator as an optimal-control problem. In a continuous-time stochastic quadratic model, we solve for the control strategy that minimizes the error of the returned average subject to a penalty on the size of the intervention. A practical approximation to this controller yields PACE, a lightweight wrapper around AdamW that pulls the live weights toward their exponential moving average with a clipped, per-coordinate control strength. We prove that a stylized version of PACE converges at the standard stochastic convex optimization rate, up to a factor depending on the averaging rule, while in the quadratic setting it can strictly improve the limiting squared error of the iterate-average estimator and can do so by an arbitrarily large factor on some instances. Empirically, our results suggest that PACE improves over AdamW and EMA-evaluated AdamW in supervised fine-tuning of 1-2B parameter LMs and in GPT-2 pretraining on FineWeb for a wide range of learning rates, decay schedules, and other hyperparameters.

</details>


### [168] [Efficient Adaptive Data Acquisition via Pretrained Belief Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25197)
*Daolang Huang,Zhuoyue Huang,Conor Hassan,Luigi Acerbi,Samuel Kaski,Tom Rainforth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POLAR（策略学习与信念表示）是一种将表示学习与策略学习解耦的通用框架，利用预训练预测基础模型作为信念状态编码器，在贝叶斯实验设计、贝叶斯优化和主动学习等自适应数据采集任务中，以更少的训练样本超越当前最先进的摊销方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应数据采集方法存在两大局限：基于后验的方法依赖可能设定错误或有偏的替代模型和后验近似；直接策略学习方法从历史观测映射，未能利用可用的模型表示，导致学习困难。需要一种能结合两者优势的统一方法。

Method: POLAR将表示学习与策略学习解耦：利用预训练的预测基础模型作为信念状态编码器（belief-state encoder），在其表示之上训练策略头（policy head）。不同任务仅通过训练策略时使用的任务特定效用函数（utility）来区分，形成统一的摊销策略学习框架。

Result: POLAR在多种任务上超越当前最先进的摊销方法，同时所需训练样本显著更少，在摊销数据采集的可扩展性和效率上取得了重要进展。

Conclusion: POLAR证明了利用预训练基础模型作为信念编码器可以有效地解耦表示学习与策略学习，为自适应数据采集提供了一种更高效、可扩展的统一框架。

Abstract: Learning effective policies for adaptive data acquisition remains challenging: posterior-based methods rely on surrogate models and posterior approximations that can be misspecified or biased, while direct policy-learning methods map from historical observations and fail to exploit available model representations, making learning harder. We introduce policy learning with belief representations (POLAR), based on the insight that optimal data acquisition depends on the observation history only through a sufficient belief state. Specifically, POLAR decouples representation learning from policy learning by leveraging pretrained predictive foundation models as belief-state encoders, training a policy head on top of their representations. This yields a simple, unified amortised policy learning framework for Bayesian experimental design, Bayesian optimisation, and active learning, differing only in the task-specific utility used to train the policy. Empirically, we find that POLAR outperforms state-of-the-art amortised methods across diverse tasks while requiring far fewer training samples, demonstrating a significant step in the scalability and efficiency of amortised data acquisition.

</details>


### [169] [Dense Supervision Is Not Enough: The Readout Blind Spot in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24898)
*Rituraj Sharma,Tu Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究循环语言模型中交叉熵损失实际控制的隐藏状态变量。发现密集的每循环交叉熵只控制读出器暴露的变量，而非所有循环状态变量。隐藏状态尺度存在具体失效模式：RMSNorm和LayerNorm等尺度不变读出器将径向尺度对交叉熵隐藏，而残差循环继续携带更新该尺度。在44M和129M的无层间归一化循环Transformer中，通过RMSNorm读出器的每循环交叉熵仍导致最终隐藏状态范数达数千或数万。尺度可见读出器和显式范数惩罚可将范数控制在数十，尺度移除循环是互补架构修复。设计规则：密集监督训练出口；循环尺度控制需使尺度对损失可见或将其移出循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型将隐藏状态作为运行时状态解码并反馈到未来计算，这产生了基本的监督问题：交叉熵实际控制哪些状态变量？作者旨在澄清交叉熵损失在循环模型中的控制范围，特别是隐藏状态尺度这一具体失效模式。

Method: 通过理论分析和实验验证，研究不同读出器（RMSNorm、LayerNorm等尺度不变读出器 vs 尺度可见读出器）下交叉熵对隐藏状态变量的控制效果。在44M和129M参数的无层间归一化循环Transformer上进行实验，对比不同读出器和正则化策略的效果。

Result: 每循环交叉熵通过RMSNorm读出器仍导致最终隐藏状态范数达数千或数万；尺度可见读出器和显式范数惩罚可将范数控制在数十量级；尺度控制变体在变深度基准测试中实现了更低的困惑度。

Conclusion: 密集监督训练出口有效；循环尺度控制需要使尺度对损失可见或将其从循环中移除。这一设计规则简单且实用：尺度控制的变体在匹配推理深度的操作点上获得更低的困惑度。

Abstract: Looped language models turn hidden states into runtime state: each state is decoded for prediction and fed back into future computation. This creates a basic supervision question: which state variables does cross-entropy actually control? We show that dense per-loop cross-entropy controls the variables exposed by the readout, not every variable active in the recurrent transition. Hidden-state scale gives a concrete failure mode. Scale-invariant readouts such as RMSNorm and LayerNorm hide radial scale from the immediate cross-entropy loss, while pre-norm residual recurrence continues to carry and update that same scale. Thus per-loop loss can make early exits usable without controlling recurrent scale. In 44M and 129M looped transformers without inter-loop normalization, per-loop cross-entropy through RMSNorm readouts still drives final hidden-state norms into the thousands or tens of thousands. Scale-visible readouts and explicit norm penalties keep norms in the tens, and scale-removing recurrence is the complementary architectural fix. The resulting design rule is simple: dense supervision trains exits; recurrent scale control requires either making scale visible to a loss or removing it from the loop. Consistent with this rule, scale-controlled variants achieve lower perplexity at matched inference-depth operating points in our variable-depth benchmarks.

</details>


### [170] [From Meta Idea to Advanced Mathematical Discovery -- Human-AI Co-Discovery of Sign-Embedding Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24899)
*Yanqiao Wang,Jin-Peng Liu,Peng Li,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过符号嵌入量子算法案例研究，展示了AI辅助系统（如AIM）在数学研究的问题形成、路线探索、连接发现和推导验证等阶段的协作价值，强调人类在关键科研判断中的主导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI辅助数学研究聚焦于解决已定义好的问题，但实际数学进步往往始于将模糊直觉转化为具体问题、可行路线和可证明定理的早期阶段。本文旨在探索这一阶段中人类与AI协同发现的工作流程价值。

Method: 通过符号嵌入量子算法这一案例研究，采用人类初始直觉（有理逼近对跳跃型函数有效）驱动，结合AI辅助探索（后集成到AIM系统中）进行路线扩展、候选公式比较、框架收敛，以及后续的连接发现、证明草稿和复杂度计算，最终由人类主导关键科学判断。

Result: AI辅助探索成功将人类直觉扩展为路线图，帮助建立了符号嵌入作为核心框架，连接了矩阵符号恒等式与更广泛的矩阵方程和矩阵函数类，并完成了证明和复杂度分析的草稿。人类在路线选择、拒绝无效近似（Cayley-trapezoidal）和优化实现（Sylvester实现从粗粒度二次间隙查询到最终因子化缩放分析）中做出了决定性判断。

Conclusion: 人类-AI协同发现工作流（以AIM为重要组件）最有价值的角色不是作为独立的定理证明器，而是作为研究伙伴，在人类主导的研究循环中参与问题形成、连接发现、推导和批判性审查。

Abstract: AI-assisted mathematics is often evaluated on solving predefined problems. In practice, however, many important advances begin earlier, when a vague research intuition is transformed into a concrete problem, a promising route, and a theorem family worth proving. This report studies that stage through a case study that led to sign-embedding quantum algorithms for matrix equations and matrix functions, foundational primitives in quantum linear algebra and operator-output quantum algorithms. The project began with a human-originated intuition that rational approximation is especially effective for jump-type functions such as the sign function, and might therefore serve as a design principle for quantum algorithms. Rather than merely assisting after the problem was fixed, AI-assisted exploration, including workflows later integrated into the agentic AI-mathematician system AIM, played a key role in expanding this intuition into a route map, comparing candidate formulations, and converging toward sign embedding as the central framework. AIM then helped connect a known matrix-sign identity to wider classes of matrix equations and matrix functions, and drafted proof and complexity calculations.
