  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低成本重建方法多基于矩阵分解，需要将高维多模态数据展平为二维快照矩阵，从而丢失了重要的结构信息。作者旨在开发一种保留数据自然张量结构的稀疏感知重建方法，以更好地利用空间、时间和物理变量维度间的相关性。

Method: 提出了低计算成本高阶奇异值分解（lcHOSVD），将稀疏传感与HOSVD相结合，在保持张量结构的同时大幅降低传统HOSVD的计算需求。方法应用于城市流场和空气质量数据集，仅使用1-4%的空间位置重建三维速度场和污染物浓度场。

Result: 与lcSVD相比，lcHOSVD在强多维耦合和异构动态配置中持续实现更低的重建误差；张量公式对不均匀传感器分布具有显著更强的鲁棒性，更贴合实际环境监测网络情况。

Conclusion: lcHOSVD是首个将稀疏传感与HOSVD结合用于场重建的方法，为高维环境数据重建提供了一种保留多维结构、计算高效且对传感器分布不均鲁棒的新框架。

Abstract: Urban flow and air-quality simulations generate high-dimensional datasets describing velocity and pollutant transport across multiple spatial, temporal, and physical-variable dimensions. Reconstructing these fields from sparse sensor measurements is a fundamental challenge in environmental monitoring, digital twins, forecasting, and data assimilation. Existing low-cost reconstruction approaches are commonly based on matrix decompositions, which require multidimensional datasets to be flattened into two-dimensional snapshot matrices, thereby discarding important structural information. This work introduces the low-cost High-Order Singular Value Decomposition (lcHOSVD), a novel tensor-based sparse-sensing reconstruction framework for high-dimensional environmental fields. To the authors' knowledge, this is the first methodology that combines sparse sensing and HOSVD for field reconstruction. Unlike matrix-based approaches, lcHOSVD preserves the natural tensor structure of the data, enabling the exploitation of correlations across spatial, temporal, and physical-variable dimensions while substantially reducing the computational requirements of conventional HOSVD. The methodology is applied to urban flow and air-quality datasets, where three-dimensional velocity and pollutant concentration fields are reconstructed using only 1-4% of the available spatial locations. While lcSVD provides larger computational speed-ups, lcHOSVD consistently achieves lower reconstruction errors in configurations characterized by strong multidimensional coupling and heterogeneous dynamics across dimensions. Additional sensor-anisotropy analyses demonstrate that the tensor formulation is significantly more robust to uneven sensor distributions, a common situation in practical environmental monitoring networks.

</details>


### [206] [Uncertainty-aware reinforcement learning for chemical language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24990)
*Borja Medina,Jon Paul Janet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强化学习在分子设计中忽略预测不确定性的问题，提出两种将不确定性纳入RL的方法：作为优化目标或用于调节策略更新。实验表明不确定性感知的RL能更鲁棒地探索化学空间，将真实命中率从0.5提升至0.75，真实命中数几乎翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL框架将所有评分函数视为确定性预言机，忽略分子属性预测中的固有不确定性，导致策略探索高不确定区域，生成训练数据支持不足的高分分子，优化过程不稳定且预测偏离真实值。

Method: 提出两种互补方式：1) 将不确定性作为额外优化目标，与评分函数共同优化，实现开发与可靠性的权衡；2) 利用不确定性调节策略更新，降低属性超出评分函数置信域的分子的影响。分别在三种设置下评估：可控模型系统（预测误差为高斯分布）、ChemProp模型、基于共形预测的随机森林分类器。

Result: 不确定性感知的RL使化学语言模型更鲁棒地探索化学空间，偏向低不确定性区域，在不牺牲分子评分的前提下提高真实命中率0.25（从0.5到0.75），真实命中数几乎翻倍。

Conclusion: 将预测不确定性纳入强化学习能提升分子设计的可靠性和鲁棒性，实现更可靠的命中发现。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become a powerful paradigm for de novo molecular design, enabling Chemical Language Models (CLMs) to navigate and explore the chemical space while optimizing specific desired properties. However, the existing RL frameworks treat all scoring functions as deterministic oracles, neglecting the inherent uncertainty attached to the predictions of the different molecular properties. This can lead to the exploration of highly-uncertain regions of the chemical space, focusing on the generation of highly scored molecules which are poorly supported by the training data. This can destabilize the optimization process, yielding predictions that are far from their true values.
  We propose and compare two complementary ways of incorporating predictive uncertainty into RL. In the first one, uncertainty is treated as an additional optimization objective and incorporated along with the rest of the scoring functions, allowing the policy to trade off exploitation against reliability. Secondly, uncertainty is used to modulate policy updates, reducing the influence of molecules whose properties lie far outside the scoring function confidence domain.
  Both approaches were evaluated across three different settings: (i) a controlled model system, in which the prediction error is modeled as a Gaussian distribution, with a variance proportional to the distance to the training data; and two real-world tasks, making use of (ii) ChemProp models and (iii) a Conformal Prediction wrapper applied to a Random forest classifier.
  We show that uncertainty-aware RL enables CLMs to explore chemical space more robustly by favoring lower-uncertainty regions. This leads to more reliable hit discovery without compromising molecular score, increasing the true hit rate by 0.25 (from 0.5 to 0.75), and nearly doubling the total number of true hits.

</details>


### [207] [The Geometry of Sequential Learning: Lie-Bracket Prediction of Transfer Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24993)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将序列学习（如领域自适应、指令微调、DPO）中的顺序依赖问题重新建模为几何锦标赛问题，利用李括号交换子（梯度更新场的交换子）作为可计算的几何量来评估数据源之间的先后顺序，并设计了一种李括号锦标赛方法，仅需O(N)次点积和O(N log N)排序即可高效规划多领域课程，在指令微调、DPO、预训练领域迁移及扩散模型上均取得高准确率，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列学习中，从N个候选数据源选择训练顺序会产生N!种可能的课程，但局部顺序的影响机制尚不明确，缺乏可计算的几何度量来指导课程规划，传统方法需要O(N^2)的成对比较，计算成本过高。

Method: 定义李括号交换子作为梯度更新场的几何量，量化两个数据源A→B与B→A的优劣；基于此设计李括号锦标赛，利用共享目标梯度参考和海森矩阵对称性，通过一次海森-向量积和O(N)点积计算Borda/行和分数，再经O(N log N)排序得到全局课程顺序，无需显式构建O(N^2)边矩阵。

Result: 在指令微调中k=1时成对准确率98.1%，DPO中98.9%；k=20时分别为73.1%和72.2%；在四个大语言模型上预训练领域迁移准确率82.4-92.0%，扩散模型91.1%；在3!种课程中87.5%恢复最优顺序；对85个Stack编程语言源域为Python目标域排序达到99th采样百分位；对56个MMLU科目达到99.0-99.6th采样百分位，远高于下降梯度范数基线。

Conclusion: 序列学习可被重新表述为几何锦标赛问题，李括号交换子不仅提供局部成对顺序信息，还构成了可扩展的多领域课程规划原语，为理解序列学习中的顺序依赖性提供了新的几何视角和高效求解方案。

Abstract: Sequential learning is order-dependent: from Pile-style next-token domain adaptation to instruction-SFT and DPO, N candidate sources induce N! possible curricula. We show that the local order effect is governed by a computable geometric quantity, the Lie-bracket commutator of gradient update fields, yielding a pairwise score for whether A->B or B->A is better for a target domain. The pairwise bracket primitive also defines a Lie-Bracket Tournament: with a shared theta_0 target-gradient reference, Hessian symmetry gives Borda/row-sum scores from one Hessian-vector product per source, O(N) dot products, and an O(N log N) sort, without materializing the O(N^2) edge matrix. Empirically, the planner reaches 98.1%/98.9% pairwise accuracy at k=1 for instruction-SFT/DPO, remains at 73.1%/72.2% at k=20, and preserves the original pretraining-domain evidence with 82.4-92.0% accuracy across four LLMs and 91.1% on diffusion. At curriculum scale, it recovers the best of all 3! schedules in 87.5% of trials, ranks 85 Stack programming-language source domains for a Python target in the 99th sampled percentile, and reaches the 99.0-99.6th sampled percentile on 56 MMLU subjects, sharply above the reported descending gradient-norm baseline. These results reframe sequential learning as a geometric tournament problem: commutators provide both local pairwise order information and a scalable primitive for many-domain schedules.

</details>


### [208] [ExTra: Exploratory Trajectory Optimization for Language Model Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24994)
*Wenyang Hu,Junxiang Jia,Zhen Shu,Daniel Dahlmeier,See-Kiong Ng,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强化学习在语言模型推理任务中因任务难度两极分化导致探索不足的问题，提出ExTra框架，通过新颖性奖励和熵引导前缀再生两种机制，在GRPO基础上提升模型在数学推理任务上的pass@1和pass@16性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR训练中，简单任务产生全正确、低多样性的rollout组，梯度信号弱；困难任务则产生全错误的rollout组，无正奖励信号。现有方法无法有效从模型自身rollout中提取探索信号，亟需一种能跨越难度两极、提升探索效率的机制。

Method: ExTra包含两个协同机制：(i) 新颖性奖励：在GRPO归一化后加入基于嵌入的多样性奖励，奖励多样化的正确解决方案；(ii) 熵引导前缀再生：利用熵信号对部分轨迹进行评分，从有希望的中间步骤继续探索，实现更深入的搜索。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准上，Qwen3-1.7B使用ExTra相比GRPO，pass@1提升约5个百分点，pass@16提升约7个百分点，表明轨迹级探索信号同时改善了单样本准确率和推理时覆盖度。

Conclusion: ExTra验证了在GRPO流程中引入轨迹级探索信号（多样性奖励+熵引导搜索）能有效缓解RLVR的探索困境，显著提升模型在数学推理任务上的表现，且不依赖外部数据或模型。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) for language-model reasoning can fail at both extremes of task difficulty: easy prompts often produce all-correct, low-diversity rollout groups with little gradient signal, while hard prompts can produce all-incorrect groups with no positive reward. We introduce ExTra (Exploratory Trajectory Optimization), a GRPO-compatible framework that extracts exploration signals from the model's own rollouts. ExTra combines two mechanisms: (i) a novelty reward that adds embedding-based diversity bonuses after GRPO normalization, rewarding diverse correct solutions; and (ii) entropy-guided prefix regeneration, which scores partial trajectories using entropy signals and continues exploration from promising intermediate steps. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, ExTra improves Qwen3-1.7B over GRPO by about +5 points on pass@1 and +7 points on pass@16, showing that trajectory-level exploration signals can improve both single-sample accuracy and inference-time coverage.

</details>


### [209] [Are Tabular Foundation Models Robust to Realistic Query Distribution Shifts in Microbiome Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24995)
*Giulia Perciballi,Ahmad Fall,Federica Granese,Edi Prifti,Jean-Daniel Zucker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了表格基础模型(TFMs)在微生物组丰度数据上面对现实分布偏移时的鲁棒性。通过六个人类肠道微生物组数据集、三种生物启发式扰动策略（去除高丰度分类群、稀疏化、零填充）的实验发现：保留判别性特征不足以保证稳定性，零填充扰动破坏性最大，稀疏化对TFMs的影响比随机森林更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型(TFMs)在微生物组丰度数据上表现优异，但其在现实分布偏移下的鲁棒性尚未得到充分研究，这限制了该类模型在真实生物医学场景中的可信部署。

Method: 构建涵盖六个肠道微生物组数据集、四种疾病场景的基准测试。采用上下文学习设置，模型接收未扰动的支持集作为上下文，在扰动的查询样本上评估。设计三种可控扰动策略：去除高丰度（非信息性）分类群、通过增加零膨胀进行稀疏化、通过注入虚假非零值进行零填充。保留最具判别力的分类群以隔离超越"捷径"特征的鲁棒性。

Result: 所有扰动均导致模型性能下降，其中零填充扰动一致性地造成最大损害；稀疏化扰动对TFMs的影响超过传统的随机森林基线模型，表明TFMs对零膨胀类偏移更敏感；保护判别性特征不足以保证支持-查询偏移下的稳定性。

Conclusion: 即使保留关键分类群，破坏全局特征结构（如零填充）也会打破模型的泛化能力；TFMs对零膨胀型分布偏移的敏感性高于传统方法，提示在真实应用中对这类偏移需格外关注。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) achieve strong performance on microbiome abundance data, yet their robustness under realistic distribution shift remains poorly characterized. We introduce a benchmark that evaluates the robustness of TFMs to biologically inspired perturbations across six gut microbiome datasets spanning four disease contexts. In this in-context learning setting, models receive unperturbed support sets as context and are evaluated on perturbed query samples. To isolate robustness beyond "shortcut" features, we preserve the most discriminative taxa and apply three controlled perturbation strategies: (i) removal of high-abundance (uninformative) taxa, (ii) sparsification via increased zero-inflation, and (iii) zero-imputation via spurious non-zero injections. Our results show that protecting discriminative features is insufficient to guarantee stability under support-query shift: across datasets, all perturbations degrade model performance, with zero-imputation consistently the most harmful, indicating that corrupting global feature structure can break generalization even when key taxa are retained. Sparsification disproportionately affects TFMs relative to a classical random forest baseline, suggesting greater sensitivity to zero-inflation-type shifts. The code is publicly available at: https://github.com/UMMISCO/metagenomics-fm/.

</details>


### [210] [From Forecasting Leaderboards to Deployment Decisions: A Fail-Closed Certification Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24996)
*Geumyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“故障闭锁认证协议”，用于判断排行榜上的预测冠军在特定部署接口（如警报阈值、预算约束、切换成本）下是否真正可部署执行。通过严格的统计检验和真实案例（Traffic-Hourly）表明，正摩擦成本会导致预测冠军在部署中表现次优，协议可有效防止虚假反转声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排行榜上的预测冠军常被直接解读为部署首选，但通过固定决策接口（如警报阈值、top-k预算、切换成本策略）传递预测时，这一解读可能失败。需要一种协议来认证预测冠军在指定接口和效用下是否真正可部署执行。

Method: 引入一种“故障闭锁认证协议”，其门控条件包括：摩擦导致的、非平局、统计支持且可复现的部署侧反转。通过锁定的原生审计测试过度声明，在22个验证候选者和362个全网格单元上进行检验。

Result: 在Traffic-Hourly数据中，零摩擦下冠军一致，但正的切换摩擦使预测冠军在部署中次优。在155个看似预测/部署冠军反转的案例中，协议成功阻止了认证。

Conclusion: 该协议并非提出新预测器、新指标或通用效用，而是一种保守的认证方法，用于决定何时应将排行榜预测冠军解读为可部署执行的首选建议。

Abstract: Forecasting leaderboards rank models by predictive quality, but their winners are often read as deployment-ready top-1 advice. That reading can fail when forecasts are passed through a fixed decision interface, such as an alert threshold, a top-k budget, or a switching-cost policy. We study when a forecast-side winner can be certified as deployment-actionable for a specified interface and deployed utility. We introduce a fail-closed certification protocol whose gates are sufficient evidential conditions for a strong claim: a friction-caused, non-tie, statistically supported, and recurrent deployment-side reversal. Traffic-Hourly provides a certified anchor: winners agree at zero friction, but positive switching friction makes the forecast winner deployed-suboptimal. A locked native audit tests overclaiming: across 22 verified candidates and 362 full-grid cells, 155 apparent forecast/deployment winner inversions are blocked before certification. The contribution is not a new forecaster, metric, or universal utility, but a conservative protocol for deciding when forecasting leaderboard winners should be read as deployment-actionable top-1 advice.

</details>


### [211] [What's in an Earth Embedding? An Explainability Analysis of Location Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24997)
*Livia Betti,Sebastian Ricke,Ivica Obadic,Adam J. Stewart,Esther Rolf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了将地理隐式神经表示（INR）中的位置嵌入分解为三类可解释特征的方法：稀疏潜在概念（稀疏自编码器）、自然语言概念（SpLiCE）和视觉特征（CLIP Surgery显著性图），揭示了编码中的森林、沙漠、城市等地理解释性结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户缺乏原则性工具来审计地理INR的位置嵌入捕获了哪些地理或语义信息，现有方法无法对这些通用地理表示进行可解释性分析。

Method: 首先使用稀疏自编码器学习稀疏潜在概念嵌入；其次应用SpLiCE在预定义地理词典上恢复自然语言概念；最后使用CLIP Surgery导出的显著性图提取视觉特征。

Result: 位置嵌入可以被分解为人类可解释的表示同时保持高重建能力，揭示了从城市结构到生物群落和气候信号的系统性编码差异，显著性图进一步补充了道路和地标等互补特征。

Conclusion: 该工作为可解释的地理空间表示提供了初步步骤，使地理INR的编码信息变得透明和可审计。

Abstract: Geographic implicit neural representations (INRs) learn to map any coordinate on Earth to a location embedding, implicitly encoding geospatial data into the weights of a neural network. Location embeddings are widely used off the shelf as general-purpose geospatial representations, yet users lack principled tools to audit what geographic or semantic information these embeddings capture. In this work, we analyze the information content of geographic INRs through their location embeddings. We decompose these embeddings into human-interpretable features$\unicode{x2014}$namely, (i) sparse latent concepts, (ii) natural language concepts, and (iii) visual features. The latent concept embeddings are learned using sparse autoencoders. To recover natural language concepts, we apply sparse linear concept embeddings (SpLiCE) over a predefined geospatial dictionary. Finally, visual features are extracted using saliency maps derived from CLIP Surgery. We show that location embeddings can be decomposed into human-interpretable representations while retaining high reconstruction capability, revealing interpretable geographic structures such as forests, deserts, and urban features. Across methods, sparse decompositions expose systematic differences in encoded information, ranging from urban structures to broader biome and climate signals, and pretraining-space saliency maps further highlight complementary features such as roads and landmarks. We hope this work provides a first step toward interpretable geospatial representations.

</details>


### [212] [Internal Data Repetition Destroys Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24998)
*Jessica Chudnovsky,Joshua Kazdan,Noam Levi,Rylan Schaeffer,Yegor Denisov-Blanch,Bo He,Mehmet Donmez,Sanmi Koyejo,David Donoho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Chinchilla缩放定律框架下重新研究了训练数据重复对语言模型的影响，提出了"计算等价增益/损失"度量方法，发现重复数据损害呈现系统性规律：中等重复次数对模型伤害最大，且该峰值与模型规模呈幂律关系；在受控实验中，10% FLOPs的重复数据可导致相当于减少33%计算量的性能损失。通过线性回归理论模型解释了这一现象源于记忆与泛化的统计权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量训练数据日益稀缺，即便经过激进去重后的语料库仍存在重复内容。此前重复数据影响的研究依赖于Chinchilla缩放定律出现之前的控制实验，只能间接衡量重复带来的代价。本文旨在Chinchilla时代重新审视该问题，提供更精确的量化方法。

Method: 通过拟合无重复缩放定律，提出Compute-Equivalent Gain/Loss指标；在FineWeb-Edu-Dedup等数据集上进行受控精确文档重复实验，系统分析重复次数、重复数据占比和模型规模之间的关系；使用简单线性回归模型（含重复数据）进行理论分析。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1) 在计算量固定的条件下，中等重复次数导致最高的评估损失；(2) 最有害的重复次数与模型规模呈幂律关系，且增长速度快于计算量；(3) 10% FLOPs的重复数据可造成相当于损失33%计算量的性能下降。这些现象不仅限于语言模型，在线性回归模型中也能复现。

Conclusion: 重复数据的损害是系统性的，可以通过统计模型从记忆与泛化的权衡角度理解。本文为从业者提供了量化预训练语料中重复数据造成计算浪费的方法，有助于指导数据筛选和训练策略设计。

Abstract: Language models are running out of high-quality training data, and even aggressively deduplicated corpora retain some amount of repetition. Earlier controlled studies predated Chinchilla-style scaling laws and could only measure the cost of repetition indirectly. We revisit repetition in the Chinchilla era, using a fitted no-repetition scaling law to report Compute-Equivalent Gain and Compute-Equivalent Loss. We show that under this modernized paradigm, repetition damage is systematic in three ways. First, holding compute allocated to repeated data constant, eval loss peaks at an intermediate repeat count $\Rep$; repeating a moderately sized subset a moderate number of times damages performance more than repeating a large subset a few times or a small subset many times. Second, the location of this peak is well-fit by a power law in model size; this scaling law reveals that the most damaging number of repeated data grows more quickly than compute. Finally, when repeated documents consume 10\% of the FLOPs budget in a controlled exact-document repetition setting, the compute-equivalent loss can be large: on FineWeb-Edu-Dedup, the most damaging repeat count for a Qwen3-style 344M-parameter model at $\OT=1$ matches the loss of a no-repetition run using 67% of the FLOPs. We demonstrate that these phenomena are not language-model-specific, and can be analytically understood in a simple statistical model: a misspecified linear regression with verbatim duplicates reproduces the same qualitative loss peak, quantifying how such peaks can arise from a statistical tradeoff between memorization and generalization. Our findings add precision to the study of duplication in language models, allowing practitioners to quantify the wasted compute incurred by the presence and repeat structure of duplicates in pretraining corpora.

</details>


### [213] [A Zeroth-Order Deep Learning Method for Fully Nonlinear Parabolic Partial Differential Equations with Unknown Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24999)
*Yanwei Jia,Du Ouyang,Huyên Pham,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对高维偏微分方程（PDE）系数未知且仅能通过黑箱仿真获取数据的问题，提出了一种“先表示后学习”的框架。该方法利用基于扰动蒙特卡洛轨迹的零阶导数（ZOD）估计器，仅通过函数值来构建梯度和Hessian网络的训练目标，实现了完全无模型学习。论文提供了统计学学习分析，包括ZOD的偏差-方差权衡，并在算子满足压缩性质的假设下，建立了非渐近误差界，将总误差分解为离散化误差、逼近误差、统计误差和ZOD偏差，最终导出了在（加权）Sobolev空间中学习表示的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在高维PDE求解中依赖重复自动微分，导致不稳定性且放大导数误差；而基于随机表示的概性方法需要明确已知数据生成动力学，不适用于黑箱环境。因此，需要一种在PDE算子仅能通过仿真和点评估访问时，仍然能够有效学习解及其导数的方法。

Method: 提出两种类型的仿真器作为数据生成机制，采用“先表示后学习”（representing-then-learning）策略。核心创新是使用基于扰动蒙特卡洛轨迹的零阶导数（ZOD）估计器，仅通过函数值（zeroth-order evaluations）生成梯度和Hessian网络的训练目标，从而避免自动微分和对数据生成动力学的显式知识依赖。同时提供了统计学习分析，包括ZOD的偏差-方差权衡，以及基于算子压缩性质的非渐近误差分解。

Result: 建立了非渐近误差界，将总误差分解为离散化误差、逼近误差、统计误差和ZOD偏差四个部分；导出了在（加权）Sobolev空间中学习表示的样本复杂度，刻画了直至二阶导数的误差；数值实验表明该方法在中高维问题上具有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 该工作为高维且算子未知（仅能通过黑箱仿真访问）的PDE求解问题提供了一种完全无模型的可行方案，理论上的误差分解和样本复杂度分析为方法的可靠性提供了保证，数值实验验证了其在中高维场景下的有效性。

Abstract: High-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs) with unknown coefficients arise widely in scientific machine learning, including continuous-time reinforcement learning, yet solving them efficiently in a data-driven way remains challenging. Existing deep learning solvers often rely on repeated automatic differentiation to evaluate differential operators, which can cause instability and amplify derivative errors in high dimensions, while probabilistic methods based on stochastic representations require explicit knowledge of the data-generating dynamics and therefore do not apply to black-box environments. We introduce two types of simulators as data-generating mechanisms, and take a ``representing-then-learning" approach that learns the solutions and their derivatives under settings where the underlying PDE operators are accessible only through simulations and pointwise evaluations. Our representation of derivatives relies on the zeroth-order derivative (ZOD) estimators derived from perturbed Monte Carlo trajectories. This fully model-free approach generates targets for the gradient and Hessian networks using only function evaluations. We provide a statistical learning analysis of the proposed approach, including a bias--variance tradeoff for ZODs. Assuming a standard contraction property of the underlying operator, we establish a non-asymptotic error bound that decomposes the total error into discretization error, approximation error, statistical error, and ZOD bias. Crucially, we derive the sample complexity of the learned representations in (weighted) Sobolev space, characterizing the error up to second-order derivatives. Numerical experiments illustrate the competitive performance of the method in moderate and high dimensions.

</details>


### [214] [Geo-Strat-RL: Learning Geological Event Reasoning from Verifiable Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25000)
*Lukas Mosser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Geo-Strat-RL合成环境，通过带可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)训练视觉语言模型进行地质事件重建。实验表明RLVR能提升地层图解的地质内容评分，且学到的推理能力可迁移至合成地震数据领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地质历史推理不仅涉及视觉模式识别，还需理解时间和结构关系，且真实地质事件历史常不可唯一识别或获取，因此需要一种可验证的方法来训练模型生成符合观测证据和地质原理的重建结果。

Method: 构建Geo-Strat-RL合成环境，包含地层生成器和可执行验证器（对年代学、事件身份、沉积和结构关系进行评分），采用带可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)训练视觉语言模型。

Result: RLVR提升了VLM在保留地层图解上的地质内容评分；从地层图解领域学到的地质推理能力可迁移至合成地震数据领域（通过声阻抗振幅剖面转换），无需地震专用训练样本。

Conclusion: RLVR能够教会模型可复用的地质推理概念，并跨相关观测格式迁移，支持了RLVR可提升跨领域地质事件重建能力的假设。

Abstract: To evaluate whether vision-language models can reason about geological histories, it is necessary to construct observations for which the underlying process history is known. Furthermore, reasoning over geological histories is not just a question of recognizing visual patterns, but also of understanding temporal and structural relationships that may be only indirectly visible or highly ambiguous. When ground-truth event histories are not uniquely identifiable or are unavailable, it remains an open challenge to teach models capable of visual reasoning to produce valid geological reconstructions that are consistent with both observed evidence and geological principles. We therefore investigate whether defining a verifiable geological reasoning task can improve geological event reconstruction across observation domains through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). To this end, we present Geo-Strat-RL, a synthetic environment that generates stratigraphic observations and compact visible-evidence event histories. The environment combines a geological generator with an executable verifier that scores chronology, event identity, deposition, and structural relationships. We show that RLVR improves geological reconstruction in vision-language models (VLMs), increasing geological content scores on held out stratigraphic diagrams. We further evaluate the same held-out geological histories in a synthetic seismic observation domain by converting the generated scenes into acoustic-impedance-derived amplitude sections. In this controlled paired-renderer setting, we present evidence that geological reasoning learned from stratigraphic diagram-domain RLVR training transfers to synthetic seismic representations without seismic-specific training examples, supporting the hypothesis that RLVR can teach reusable geological reasoning concepts across related observation formats.

</details>


### [215] [Erased, but Not Gone: Output Forgetting Is Not True Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25001)
*Teresa Pui Yee Yong,Win Kent Ong,Chee Seng Chan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器学习卸载（MU）中输出级遗忘评估的局限性，提出以重训练模型作为参考标准来衡量表征空间中的遗忘一致性。理论分析和实验表明，当前的输出级评估系统性地高估了卸载成功度，实际仅达到“表面遗忘”而非“重训练一致遗忘”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MU评估主要依赖输出级遗忘（如低遗忘集准确率或logit级成员推理），但输出层面的成功可能掩盖表征空间中与重训练不一致的残留信息。作者质疑当前评估实际认证的是何种遗忘，并试图揭示输出级评估与真正遗忘之间的鸿沟。

Method: 以从零训练（不含遗忘数据）的重训练模型作为正确遗忘的操作性参考，定义并度量重训练一致的表征遗忘。通过理论分析和跨多种卸载方法、数据集、模型的实证实验，比较输出级评估与表征级重训练一致性之间的差异。

Result: 标准输出级评估系统性地高估卸载成功性。当前方法在输出层看似已遗忘，但在表征空间存在与重训练的结构性不匹配：遗忘样本部分对齐、保留样本更不一致、残留差异集中在重训练相关方向而非在表征空间中弥散。这种结构性不匹配表现为遗忘/保留不对称、方向性不匹配以及沿重训练相关方向的集中残留。

Conclusion: 当前MU评估通常只认证了表面遗忘而非重训练一致遗忘。重训练揭示了输出级遗忘所隐藏的问题，提示需要以表征级重训练一致性作为更严格的卸载评估标准。

Abstract: Machine unlearning (MU) is commonly judged by output forgetting, such as low forget-set accuracy or reduced logit-level membership inference. But if output-level success can coexist with retraining-inconsistent residuals in representation space, what kind of forgetting are current evaluations actually certifying? We study this question through retraining-consistent representation forgetting, using the retrained model (i.e., trained from scratch without the forget data) as an operational reference for correct forgetting. Across multiple unlearning methods, datasets, and models, our theoretical analysis and empirical results show that standard output-level evaluation can systematically overestimate the success of unlearning. Under this stronger lens, current methods often appear forgotten at the output layer while exhibiting a structured mismatch relative to retraining. They partially align with retraining on forget samples, remain more inconsistent on retain samples, and leave residual discrepancy concentrated along retraining-related directions rather than diffuse in representation space. This structured mismatch is characterized by forget/retain asymmetry, directional mismatch, and concentrated residuals along retraining-related directions. These results suggest that current MU is often evaluated for apparent forgetting rather than retraining-consistent forgetting. More broadly, retraining reveals what output forgetting hides.

</details>


### [216] [TRACER: Training-Free Closed-Loop Structured Inference for Traffic Accident Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25002)
*Yanchen Guan,Chengyue Wang,Bin Rao,Haicheng Liao,Jiaxun Zhang,Shang Gao,Chengzhong Xu,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACER是一种无需训练的交通事故事件重构框架，通过基于事件锚点的运动假设迭代优化，在几何、运动学和交互约束下实现物理一致的重建，在真实事故数据上优于数据驱动和物理基线的几何保真度、速度一致性和碰撞精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习方法优化语义合理性或视觉真实感，而非与可测量几何和动力学的定量一致性，且事故重建需要从稀疏异构证据中恢复物理一致的运动。

Method: 提出TRACER框架，将重建公式化为闭环结构化推理过程：构建并迭代优化事件锚定的运动假设，在几何、运动学和交互约束下进行，由结构化案例记忆和一致性驱动诊断引导。

Result: 在真实事故数据上，TRACER在几何保真度、速度一致性和碰撞精度方面均优于数据驱动和基于物理的基线方法。

Conclusion: TRACER实现了增量式、可解释的修正，使事故重建过程更符合人类专家的工作流程。

Abstract: Traffic accident reconstruction is a forensic inverse problem that requires recovering physically consistent motion from sparse and heterogeneous evidence. Existing learning-based approaches predominantly optimize for semantic plausibility or visual realism, rather than quantitative agreement with measurable geometry and dynamics. Here, we present TRACER, a training-free framework that formulates reconstruction as a closed-loop structured inference process. Instead of directly generating dense trajectories, our framework constructs and iteratively refines event-anchored motion hypotheses under geometric, kinematic, and interaction constraints, guided by structured case memory and consistency-driven diagnosis. This design enables incremental, interpretable corrections when evidence is insufficient, making the accident reconstruction process more aligned with the workflow of human experts. Experiments on real-world accident data show that TRACER achieves improved geometric fidelity, velocity consistency, and collision accuracy over both data-driven and physics-based baselines.

</details>


### [217] [Adaptive Joint Compression and Synchronisation in Federated Split Learning for IoT Rainfall Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25003)
*Wenjie Ding,Yi Sin Lin,Jiale Liu,Baoyi Liu,Guanghua Liu,Zhuolu Li,Suleiman Sabo,Chuadhry Mujeeb Ahmed,Aydin Abadi,Rehmat Ullah,Rajiv Ranjan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合优化激活压缩与同步频率的联邦拆分学习框架，用于IoT降雨预测。通过服务器端延迟驱动调度器和EMA平滑，在模拟和树莓派实际部署中，采用int8量化和rho=3的配置相比float32基线减少了87%的上传负载和54%的同步流量，同时将运行时抖动从±688秒降至±10秒，而AUPRC几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦拆分学习（FSL）在带宽受限的IoT设备上存在通信瓶颈，现有工作孤立地优化激活压缩或同步频率，缺乏联合优化方案。

Method: 提出一个FSL框架，通过服务器端的延迟驱动调度器联合优化激活压缩和同步间隔ρ，并对每个客户端使用EMA平滑；在11个气象站的ERA5数据上进行评估，包括17场景模拟矩阵和4场景树莓派实际广域网部署。

Result: 模拟中AUPRC仅在0.6381-0.6484范围内波动；树莓派上AUPRC波动在0.011以内。最优配置（int8, ρ=3）实现激活上传负载降低87%，同步流量减少54%，运行时抖动从±688秒降至±10秒。

Conclusion: 在该降雨预测场景中，激进的量化与稀疏聚合不会显著降低预测质量，同时能大幅减少通信开销和运行时抖动，验证了联合优化策略的有效性。

Abstract: Federated split learning (FSL) enables collaborative training across bandwidth-constrained IoT devices, but repeated activation and gradient exchange creates a communication bot-tleneck. Prior work optimises either activation compression or synchronisation frequency in isolation. This paper presents an FSL framework for IoT rainfall prediction that jointly regulates activation compression and the synchronisation interval \r{ho} via a latency driven scheduler on a server with per client EMA smoothing. The system is evaluated on hourly ERA5 data from 11 weather stations through a 17 scenario simulation matrix and a four scenario Raspberry Pi deployment over a real wide-area link. The simulation matrix validates scheduler switching across low, high, and mixed latency profiles, while the Pi deployment validates the high latency endpoint selected by the same policy. AUPRC varies only slightly across configurations (0.6381-0.6484 in simulation; within 0.011 on Pi), indicating that aggressive quantisation and sparser aggregation do not materially degrade predictive quality in this setting. On Pi, the selected endpoint (int8 with rho=3) achieves an 87% reduction in activation upload payload and a 54% reduction in synchronisation traffic relative to the float32 baseline, while reducing runtime jitter from +/-688 s to +/-10 s.

</details>


### [218] [Scalable Peptide Design via Memory-Efficient Equivariant Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25006)
*Rui Jiao,Xiangzhe Kong,Yinjun Jia,Yijia Zhang,Ziyi Yang,Yang Liu,Jianzhu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MEET是一个E(3)等变骨干网络，通过内存高效注意力机制实现线性内存扩展，集成到VAE和潜扩散管道中，在全原子肽生成任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜生成框架在肽设计中通过将细粒度原子结构压缩为块级潜表示进行条件生成，但其可扩展性严重依赖于编码、解码和去噪组件中使用的几何骨干网络。现有方法存在内存瓶颈，难以支持大规模原子级肽建模。

Method: 提出MEET（Memory Efficient Equivariant Transformer），核心设计包括：(1) 耦合不变标量和等变向量特征流；(2) 基于内存高效注意力重新设计几何计算；(3) 通过全局坐标聚合初始化向量特征；(4) 通过增强的查询-键点积融入成对距离；(5) 通过稀疏键自适应注入共价键信息。集成到VAE和潜扩散管道中实现全原子肽生成。

Result: MEET实现了随原子数线性增长的内存缩放，改进了生成质量。在AFDB大规模数据集上的实验表明，该骨干网络支持系统和数据的系统性缩放，在结合亲和力、物理有效性和样本多样性方面均优于现有肽设计方法。

Conclusion: MEET通过内存高效的等变Transformer设计，解决了潜生成框架中几何骨干网络的可扩展性瓶颈，为大规模原子级肽设计提供了高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Target-specific peptide design requires sequence and structure co-design under full atom geometric constraints. Latent generative frameworks offer an effective route for this problem by compressing fine grained atomic structures into block level latent representations and performing conditional generation in a compact latent space. However, the scalability of such systems depends heavily on the geometric backbone used throughout their encoding, decoding, and denoising components. We introduce MEET (Memory Efficient Equivariant Transformer), an E(3) equivariant backbone for scalable atomistic peptide modeling. MEET maintains coupled invariant scalar and equivariant vector feature streams, while reformulating geometric computation around memory efficient attention. It initializes vector features through global coordinate aggregation, incorporates pairwise distances through augmented query and key dot products, and injects covalent bond information through sparse bond adaptation. Integrated into a VAE and latent diffusion pipeline for full atom peptide generation, \model{} achieves linear memory scaling with atom count and improves generation quality over existing peptide design methods. Experiments on large scale AFDB derived datasets further show that the proposed backbone supports systematic model and data scaling, leading to better binding affinity, physical validity, and sample diversity.

</details>


### [219] [Multi-Stream Temporal Fusion for Financial Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25007)
*Mohammadamin Dashti Moghaddam,Nick Sciarrilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多流欺诈检测Transformer（MSFT），统一编码多个异构事件流（交易、登录、风险信号），通过五种融合策略的系统性消融实验，在大规模数据集上验证了多流架构相比单流和传统方法的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字银行中的金融欺诈检测需要对多个异构事件流进行推理，单个流看似正常但联合起来可揭示欺诈模式，现有方法缺乏对多流时序信息的统一建模能力。

Method: 提出MSFT架构，每个事件流使用独立Transformer编码器，通过五种融合策略（拼接、门控融合、时序感知位置编码、跨流注意力、全组合）进行表征融合，在1000万用户、85M参数模型规模上进行系统消融。

Result: (1)序列模型显著优于梯度提升树（0.74 vs 0.99 AUROC）；(2)多流编码必要，单流仅0.82 AUROC（18点差距）；(3)时序感知位置编码最优（0.9961 AUROC）；(4)门控融合精度最高（0.989），适合生产部署；(5)风险事件流贡献最大；在私有生产数据上AUROC相对提升22%。

Conclusion: 多流Transformer架构对异构事件流的联合建模是数字银行欺诈检测的有效范式，不同融合策略可根据部署需求（最大化判别 vs. 精确率优先）灵活选择。

Abstract: Financial fraud detection in digital banking requires reasoning over multiple heterogeneous event streams -- transactions, login sessions, risk signals -- that individually appear benign but collectively reveal fraudulent patterns. We propose the Multi-Stream Fraud Transformer (MSFT), a unified architecture that encodes each event stream with independent Transformer encoders and fuses their representations through configurable mechanisms. We conduct a systematic ablation study comparing five fusion strategies: concatenation, gated fusion, time-aware positional encoding, cross-stream attention, and a full combination. On a large-scale dataset (10M users, 1.5% fraud rate) with 85M parameter models, we demonstrate that (1) sequence models significantly outperform gradient-boosted trees operating on aggregated features (0.74 vs. 0.99 AUROC), (2) per-stream encoding is essential -- a single-stream Transformer baseline with matched parameter budget reaches only 0.82 AUROC, an 18-point gap that confirms the multi-stream inductive bias is necessary, (3) time-aware positional encoding achieves the highest discrimination (0.9961 AUROC), (4) gated fusion yields the best precision (0.989) suitable for production deployment, and (5) the risk event stream provides the strongest individual signal contribution. We further validate on proprietary production data from a digital banking platform, showing over 22% relative AUROC improvement over the XGBoost baseline.

</details>


### [220] [Neural Scaling Universality: If Exponents Are Fixed, Time to Understand Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25008)
*Yizhou Liu,Jeff Gore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证神经缩放定律中的幂律指数由通用机制（Softmax非线性带来的1/3时间缩放、表征叠加带来的逆宽度缩放、Transformer层集成平均带来的逆深度缩放）固定，而系数则对数据和架构细节敏感，决定了最优模型形状和计算最优前沿，因此理解系数是近期性能提升的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解释神经缩放定律中指数固定的根本原因，并指出当前研究应关注系数而非指数，以推动实际性能提升。

Method: 通过理论论证提出三个通用机制：Softmax强非线性导致1/3时间缩放指数、表征叠加导致逆宽度缩放、Transformer层集成平均导致逆深度缩放，形成固定指数的普适类。

Result: 发现缩放指数由通用机制固定，对数据和架构细节鲁棒；系数则敏感依赖于数据和架构细节，直接决定最优模型形状和计算最优前沿。

Conclusion: 理解系数是近期性能改进的关键，且当前普适类的深入分析可能导向更优的普适类。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws describe how pre-training loss decays as power laws with training time, model size, and compute. This position paper argues that the exponents of these power laws are fixed by generic mechanisms: a one-third time scaling due to the strong nonlinearity of Softmax, an inverse width scaling due to representational superposition, and an inverse depth scaling due to ensemble averaging of Transformer layers. These mechanisms are robust to a wide range of data structures and architectural details, placing current large language models in a universality class with fixed exponents. The coefficients, however, are expected to be sensitive to data and architecture details, and directly determine practical quantities such as the optimal model shape and the compute-optimal frontier. We therefore argue that understanding the coefficients is the key to near-term performance improvements, and that a closer examination of the current universality class may reveal pathways to better universality classes.

</details>


### [221] [Emergent Capabilities Arise Randomly from Learning Sparse Attention Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25010)
*Vatsal Baherwani,Zixi Chen,Shikai Qiu,Andrew Gordon Wilson,Pavel Izmailov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了Transformer语言模型中涌现能力（如上下文学习）并非仅在特定模型规模下突然出现，而是在训练过程中随机涌现，较大模型平均更早获得。通过合成数据集实验，发现涌现能力与任务相关注意力模式的突然学习对应，且注意力模式的学习难度取决于上下文长度和稀疏性。缩放注意力头数可提升学习效率，而增加头维度超过最小容量后收益递减。MLP-Mixer在复杂注意力模式任务上优于Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer语言模型中涌现能力（特别是上下文学习）出现的机制，解释为何下游能力会在特定模型规模下突然出现，而非随参数增长平滑提升。

Method: 在合成线性映射和元胞自动机数据集上训练Transformer模型，分析注意力模式的学习动态；对比不同注意力头数量、头维度的影响；比较MLP-Mixer与Transformer在复杂注意力模式任务上的表现。

Result: 涌现能力在训练过程中随机出现，较大模型平均更早获得；能力涌现与任务相关注意力模式的突然学习对应；注意力模式学习难度受上下文长度和稀疏性影响；缩放注意力头数提升效率，增加头维度超过最小容量后收益递减；MLP-Mixer在复杂注意力模式任务上优于Transformer。

Conclusion: 下游能力的涌现源于Transformer模型中学习稀疏注意力模式的内在难度——当模型能够有效学习到任务相关的注意力模式时，能力便突然出现。这为理解神经语言模型中的涌现现象提供了机制层面的解释。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws for transformer language models predict smooth improvements in pretraining loss with increasing parameters, but downstream capabilities such as in-context learning are known to emerge abruptly past a certain model scale. In this paper, we show that emergent capabilities arise stochastically throughout training, with larger models acquiring them earlier on average. We demonstrate that the emergence of capabilities such as pattern completion and indirect object identification corresponds to the abrupt learning of task-relevant attention patterns. To isolate this phenomenon, we train transformer models on synthetic linear map and cellular automata datasets, and we show that the difficulty of learning attention patterns depends on context length and pattern sparsity. Moreover, scaling the number of attention heads improves learning efficiency on our synthetic tasks, while increasing the head dimension yields diminishing returns past a minimum capacity. We additionally investigate architectures with alternative attention mechanisms, showing that MLP-Mixer outperforms a transformer on linear map tasks with complex attention patterns. Our findings provide a mechanistic insight into emergence, showing that downstream capabilities arise abruptly due to the intrinsic difficulty of learning sparse attention patterns in transformer models.

</details>


### [222] [Bias-Controlled Primal-Dual Natural Actor-Critic: Optimal Rates for Constrained Multi-Objective Average-Reward RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25012)
*Ankur Naskar,Swetha Ganesh,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MLMC的原-对偶自然演员-评论家算法，用于无限时域平均奖励设置下的多目标约束强化学习，在无需混合时间知识的情况下实现了$\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$的最优全局收敛率和约束违反率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均奖励设置下的多目标约束强化学习问题中，凹标量化方法在策略梯度和演员-评论家方法中引入偏差（因梯度需用$J^\pi$的噪声估计求值，且$\mathbb{E}[\partial f(J^\pi)] \neq \partial f(\mathbb{E}[J^\pi])$），该偏差在原始和对偶更新中传播，现有方法缺乏对偏差的理论控制和最优收敛保证。

Method: 提出基于多层蒙特卡洛（MLMC）的原-对偶自然演员-评论家算法，在标量化目标、约束评估和演员-评论家估计中控制偏差，无需知晓混合时间信息。

Result: 算法达到了$\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$的最优全局收敛率和约束违反率。

Conclusion: 这是首个在平均奖励设置下为凹标量化多目标强化学习（含约束和无约束情形）建立最优收敛结果的工作，且即使在无标量化情况下也无需混合时间信息。

Abstract: Many reinforcement learning (RL) problems in the infinite-horizon average-reward setting require optimizing multiple conflicting objectives while satisfying multiple safety constraints. A common approach is concave scalarization, where the agent maximizes a utility $ f(J^π_{r_1}, \ldots, J^π_{r_M}) $ subject to a scalarized constraint $ g(J^π_{c_1}, \ldots, J^π_{c_N}) \ge 0 $, where $J^π_{r_m}$ and $J^π_{c_n}$ denote the average-reward and cost under policy $π$. However, the nonlinearity of $f$ and $g$ introduces bias in policy-gradient and actor-critic methods, since gradients must be evaluated using noisy estimates of $J^π,$ and $ \mathbb{E}[\partial f(J^π)] \neq \partial f(\mathbb{E}[J^π]),$ and this bias propagates through both primal and dual updates. We propose an MLMC-based primal-dual Natural Actor-Critic algorithm for average-reward MDPs that controls bias in scalarized objectives, constraint evaluation, and actor-critic estimation without requiring mixing-time knowledge. We show that the algorithm achieves optimal global convergence and constraint-violation rates of $ \tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{T}) $. To our knowledge, this is the first result establishing optimal convergence for concave scalarized multi-objective RL in the average-reward setting, both with and without constraints, and the first to do so without mixing-time information even in the absence of scalarization.

</details>


### [223] [Do Thinking Tokens Help with Safety?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25013)
*Narutatsu Ri,Abhishek Panigrahi,Sanjeev Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 推理模型的"思考"过程并非真正的审慎决策，其拒绝/遵从结果在第一个token的隐藏表示中已高度可预测（AUROC 0.84-0.95），思考过程更像前缀补全而非审慎修正，且现有安全干预反而抑制了本就稀缺的审慎信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑"推理模型通过思考token进行更审慎的决策从而提升对齐与安全性"这一普遍直觉，验证当前推理模型的安全行为是否真的具有审慎性。

Method: 在GPT-OSS、Qwen、Olmo、Phi等多个前沿开放权重推理模型上，通过第一个token的隐藏表示训练分类头预测拒绝/遵从结果；分析思考过程中结果转变的时间点与频率；评估现有安全干预对审慎信号的影响。

Result: 1) 首个token即可高精度预测最终结果（AUROC 0.84-0.95, 平衡准确率~88%）；2) 思考过程约在前20%阶段后结果几乎不再变化，74%的文本级审慎发生在响应分布已锁定状态；3) 现有安全干预（推理时和训练时）主要导致过度拒绝，抑制了本就稀少的审慎信号。

Conclusion: 当前推理模型的安全行为远不如通常假设的那样具有审慎性，需要开发能真正引发安全审慎决策的新方法。

Abstract: Today's reasoning models use thinking tokens to attain stronger performance on benchmarks than their instruction-tuned counterparts. It is also generally believed that this more "deliberative" mode should improve alignment and safety, by providing the model a safe space to consider whether its planned answer to a request violates its safety principles. We present evidence that this intuition is not always correct. Across frontier open-weight reasoning models spanning GPT-OSS, Qwen, Olmo, and Phi families, we find that the eventual refusal/compliance outcome is already strongly predictable via a trained head on the first token's hidden representation ($0.84$-$0.95$ AUROC and $\sim88\%$ balanced accuracy for predicting refusal/compliance) before any visible thinking. The thinking process turns out to be more akin to prefix completion than to deliberative revision, with the final outcome rarely changing after the first $\sim20\%$ of thinking, despite giving the appearance of deliberation at the text level ($\sim74\%$ of text-level deliberations occur when the response distribution is already locked to one refusal/compliance side). We also find that existing inference-time and training-based safety interventions, despite being motivated by the goal of inducing deliberation, largely shift model behavior toward over-refusal while suppressing already-scarce deliberation signals. Our results suggest that safety behavior in current reasoning models is much less deliberative than commonly assumed, and highlight the need for methods that induce real safety deliberation.

</details>


### [224] [LLM-ACES: Closed-Loop Discovery of Dynamical Systems with LLM-Guided Adaptive Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25039)
*Nikhil Abhyankar,Sha Li,Sanchit Kabra,Naren Ramakrishnan,Yulia Gel,Chandan K. Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-ACES框架，利用大语言模型引导的主动闭环方程搜索，通过联合优化符号假设构建和自适应数据采集，从数据中恢复常微分方程。在122个ODEs系统上，LLM-ACES取得了最低的中位NMSE，符号准确率分别达到46.2%和52.4%，且样本效率更高，对噪声更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将ODE发现视为静态推理问题，假设观测轨迹信息量充足。然而动力系统状态空间庞大，有限数据下多个方程在观测上难以区分，导致可辨识性差距和错误的方程恢复。

Method: LLM-ACES是一个闭环框架，包含两个核心环节：(1) LLM提出算子先验，将庞大的搜索空间划分为不同区域；(2) 在各区域中将候选方程拟合到观测数据，候选方程之间的分歧指导获取信息量最大的轨迹，形成迭代反馈循环，逐步优化假设空间和发现的动态方程。

Result: 在ODEBench和ODEBase的122个ODE系统上，LLM-ACES取得最低中位NMSE，性能优于最先进基线数个数量级，符号准确率分别达46.2%和52.4%。仅需十分之一数据即可达到更好性能，且对噪声鲁棒，能恢复正确符号结构。

Conclusion: LLM-ACES通过主动数据采集与符号发现联合优化的闭环策略，有效解决了数据有限时ODE可辨识性不足的问题，在准确性、样本效率和鲁棒性方面均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Recovering governing Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) from data is a central challenge in modeling dynamical systems across scientific domains. Existing approaches cast discovery as a static inference problem over fixed datasets, assuming that the observed trajectories are sufficiently informative. However, dynamical systems evolve over large state spaces, and limited data can make multiple equations observationally indistinguishable, leading to identifiability gaps and the recovery of incorrect governing equations. To address this, we introduce LLM-ACES, or LLM-guided Active Closed-loop Equation Search, a closed-loop framework that jointly optimizes symbolic hypothesis construction and adaptive data acquisition. In LLM-ACES, a large language model (LLM) proposes operator priors that partition the large search space into distinct regions, within which candidate equations are fit to the observed data. The disagreement among these candidates guides the acquisition of informative trajectories, creating a feedback loop that iteratively refines both the hypothesis space and the discovered dynamics. On 122 ODE systems spanning ODEBench and ODEBase, LLM-ACES achieves the lowest median NMSE, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines by several orders of magnitude while achieving a high symbolic accuracy of 46.2% and 52.4%, respectively. Our analysis further shows that LLM-ACES is sample-efficient, achieving better performance with one-tenth the data. Furthermore, LLM-ACES's feedback-driven data acquisition makes it robust to noise and recovers the correct symbolic structure, while baselines introduce spurious terms that fit the data locally but obscure the true governing relationships.

</details>


### [225] [Adapt Only When It Pays: Budgeted Decision-Loss Priority for Delayed Online Time-Series Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25068)
*Xibai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ADOWIP框架，通过决策损失优先级门控机制，在有限计算预算下选择性更新在线时序预测器，仅在收到反馈且下游损失超过校准分位数时更新，在多项任务上优于固定周期和漂移触发基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线时序预测器在每次适应步骤中计算资源有限，且标签存在依视野延迟。现有工作关注"如何适应"而非"何时更新"，缺乏在有限计算预算下做出更新决策的机制。

Method: 提出ADOWIP残差适配器框架，包含密封延迟队列、精确预算核算和可审计更新遥测。核心调度器为观察到的决策损失优先级门控，仅在反馈揭示后、下游损失（可选加MSE惩罚）超过校准经验分位数且预算充足时更新。

Result: 在ETT容量规划任务上，冻结校准/评估分割选择的门控在匹配计算下降低了留出决策损失。次要实验中41个选定对比中33个通过了Holm族检验。在UCI Bike代理任务中实现20/0留出胜场，Capital Bikeshare中固定门控通过三个全年对比。探针和金融实验结果为阴性。

Conclusion: 提出了决策优先级适应方法，在有限计算预算下通过选择性更新提升预测性能，同时划定了当前方法的适用范围。

Abstract: Online time-series forecasters receive labels only after horizon-dependent delays, while every adaptation step spends limited compute. We study when an online learner should update, not how to adapt at every opportunity, and introduce ADOWIP: a residual-adapter framework with sealed delay queues, exact budget accounting, and auditable update telemetry. Its main scheduler is an observed decision-loss priority gate that updates only after feedback is revealed, when downstream loss, optionally penalized by prediction MSE, exceeds a calibrated empirical quantile and budget remains. We prove hard-budget feasibility, projected-OGD regret for a convex linear accepted-update subproblem, and stability plus conditional finite-sample gate-selection statements. On public ETT capacity-planning tasks, a frozen calibration/evaluation split selects a gate that lowers held-out decision loss against always, fixed-period, and drift-triggered exact-update baselines under matched compute. Secondary threshold/load-index ETT suites are mixed: 33 of 41 selected contrasts clear the stricter cross-artifact Holm family, and the 8 nonpassing rows are explicitly excluded from primary claims. The same protocol improves an external UCI Bike capacity proxy with 20/0 held-out wins, and a fixed gate passes three full-year Capital Bikeshare station-rebalancing contrasts. Probe-based and finance experiments remain negative, delimiting the current scope of decision-prioritized adaptation.

</details>


### [226] [GCT-MARL: Graph-Based Contrastive Transfer for Sample-Efficient Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25073)
*Animesh Animesh,Satheesh K Perepu,Kaushik Dey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GCT-MARL，一种基于多视图图对比学习骨干网络的迁移学习框架，通过逐视图自适应加权对齐损失和两阶段训练协议，实现跨不同规模与组成种群的多智能体强化学习任务迁移，显著加速目标任务收敛，并支持持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从部署角度看，在每个新环境或任务中从头训练智能体既具挑战性又成本高昂，亟需有效的迁移学习方法。

Method: 基于MAIL的多视图图对比学习骨干，引入逐视图自适应加权对齐损失，设计两阶段训练协议，支持同构（同阵营变规模N）和异构（跨阵营及混合单元类型）迁移场景。

Result: 在同类（同阵营变规模）和异类（跨阵营及混合单元类型）迁移场景中，相比从头训练显著加速目标任务收敛；支持通过顺序链接两阶段协议实现持续学习。

Conclusion: 本文为缓解当前MARL迁移方法的关键局限提供了统一方案，在方法论和实证层面均贡献了新见解。

Abstract: In cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), from a deployment perspective, it is challenging and expensive to train agents from scratch for each new environment or task. In this work, we propose GCT-MARL, a transfer learning framework that builds on the multi-view graph contrastive backbone of MAIL and augments it with a per-view, adaptively weighted alignment loss and a two-phase training protocol specifically designed for transfer across populations of varying sizes and compositions. We empirically demonstrate that the proposed framework markedly accelerates convergence on the target task relative to from-scratch training, in both homogeneous (within-faction, varying N) and heterogeneous (cross-faction and mixed unit-type) transfer scenarios. Furthermore, we show that the framework naturally supports continual learning by sequentially chaining the two-phase transfer protocol across a series of related tasks. Overall, this work provides a unified approach to mitigating key limitations in current MARL transfer methods with new insights at both methodological and empirical levels.

</details>


### [227] [How Modular Is a Frontier Mixture-of-Experts? A Pre-registered Causal Test in Which Apparent Expert Modularity Mostly Dissolves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25092)
*Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi,Ali Asaria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Command A+（218B参数，128专家，MoE架构）进行了因果性模块化分析。通过预先注册六个家族-轴假设，在推理时对每个专家家族进行消融实验，并使用多种指标和独立语料库验证。结果发现：仅有1/6的预注册家族（阿拉伯语家族）表现出干净的、选择性的功能模块化。其他家族虽有因果效应但缺乏选择性，且其模块化结果高度依赖于测量方式（语料库、指标和统计标准）。正向对照实验（在Qwen3-30B-A3B上复现其已知的分离结构）证实了方法的有效性。结论：消融实验得出的模块化结论不可靠，除非语料库、指标和统计标准都被严格控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证稀疏MoE模型中专家是否形成与特定能力或语言绑定的功能模块，探究专家路由模式与功能模块化的因果关系。

Method: 构建路由质量图谱（routing-mass atlas）；预先注册六个家族-轴假设；在推理时对每个专家家族进行消融实验，以大小匹配的随机专家消融作为零假设对照；使用四种指标和独立语料库进行验证，采用bootstrap置信区间进行统计推断。

Result: 仅1/6的预注册家族（阿拉伯语）通过所有选择性检验（包括独立语料库和保守统计标准）。更宽松的阈值下通过3/6，但对阈值敏感。其余家族虽有因果效应但缺乏选择性，且结果随语料库、指标和统计标准的变化而翻转。

Conclusion: 基于消融实验的模块化结论不可靠，其有效性高度依赖于语料库、指标和统计标准的选择。研究者需严格控制这些变量后才能得出可信的模块化判断。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models route each token to a few of many experts, inviting the hypothesis that experts form functional modules tied to capabilities or languages. We test this causally on Command A+, a frontier open-weights MoE (218B total / 25B active; 128 experts, 8 active, +1 shared). We build a routing-mass atlas, pre-register six family-to-axis hypotheses before any intervention, and ablate each family at inference time against a size-matched random-expert null, measuring whether it selectively breaks its own axis (worst off-target effect at most one third of on-target). Crucially, we test the same families under four metrics and a held-out, independent-corpus run with bootstrap confidence intervals. Our finding is cautionary: robust functional modularity is rare and measurement-dependent. Of six pre-registered families, only one, the Arabic-language family, is a clean selective module that survives an independent corpus and a conservative statistical bar (1/6; a more permissive pre-registered point rule admits 3/6, but that count is threshold-sensitive). Every other family has a real causal effect yet fails selectivity, and its apparent modularity flips with the measurement: with the corpus, the metric, and the statistical bar. A positive control on Qwen3-30B-A3B recovers its published disjoint structure, confirming the method detects modularity when present. The verdict reproduces on the un-quantized BF16 model, ruling out a 4-bit quantization artifact. We conclude that ablation-based modularity verdicts are not safe unless the corpus, metric, and statistical bar are controlled. We release the atlas and ablation data.

</details>


### [228] [A Framework for Directed Hypergraph Signal Processing via tensor t-SVD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25112)
*Carlos Mundo-Levano,Nicolás Bello,Daniel L. Lau,Gonzalo R. Arce*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有向超图谱信号处理（DHGSP）框架，利用t-SVD和张量t-积代数，统一处理高阶和不对称关系，在交通网络去噪任务中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图信号处理方法无法同时处理高阶关系（超图）和方向性（有向图），亟需一个统一框架来建模兼具这两种特性的复杂系统。

Method: 基于张量奇异值分解（t-SVD）和t-积代数，定义有向超图的邻接张量、拓扑保真移位算子及有向超图傅里叶变换（t-DHGFT）。

Result: 在真实交通网络去噪任务中，DHGSP优于基于矩阵的图/有向图方法和基于无向张量的超图方法。

Conclusion: DHGSP有效融合高阶性与方向性，为有向超图信号处理提供了统一且性能更优的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce Directed Hypergraph Signal Processing (DHGSP), a unified framework that extends graph signal processing to accommodate both higher-order (polyadic) and asymmetric (directional) relationships simultaneously. Using the tensor singular value decomposition (t-SVD) within the t-product algebra, we define a novel adjacency tensor for directed hypergraphs, a topologically faithful shift operator, and a lossless Directed Hypergraph Fourier Transform (t-DHGFT). Experiments on real traffic networks demonstrate that DHGSP outperforms matrix-based (graph and digraph) and undirected tensor-based (hypergraph) baselines in denoising tasks.

</details>


### [229] [Forget to Improve: On-Device LLM-Agent Continual Learning via Budget-Curated Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25115)
*Beining Wu,Zihao Ding,Jun Huang,Yanxiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NetScore，一个净价值每字节评分系统，用于管理设备端语言模型代理的经验记忆生命周期，通过统一的评分标准决定记忆的保留、共享和信任，在减少内存、上行链路和注入攻击成功率的同时保持准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统各自只覆盖问题的一部分：代理记忆无预算增长、设备端方法仅基于成功保留条目、投毒攻击主要被视为攻击而非记忆治理问题。缺乏一个统一的框架来管理设备端语言模型代理的硬边界暴露记忆的整个生命周期。

Method: 提出NetScore——一个净价值每字节评分系统，将预算作为策展人：每个条目以价值减去危害每字节评分，单一规则决定保留、共享和信任。包含三个决策：(1) KEEP：在RAM和能量预算下驱逐低价值字节；(2) SHARE：仅在洞见价值超过上行链路成本时发送；(3) TRUST：基于来源门控对等条目。

Result: 在语言模型代理任务漂移基准和包含两个机械臂节点与一个中心的真实异构Jetson测试台上，NetScore将记忆减少2.7倍，上行链路减少2.4倍，注入成功从0.75降至零，提高了被毒化或陈旧记忆损坏案例的准确性。

Conclusion: 通过净价值策展可以减少占用空间、能量、上行链路和注入成功率，同时不降低准确性。在这种设置下，基于净价值的遗忘能增强代理而非削弱它。

Abstract: On-device language-model agents improve by accumulating experience in retrieved memory rather than by updating weights. This memory is hard-bounded and exposed: it consumes RAM and energy, reaches peers through a thin uplink, and becomes an attack surface because it is writable by what the agent reads. Existing systems each cover one part of this problem: agentic memories grow without a budget, on-device methods keep entries by success alone, and poisoning is studied mainly as an attack rather than as a memory-governance problem. We propose \sys{}, a single net-value-per-byte score that governs an agent's experience-memory lifecycle. The main idea is to let the budget act as the curator: each entry is scored as value minus harm, per byte, so one ruler decides what to keep, share, and trust. \sys{} makes three decisions: (1) \textbf{KEEP} evicts low-value bytes under the RAM and energy budget; (2) \textbf{SHARE} sends an insight only when its value exceeds its uplink cost; and (3) \textbf{TRUST} gates a peer entry by provenance. On language-model-agent task-drift benchmarks and a real heterogeneous Jetson testbed with two robot-arm nodes and a hub, \sys{} reduces memory by $2.7\times$ and uplink by $2.4\times$, drives injection success from 0.75 to zero, and raises accuracy on cases corrupted by poison or stale memory. Curating by net value reduces footprint, energy, uplink, and injection success together without reducing accuracy. In this setting, forgetting by net value improves the agent rather than weakening it.

</details>


### [230] [An iterative energy-based multimodal transformer for joint retrieval of wheat soil moisture, leaf area index, and plant height from Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25174)
*Shubham Kumar Singh,Peilei Fan,Suraj A. Yadav,Rajendra Prasad,Prashant K Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代能量基Transformer（iEBT）模型，联合Sentinel-1 C波段SAR和Sentinel-2多光谱时间序列数据，通过迭代更新最小化学习能量函数，实现了土壤水分、叶面积指数和植株高度的协同反演，在印度实地数据上取得优异性能，并利用能量函数作为后验质量诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 田间尺度下土壤水分、叶面积指数和植株高度的联合反演是精准农业的关键，但传统前馈回归模型难以应对雷达后向散射与光谱响应中的土壤-冠层状态耦合歧义问题，尤其在异质性小农耕作系统中效果不佳。

Method: iEBT将多模态预测器嵌入共享序列，生成初始状态估计，然后通过归一化梯度下降迭代更新目标[SM, LAI, PH]向量，最小化学习到的标量兼容性能量函数，实现联合反演。

Result: 在印度瓦拉纳西700个质控实地测量数据上，iEBT在随机测试集上四种子平均R²达0.854±0.012（SM: 0.841, LAI: 0.905, PH: 0.821）。模态消融实验证实Sentinel-1主导SM反演，Sentinel-2主导LAI，PH依赖两者联合。筛选10%最高能量样本可显著降低RMSE。

Conclusion: iEBT提供了一种结构化自诊断路径以实现可靠的生物物理参数估计，但留一验证实验表明跨季节域偏移仍因局部管理差异而存在挑战。

Abstract: Field-scale retrieval of surface soil moisture (SM), leaf area index (LAI), and plant height (PH) is essential for precision agriculture, yet it remains an ill-posed inverse problem. Concurrent variations in soil moisture and canopy density generate substantial ambiguities in radar backscatter and spectral responses, which reduces the effectiveness of traditional feedforward regression models in heterogeneous smallholder cropping systems. This study presents the Iterative Energy-Based Transformer (iEBT) for the joint retrieval of coupled soil-canopy states from Sentinel-1 C-band SAR and Sentinel-2 multispectral time series. Instead of direct regression, iEBT embeds multi-modal predictors within a shared sequence, produces an initial state estimate, and iteratively updates the target [SM, LAI, PH] vector through normalized gradient descent to minimize a learned scalar compatibility energy function. Using 700 quality-controlled field measurements from Varanasi, India, iEBT achieved the highest learned-model performance on the random test split, with a four-seed mean R^2 of 0.854 \pm 0.012 (R_SM^2 = 0.841, R_LAI^2 = 0.905, R_PH^2 = 0.821). WCM and PROSAIL were retained as physically interpretable SAR and optical reference models for comparison. Modality ablations confirmed that Sentinel-1 drives SM retrieval, while Sentinel-2 dominates LAI, whereas PH relies on combined structural-phenological signatures. Crucially, the model's terminal energy functions as an uncalibrated post-retrieval quality diagnostic; screening the 10% highest-energy samples markedly reduced target level root-mean-square errors. While leave-one-campaign-out validation highlights persistent cross-season domain shift challenges due to localized management variations, compatibility-guided multimodal fusion offers a structured self-diagnostic path toward reliable biophysical parameter estimation

</details>


### [231] [Reward-Conditioned Attention: How Reward Design Shapes What Autonomous Driving Agents See](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25127)
*Mohamed Benabdelouahad,Ahmed Djalal Hacini,Nadir Farhi,Aissa Boulmerka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了奖励设计如何影响基于Perceiver架构的强化学习智能体在自动驾驶任务中的内部注意力模式。关键方法论发现是：跨时间步的简单平均池化会严重低估注意力-风险关系，而使用Fisher z变换聚合的时序相关分析才是正确的统计方法。基于此方法论，实验发现：导航奖励使智能体对GPS路径令牌的关注度提升高达2.0倍；连续碰撞时间惩罚会形成"习得性警觉先验"——即使在无碰撞阶段也保持高警觉水平；在某些场景中，完整奖励模型与最小奖励模型的注意力-风险相关方向完全相反，表明奖励设计可以定性翻转注意力策略。这表明注意力分析可作为验证安全关键型RL系统中奖励函数是否产生预期表征行为的实用诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在安全关键型强化学习系统（如自动驾驶）中，奖励函数设计如何影响智能体的内部注意力机制，以验证注意力分析是否能作为奖励函数设计的诊断工具。

Method: 使用三个架构和数据完全相同但奖励配置不同的Perceiver-based智能体（从基本违规惩罚到连续接近惩罚），在Waymo Open Motion Dataset的50个真实场景中分析交叉注意力分配。提出使用时序内相关性和Fisher z变换聚合替代朴素时间步池化方法。

Result: 1) 朴素时间步池化严重低估注意力-风险关系；2) 导航奖励使GPS路径令牌关注度提升2.0-4.7倍；3) 连续碰撞时间惩罚形成"习得性警觉先验"；4) 不同奖励配置下注意力-风险相关方向可完全相反。

Conclusion: 注意力分析可作为安全关键型RL系统中奖励函数表征行为的实用诊断工具，奖励设计不仅能调节注意力强度，还能定性改变注意力策略方向。

Abstract: We investigate how reward design shapes the internal attention patterns of reinforcement learning agents trained for autonomous driving. Using three Perceiver-based agents that share identical architectures and training data but differ only in their reward configurations$\unicode{x2014}$ranging from basic violation penalties to continuous proximity penalties$\unicode{x2014}$we analyze cross-attention allocation across 50 real-world scenarios from the Waymo Open Motion Dataset. A central methodological finding is that naïve pooling of timesteps across episodes substantially underestimates the attention$\unicode{x2013}$risk relationship; within-episode correlation with Fisher z-transform aggregation is the appropriate statistic and reveals a robustly positive link between collision risk and agent-directed attention. Building on this validated methodology, we demonstrate two reward-conditioned effects: agents trained with navigation rewards allocate up to $2.0\times$ more attention to GPS-path tokens than those trained with additional proximity penalties$\unicode{x2014}$and $4.7\times$ more than agents with no navigation incentive$\unicode{x2014}$revealing that reward content directly determines which scene elements the encoder prioritizes, and continuous time-to-collision penalties create a $\textit{learned vigilance prior}$$\unicode{x2014}$elevated resting agent surveillance maintained throughout collision-free phases. In several scenarios, the complete-reward and minimal-reward models exhibit opposite attention$\unicode{x2013}$risk correlation directions, demonstrating that reward design can qualitatively reverse attentional strategy rather than merely modulating its magnitude. These results suggest that attention analysis is a practical diagnostic for verifying that a reward function produces the intended representational behaviour in safety-critical RL systems.

</details>


### [232] [Silent Failures in Physics-Informed Neural Networks: Parameter Poisoning and the Limits of Loss-Based Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25151)
*David McShannon,Nicholas Dietrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在编码物理方程参数被篡改时，即使训练损失极低，其求解结果也可能严重错误。作者提出了一种后验防御方法：在不重新训练的情况下扫描残差损失随参数的变化，损失最小值能准确恢复真实的训练参数。该方法在三种PDE系统、五种网络架构下均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINNs以低训练损失作为解具有物理正确性的证据，但该假设在编码的物理方程参数不正确时（参数误设定/投毒）会失效。本文旨在揭示这一漏洞，并探索有效的检测与防御方法。

Method: 对PDE参数进行扰动后训练（参数投毒），使模型达到低损失但给出错误解；定义检测困难比R（解误差/训练损失）；评估六种候选防御方法；提出后验防御：在不重新训练的情况下，扫描PDE残差损失随参数值的变化，通过损失最小值恢复真实训练参数。

Result: 在Burgers方程、Navier-Stokes空腔流、对流扩散方程上，投毒模型训练损失匹配或低于清洁基线，但解误差高达71%（固定扫描）和128%（对抗搜索）；在Cavity Re=400时投毒损失低于清洁基线；六种候选防御均不可靠；所提后验防御在所有三种PDE系统和五种架构（8.7K-133K参数）下均能恢复真实参数。

Conclusion: 低残差损失不能保证PINNs解的物理准确性；所提出的后验参数扫描方法是一种无需外部数据、无需重新训练的有效防御手段，具有跨系统、跨架构的泛化能力。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) embed governing equations in their loss function, enabling mesh-free solutions to partial differential equations. Low training loss is treated as evidence that the learned solution is physically correct. This paper shows that assumption breaks down when encoded physics are incorrect. By perturbing PDE parameters before training, a setting we describe as physics parameter poisoning or parameter misspecification, we produce models that train to low loss but give incorrect answers; we treat the perturbation schedule as sensitivity analysis rather than only as a security threat, and none of our claims requires an adversary. Achieving low residual loss does not discriminate accurate from inaccurate solutions: poisoned models reach losses at or below the clean baseline yet differ by large margins, so driving the residual down is not evidence of physical accuracy. Across three PDE systems (Burgers equation, Navier-Stokes cavity, and convection-diffusion), poisoned models match or beat the clean-model training loss while their solutions differ by up to 71% in the fixed sweep and up to 128% under adversarial search; at Cavity Re=400 the poisoned loss falls below the clean baseline. We define a detection difficulty ratio R (solution error divided by training loss) to summarize how invisible the corruption is, though cross-PDE comparison is complicated by differences in loss scale. We test six candidate defenses, none of which reliably detects corruption across all regimes. We propose a post-hoc defense: sweeping the PDE residual loss across parameter values without retraining. The loss minimum recovers the true training parameter without external data, and generalizes across all three PDE systems. The effect holds across five network architectures (8.7K to 133K parameters), is bidirectional, and is confirmed across multiple random seeds.

</details>


### [233] [Semantic Allocation in Ordered Bottlenecks: Predictive Residual Inference for Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25232)
*Erik Ayari,Manuel Traub,Martin V. Butz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRIOR是一种新颖的有序瓶颈框架，通过对数缩放层级和层级预测器替代传统掩码机制，在对比学习和图像重建任务中实现了更好的有序表示学习，尤其在离散/量化设置下显著优于MBOP基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于掩码的有序压力（MBOP）方法存在两个固有弱点：缺乏显式细化目标，仅将梯度暴露作为重要性代理；在离散或量化表示等优化敏感场景中表示变得脆弱。因此需要一种更鲁棒的有序表示学习框架。

Method: PRIOR用log2缩放层级和层级预测器替代激活率控制。预测器将已解释信息与未解释信息分离，使每个层级专注于残差误差。在对比学习和图像重建任务中，与MBOP-TD和MBOP-ITD基线进行比较。

Result: PRIOR在所有实验中学习了良好有序的表示：低预算提供粗糙描述，高预算增加细化。在几乎所有设置中全预算性能更高，仅在一种设置中性能相当。在离散和量化设置中，MBOP基线严重受限，而PRIOR接近连续对应方法的性能。

Conclusion: PRIOR被确立为一种有效的有序表示学习框架，克服了MBOP方法的固有缺陷，在多种设置下展现出更优的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Ordered bottlenecks aim to provide utility at flexible budgets by assigning coarse information to early tokens and task-relevant detail to later ones. Prior work, including tail dropping (TD), typically enforces ordering by means of a masking-based ordering pressure (MBOP): Late tokens are masked more frequently than early tokens and are therefore encouraged to store less essential fine details. We introduce predictive residual inference for ordered representations (PRIOR), a framework designed to address inherent weaknesses of MBOP. MBOP is prone to weak late-token utility because it lacks an explicit refinement objective and uses gradient exposure as a proxy for importance. Furthermore, representations may become particularly brittle in optimization-sensitive settings, such as when using discrete or quantized token representations. PRIOR replaces activation-rate control with log2-scaled levels and level-wise predictors. These predictors separate already explained from unexplained information, focusing each level on residual error. We compare PRIOR against MBOP-TD and independent tail-biased dropout (MBOP-ITD) in contrastive learning and image reconstruction tasks. Unlike the baselines, PRIOR learns well-ordered representations across experiments: low budgets provide coarse descriptors, while high budgets add refinements. Simultaneously, full-budget performance with PRIOR is higher in all but one experimental setting, where performance remains comparable. MBOP baselines are severely limited in discrete and quantized settings, while PRIOR approaches the performance of continuous counterparts. Taken together, these findings establish PRIOR as an effective framework for ordered representation learning.

</details>


### [234] [ATMA: Length-Invariant Language Modeling via Polar Attention and Gated-Delta Compression Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25156)
*Habibullah Akbar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ATMA，一种混合卷积-注意力架构，通过三通道注意力机制（极化方向通道、有界幅度通道、门控delta快速权重循环记忆）解决大语言模型在长上下文中的softmax注意力稀释和结构矛盾问题。实验表明，ATMA在64K tokens（训练长度2K的32倍）上保持90%以上检索准确率，且文档困惑度单调下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于softmax点积注意力的LLM受限于训练序列长度：当KV序列增长时，softmax概率质量在更宽分布上稀释，导致激活偏移和长上下文性能崩溃。同时，滑动窗口注意力虽保持低困惑度但缺乏长程依赖，全上下文注意力虽保持全局记忆但面临分布外困惑度爆炸。

Method: ATMA采用混合卷积-注意力架构，将注意力混合步骤分解为三个通道：(1) 不计数的单位向量方向通道；(2) 由有效匹配参与比驱动的有界幅度通道，结合极值校正的空值汇；(3) 通过门控delta快速权重规则优化的长时循环压缩记忆。极化注意力核心与循环记忆两者缺一不可。

Result: 100次运行的全因子消融实验表明，结合极化注意力和记忆的模型在64K tokens（训练长度2K的32倍）上保持诱导针检索准确率高于90%，文档困惑度单调改善，优于在极端上下文长度下崩溃的softmax基线。

Conclusion: 极化注意力核心与循环记忆的组合能够同时实现困惑度单调降低和高保真长程检索，突破了传统softmax注意力在长上下文场景下的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Modern large language models based on softmax scaled-dot-product attention are constrained by their training sequence length: as the key-value sequence grows, softmax probability mass can dilute across a wider distribution, inducing activation shift and long-context performance collapse. Moreover, long-context language modeling faces a structural tension: a sliding-window attention core maintains a bounded local representation and low perplexity but is blind to long-range dependencies, while full-context attention preserves global recall but suffers from out-of-distribution perplexity explosion. To resolve these limitations, we introduce ATMA, a hybrid convolutional-attention architecture that integrates a novel three-channel attention mechanism. ATMA factorizes the attention mixing step into: (1) a count-blind, unit-vector direction channel, (2) a bounded magnitude channel driven by the participation ratio of effective matches over an extreme-value-corrected null sink, and (3) a long-term recurrent compression memory optimized via a gated-delta fast-weights rule. Neither the Polar Attention core nor the recurrent memory is sufficient alone; their combination enables monotonic perplexity reduction and high-fidelity long-range retrieval simultaneously. We evaluate ATMA using a 100-run factorial ablation sweep, demonstrating that the combined Polar + memory model maintains induction needle-in-a-haystack retrieval accuracy above 90% out to 64K tokens (32 times the training length of 2K) while its document perplexity improves monotonically, outperforming softmax-based memory baselines which collapse at extreme context lengths. Code: https://github.com/kreasof-ai/atma

</details>


### [235] [Geometry-Anchored Transport Framework for Exemplar-Free Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25347)
*Hongye Xu,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出几何锚定传输框架，将特征传输作为内生训练约束而非后处理步骤，通过解析几何锚定和拓扑感知演化目标，在无样本类增量学习中有效缓解各向异性表征漂移和局部流形退化，在多个数据集上优于现有后处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无样本类增量学习方法采用解耦的后处理范式，在优化骨干网络时缺乏显式几何约束，导致旧类别流形发生各向异性漂移，限制了后验对齐的精度，进而影响分类边界稳定性。

Method: 首先通过马氏对齐回归推导解析几何锚定以缓解宏观各向异性漂移；其次引入拓扑感知演化目标，在训练阶段正则化局部流形退化并校准残差网络与解析先验的一致性，将流形演化与传输约束耦合到主训练阶段。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet和ImageNet-100数据集上的实验表明，在严格无样本约束下，所提框架一致优于现有的后处理替代方法。

Conclusion: 将特征传输作为内生训练约束而非解耦的后处理步骤，能够更有效地缓解表征漂移和流形退化，提升无样本类增量学习的分类性能。

Abstract: Exemplar-free class-incremental learning (EFCIL) requires stable decision boundaries within a shifting feature space. While maintaining class-conditional Gaussian statistics provides a principled classification strategy, these parametric summaries remain sensitive to anisotropic representation drift. Existing methods often transport these statistics across tasks using a decoupled, post-hoc paradigm: optimizing a backbone without explicit geometric constraints can distort the legacy manifold, limiting the precision of retroactive alignment. In this paper, we formulate feature transport as an endogenous training constraint rather than a separate post-task step, presenting the Geometry-Anchored Transport Framework. First, we derive an Analytic Geometric Anchor via Mahalanobis-aligned regression to mitigate macroscopic anisotropic drift. Second, we introduce a Topology-Aware Evolution objective that regularizes localized manifold degradation while calibrating a residual network against the analytic prior. By coupling manifold evolution with transport constraints during the primary training phase, our framework mitigates evaluation errors without requiring decoupled fine-tuning. Experiments across CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and ImageNet-100 demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves upon existing post-hoc alternatives under strict exemplar-free constraints.

</details>


### [236] [The Gentle Collapse: Distributional Metrics for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25165)
*Ahmed Anwar,Andreas Wagner,Federico Raue,Tobias Nauen,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出六种基于softmax的连续遗忘度量指标（TLR、置信度、分布散度等），在准确率饱和时仍能捕捉遗忘的内部结构，并证明这些指标可有效用于缓解灾难性遗忘，在CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet上优于传统经验回放基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确率作为灾难性遗忘的标准度量存在饱和（极端值失效）和离散塌缩（任务边界处突变）的缺陷，无法揭示遗忘的内部结构信息。作者希望引入更细粒度、连续的度量指标来表征遗忘过程，并利用这些信息缓解遗忘。

Method: 提出六种softmax衍生度量：真实标签排名（TLR）、预测置信度、混淆间隔等，均归一化至[0,1]且不修改训练过程。通过两种方式利用这些信号：1）以样本级度量分数作为损失权重；2）基于小窗口内度量斜率作为采样标准（趋势采样）。在CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet上使用经验回放（ER）框架验证。

Result: 1）在0%准确率时，混淆间隔在不同类别间IQR为[0.32, 0.50]，准确率无法区分；2）损失权重法在CIFAR-100上比均匀ER降低1.3个百分点的遗忘；3）小窗口（3 epoch）下准确率趋势退化至34.79%（std=2.32），而log-TLR达41.07%（std=0.57）；4）在TinyImageNet上，log-TLR趋势采样比ER基线降低7.7个百分点的遗忘。

Conclusion: 连续度量比准确率能更丰富地表征遗忘结构，且这些信号可有效用于缓解灾难性遗忘，尤其在趋势估计中，连续信号相比离散的准确率具有结构性优势。

Abstract: Accuracy degradation is the standard metric for Catastrophic Forgetting (CF), however, it records only whether forgetting occurred or not. It saturates at the extremes and collapses discretely at task boundaries, hiding the internal structure of what is being forgotten. We introduce six softmax-derived metrics spanning true-label rank (TLR), predictive confidence, and distributional divergence that characterize forgetting continuously, each normalized to [0, 1] with no modification to training. On CIFAR-100, these metrics carry information where accuracy does not: at 0% accuracy, the Confusion Margin spans an IQR of [0.32, 0.50] across classes that accuracy treats identically. We demonstrate that this richer signal is actionable in mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Per-sample metric scores used as loss weights reduce forgetting by 1.3 percentage points over uniform experience replay (ER) on CIFAR-100. Furthermore, the slope of a metric over a small window provides a stable sampling criterion: at a small-window size (e.g. 3 epochs), accuracy-trend degrades to 34.79% (std. = 2.32) while log-TLR achieves 41.07% (std. = 0.57). This gap is structural since reliable small-window trend estimation requires a continuous signal. On TinyImageNet, log-TLR trend sampling reduces forgetting by 7.7 percentage points over the ER baseline.

</details>


### [237] [Brevity is the Soul of Inference Efficiency: Inducing Concision in VLMs via Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25432)
*DatologyAI,:,Matthew L. Leavitt,Siddharth Joshi,Haoli Yin,Rishabh Adiga,Haakon Mongstad,Alvin Deng,David Schwab,Bogdan Gaza,Ari Morcos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将"简洁性"作为推理效率的新杠杆，通过预训练数据筛选训练模型输出更简洁的答案，从而降低每次正确回答的FLOPs成本。在1B-4B VLMs上，经数据筛选的模型相比最冗长的基线实现了35倍的成本优势，准确率几乎无损，且简洁性带来的提升随模型规模扩大而增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理效率优化方法（蒸馏、剪枝、量化、稀疏路由）都专注于降低单token成本，却忽略了输出长度不断膨胀的问题。本文论证"简洁性"是被忽视的效率杠杆，而预训练数据筛选是实现简洁性的实用途径。

Method: 将VLM数据筛选管线应用于MAmmoTH-VL单图像子集，训练简洁模型。通过每个模型的回归模型固定输出长度，将简洁性与质量解耦，以每次正确回答的FLOPs衡量效率。在20个评估集和14个VLM（1B-4B激活参数）上进行受控比较。

Result: 数据筛选带来35倍成本优势（0.41 vs 14.58 TFLOPs/正确回答），准确率几乎无损（0.691 vs 0.704）。相比未筛选基线，匹配长度后准确率提升17.55个百分点，且随规模扩大（1B的+16.7pp到4B的+21.2pp）。通用冗长性在任何能力水平都不带来准确率收益；推理结构化的冗长性收益窗口随规模扩大急剧缩小（2B时4/8能力组→4B时1/8）。简洁模型甚至能回答冗长推理模型答错的问题。

Conclusion: 推理效率本质上是"每次正确回答的token数"问题，简洁性直接瞄准这一目标。推理本身应作为独立的数据筛选目标，而非简洁性需要牺牲的东西。

Abstract: Inference efficiency is typically pursued by shrinking the model: distillation, pruning, quantization, and sparse routing each lower per-token cost while treating token count as fixed. But output length has been inflating, and it is precisely the component the standard toolkit leaves untouched. Here, we argue that brevity is the missing inference-efficiency lever, and that pretraining data curation is a practical way to pull it: a model trained on concise, correct data learns to answer in fewer tokens; i.e. it has a lower Cost-of-Pass. We apply our VLM curation pipeline to the MAmmoTH-VL single-image subset, and compare models trained on our curated data, the standard MAmmoTH-VL data, and external open-weight frontier VLMs. On a controlled 20-evaluation set and 14 VLMs at 1B-4B activated parameters, we hold output length fixed with a per-model regression, separating brevity from quality, and price models in FLOPs per correct answer. Curation buys a 35x Cost-of-Pass advantage over the most verbose 4B comparator (Qwen3.5-4B) within $\sim$1 pp of accuracy (0.41 vs 14.58 TFLOPs per correct answer; 0.691 vs 0.704 mean accuracy). Curation also buys a +17.55-percentage-point matched-length accuracy gain over the uncurated baseline that grows with model scale (from +16.7 pp at 1B to +21.2 pp at 4B). This brevity improvement concedes no quality: generic verbosity buys no accuracy at any capability or scale, and the window where reasoning-structured verbosity still earns its tokens shrinks from 4 of 8 capability groups at 2B to 1 of 8 at 4B. Per example, the concise model even reaches correct answers the verbose reasoning model misses, marking reasoning as a distinct curation target rather than something brevity gives up. Inference efficiency in this regime is a tokens-per-correct problem, and brevity is the lever that targets it directly.

</details>


### [238] [Uncertainty Quantification for Computer-Use Agents: A Benchmark across Vision-Language Models and GUI Grounding Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25760)
*Divake Kumar,Sina Tayebati,Devashri Naik,Amanda Sofie Rios,Nilesh Ahuja,Omesh Tickoo,Ranganath Krishnan,Amit Ranjan Trivedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Argus，一个跨场景的后验不确定性量化（UQ）基准，用于单步可执行GUI定位。通过4个VLM智能体、4个数据集和27种开源方法（以及8种闭源方法）的系统评估，发现UQ排名在固定模型下跨数据集稳定，但在模型类别和可观测接口变化时显著退化。隐藏状态和密度估计方法是开源模型中最稳定的，而CoCoA-1MCA、Focus、基于采样的分数和口头化置信度在特定场景中表现最佳。此外，仅凭分数级区分不足以部署，需要校准的UQ来缩小置信区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于GUI智能体后验不确定性量化的研究分散在孤立的模型与数据集对中，缺乏统一基准来评估UQ排名在不同智能体、基准测试或可观测接口变化时的稳定性，阻碍了可靠的不确定性估计在拒绝、校准、严重性排序和安全空间区域等关键任务中的应用。

Method: 构建Argus跨场景基准：包含4个VLM智能体（开源和闭源前沿供应商）、4个数据集、27种开源UQ方法（涵盖logit分数、采样与一致性度量、隐藏状态与密度估计器、注意力分数、P(True)和口头化置信度提示、裂分共形预测）以及8种闭源方法（仅有黑盒访问）。系统评估UQ排名在跨数据集、跨模型类、跨接口场景下的稳定性。使用Spearman秩相关系数衡量排名一致性，并评估共形预测区域的覆盖率和半径。

Result: 1) UQ排名在固定模型下跨数据集稳定（Spearman rho高达0.969）；2) 跨模型类和接口时排名显著退化；3) 隐藏状态和密度方法在开源模型中最稳定；4) CoCoA-1MCA、Focus、基于采样的分数和口头化自评估在特定场景最佳；5) 跨层级（开源→闭源）排名迁移仅平均+0.08，闭源UQ需在目标模型上重新排名；6) 共形预测区域表明分数级区分不足：校准后的局部加权圆盘半径缩小40-60%，但在校准-测试或接口不匹配时覆盖度下降。

Conclusion: 选择性迁移是核心发现：UQ排名在固定模型下跨数据集稳定，但跨模型类和接口时失效。闭源UQ不应从开源模型外推，而需在目标模型上重排。共形预测揭示了分数级UQ与部署就绪之间的差距，校准的局部加权区域可显著缩小半径，但需注意覆盖度退化问题。发布开源数据集和分析脚本以支持场景感知的UQ选择。

Abstract: Computer-use agents turn vision-language model (VLM) predictions into executable GUI clicks, so reliable uncertainty estimates are essential for rejection, calibration, miss-severity ranking, and spatial safety regions. Yet evidence on post-hoc uncertainty quantification (UQ) for these agents is fragmented across isolated model and dataset pairs, leaving it unclear whether UQ rankings stay stable when the agent, benchmark, or observable interface changes. We present Argus, a cross-regime benchmark for post-hoc UQ in single-step executable GUI grounding: a 27-method open-weight matrix over 4 VLM agents and 4 datasets, plus an 8-method closed-source matrix across 3 frontier vendors where logits, hidden states, and attention maps are unavailable. Evaluated methods span logit-based scores, sampling and consistency measures, hidden-state and density estimators (Mahalanobis, SAPLMA), attention-based scores, P(True) and verbalised-confidence prompting, and split-conformal prediction. The main finding is selective transfer: UQ rankings are stable across datasets for a fixed model, but degrade across model classes and observable interfaces. Hidden-state and density methods are the most stable open-weight family, while CoCoA-1MCA, Focus, sampling-based scores, and verbalised self-assessment win in specific regimes.
