Within-model ranking transfer is strong (Spearman rho up to 0.969), but cross-tier transfer to closed-source vendors averages only +0.08, so closed-source UQ should be reranked on the target rather than extrapolated. Conformal click regions show score-level discrimination is not enough for deployment: locally weighted disks shrink radii by 40-60% when the plug-in UQ is calibrated, but coverage degrades under calibration-test or interface mismatch. We release per-item records, calibration/test splits, UQ scores, and analysis scripts for regime-aware UQ selection in GUI agents.

</details>


### [239] [EveLoad: Cognitive Workload Recognition from Event-Based Eye Movements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25177)
*Guorui Lu,Shaohua Guan,Zhen Xu,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个事件相机眼动数据集EveLoad，采用固定凝视范式在六等级认知负荷下采集数据，并建立基准模型，实现了96%以上的识别准确率，为康复环境中的认知负荷监测提供了新的感知途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于帧的眼动追踪器在认知负荷识别中存在时间分辨率低、快速眼动下鲁棒性差的问题；而事件相机虽有高时间分辨率优势，但缺乏标准化的认知负荷眼动数据集和空间约束下的研究范式，现有模型可能混入注视位置分布而非眼动特征本身的混淆。

Method: 在受控N-back引导固定凝视范式下，通过事件相机采集20名健康被试的六等级认知负荷眼动数据，构建EveLoad数据集；提出基于时空事件表征编码的学习框架，将事件数据转换为适合深度学习处理的结构化表示，并建立多级认知负荷识别基准。

Result: 在六等级分类任务中，所提方法在受试者特异性评估下平均准确率为96.36%，在混合随机划分评估下为96.13%。

Conclusion: 事件相机能够有效捕捉与认知负荷相关的细粒度眼动特征，为自适应康复和辅助界面中无扰式认知状态监测提供了可行的感知通路。

Abstract: Cognitive workload monitoring is important for adaptive rehabilitation and assistive interfaces, where task difficulty, pacing, and feedback should be adjusted according to the user's cognitive state to avoid overload and under-challenge. Emerging extended reality and robot-assisted rehabilitation environments provide controllable training tasks, but they require unobtrusive sensing methods that can capture rapid ocular dynamics during interaction. Existing eye-movement-based cognitive workload recognition methods mainly rely on frame-based eye trackers, which often suffer from limited temporal resolution and degraded robustness under rapid eye movements. In contrast, event cameras provide microsecond-level temporal resolution, high dynamic range and low latency, making them suitable for capturing fine-grained ocular dynamics. Many previous studies rely on free-viewing or similar paradigms, where gaze locations can vary across tasks. As a result, models may learn associations between gaze-location distributions and cognitive workload, rather than workload-related eye movement characteristics themselves. In this work, we introduce EveLoad, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first event-based eye-movement dataset with graded cognitive workload annotations, collected from 20 healthy participants under spatially constrained and task-driven conditions using a controlled N-back-guided fixation paradigm. Based on this dataset, we establish a benchmark for cognitive workload recognition with six workload levels and propose a learning framework that encodes spatiotemporal event representations. Experimental results show that our approach achieves an average subject-specific accuracy of 96.36% and 96.13% under mixed random split evaluation. These results suggest that event-based eye movements may provide a useful sensing pathway for future workload-aware rehabilitation.

</details>


### [240] [Re-mixing Embeddings for Patient Augmentation in Data Scarce Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25770)
*Muhammed Furkan Dasdelen,Fatih Ozlugedik,Anastasia Litinetskaya,Nassir Navab,Carsten Marr,Ario Sadafi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在嵌入空间中基于统计学原理的患者增强方法（RECIPE），通过高斯混合模型学习疾病特异性实例分布“食谱”，从而生成合成患者数据，有效解决了医学多实例学习中的数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学多实例学习（MIL）面临严重的数据稀缺问题，尤其是针对罕见病或昂贵模态的数据获取困难，现有增强方法通常需要所有类别的样本示例，限制了其在缺失类别场景下的应用。

Method: 使用高斯混合模型（GMM）对所有患者的池化实例嵌入进行概率聚类，学习疾病特异性的实例分布"食谱"；然后基于学习到的食谱从聚类中采样嵌入来生成新患者，并通过不确定性量化筛选高质量生成样本。

Result: 在跨数据集迁移（生成缺失的健康类别）、低数据 regime 和小样本非图像任务（单细胞RNA-seq、流式细胞术）三种稀缺场景下，该方法均优于基线方法；在缺失类别场景中达到了与全数据集训练相当的性能。

Conclusion: RECIPE方法为医学MIL中的数据稀缺问题提供了一种有效的解决方案，尤其适用于罕见病诊断和隐私保护的病人数据增强。

Abstract: Data scarcity is a major bottleneck in medical Multiple Instance Learning (MIL), especially for rare diseases or expensive modalities. We introduce a statistically grounded patient augmentation approach that generates realistic patients directly in embedding space. Using Gaussian Mixture Models as a probabilistic clustering approach on pooled instance embeddings from all patients, our method learns disease-specific "recipes"-statistical distributions of instances across unsupervised clusters. New patients are then generated by sampling embeddings from clusters based on learned recipes. Unlike existing methods that require examples from all categories, our method can generate patients offline by re-mixing pooled embeddings. Generated patients are further selected based on uncertainty quantification to improve MIL performance. We evaluate our method across three clinically relevant scarcity scenarios: (i) cross-dataset transfer, where an entirely missing "healthy" class is generated using statistics from an external cohort; (ii) low-data regimes, where class sizes are extremely limited; and (iii) small-cohort non-image tasks, including single-cell RNA-seq and flow cytometry. Across all experiments, our method improves performance over baseline, often outperforming other bag-mixing strategies. Notably, in the missing-class scenario, a performance comparable to full-dataset training is achieved, demonstrating its potential for rare disease diagnostic and privacy-preserving patient augmentation. The code is available at https://github.com/marrlab/RECIPE

</details>


### [241] [Neural operator-based digital twins for modeling amyloid-$β$ and tau propagation and treatment optimization in Alzheimer's disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25185)
*Xiaofeng Xu,Tingting Dan,Zifan Zhou,Bin Li,Guorong Wu,Wenrui Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的数字孪生框架，通过算子学习与降阶表示从纵向PET成像数据中推断阿尔茨海默病中Aβ和tau蛋白的反应-扩散动力学方程，分别达到87%和81%的预测准确率，并进一步构建PDE约束最优控制问题实现个性化治疗策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病中Aβ和tau蛋白的时空演化预测对于诊断和治疗至关重要，但潜在的非线性聚集机制未知，且纵向PET数据稀疏、噪声大、异质性强，亟需一种能从数据中直接学习疾病动力学的框架。

Method: 提出数据驱动框架，将算子学习与降阶表示相结合，从稀疏、带噪声的纵向PET影像数据中推断病理性蛋白在皮层表面传播的反应-扩散方程；在此基础上，构建PDE约束的最优控制问题以设计个性化治疗策略。

Result: 该框架对Aβ的预测准确率达87%，对tau蛋白的预测准确率达81%。

Conclusion: 该数字孪生框架为理解神经退行性疾病进展和实现精准干预提供了可解释、可预测的平台，能够将数据驱动的动力学建模与治疗优化相结合。

Abstract: Accurately predicting the spatiotemporal evolution of amyloid-$β$ and tau proteins at the individual level is critical for improving the diagnosis and treatment of Alzheimer's disease. We consider the problem of constructing patient-specific digital twins that model the propagation of these biomarkers on the cortical surface using reaction--diffusion dynamics. A major challenge is that the underlying nonlinear aggregation mechanisms are unknown and must be inferred from sparse, noisy, and heterogeneous longitudinal PET imaging data. To address this, we develop a data-driven framework that learns biomarker dynamics directly from clinical observations. The approach combines operator learning with reduced-order representations to infer governing equations of disease progression from data. Using this framework, we achieve predictive accuracies of 87\% for amyloid-$β$ and 81\% for tau. Building on the learned dynamics, we further formulate a PDE-constrained optimal control problem to design personalized therapeutic strategies that regulate pathological protein propagation. By integrating data-driven dynamical modeling with treatment optimization, the proposed digital twin framework provides an interpretable and predictive platform for understanding disease progression and enabling precision interventions in neurodegenerative disorders.

</details>


### [242] [Efficient Analytic Uncertainty Quantification for Multi-Modal Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25188)
*Kun Jin,James Harrison,Jiawei Li,Sihan Liu,Jiayi Liu,Randolph Linderman,Yuening Li,Arnab Bhadury,Sourabh Prakash Bansod,Liang Liu,Jasper Snoek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变分贝叶斯推断的统一框架，将不确定性量化技术扩展到分位数回归和分类恢复两种半参数回归模型，能够高效处理多模态标签分布的大规模回归任务，并在理论上推导了解析ELBO，实验上在三个大规模回归基准上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有回归任务的不确定性量化方法主要假设条件标签分布为单峰参数模型（如高斯），无法处理多模态分布；而半参数方法虽能处理多模态分布，但缺乏对预测方差的高效量化。

Method: 将变分贝叶斯推断扩展到分位数回归（QR）和分类恢复（CR）两种半参数回归模型中，推导出解析形式的证据下界（ELBO）以简化训练，并得到闭式或解析近似的预测密度以实现高效推理。

Result: 在三个大规模多模态回归基准上，该方法超越了最先进的多模态回归基线，甚至匹配了计算昂贵的集成模型的预测性能；同时通过认知不确定性估计实现了高数据效率的主动学习策略。

Conclusion: 提出的统一框架能够同时实现对复杂条件分布的精确估计和高效率的不确定性量化，且分布无关，具有理论和实践上的优势。

Abstract: Efficient uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for trustworthy large-scale learning. Existing UQ methods for regression tasks mainly operate under the assumption that the conditional label marginal satisfies single-peak parametric models, e.g., Gaussians, where the negative log-likelihood function simplifies to the mean square error. However, such single-peak assumptions fail in regression tasks featuring multi-modal distributions. On the other hand, semi-parametric methods which achieve strong regression performance for multi-modal distributions often lack efficient quantification on their prediction variances. In this work, we extend UQ techniques based on Variational Bayesian Inference (VBI) to two widely used semi-parametric regression models that yield histogram-like reconstructions of the conditional label densities: Quantile Regression (QR) and Classification Restoration (CR). Our approach introduces a unified, distribution-agnostic framework that simultaneously achieves accurate estimation of complex conditional distributions and highly efficient UQ. Theoretically, our method is grounded in novel formulations of QR and CR within the VBI framework, yielding analytic Evidence Lower Bounds (ELBO) to streamline training and a closed-form or analytically approximated predictive density for efficient inference. Empirically, we evaluate our methods on three large-scale regression benchmarks with multi-modal label distributions. Our framework outperforms state-of-the-art multi-modal regression baselines, and even matches predictive performance of computationally expensive ensemble models. Furthermore, by leveraging epistemic uncertainty estimation, our approach enables highly data-efficient active learning strategies.

</details>


### [243] [Tensorion: A Tensor-Aware Generalization of the Muon Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25975)
*Vladimir Bogachev,Vladimir Aletov,Alexander Molozhavenko,Sergei Kudriashov,Maxim Rakhuba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tensorion是一种张量感知优化器，它将Muon的谱范数约束优化从矩阵扩展到高阶张量，通过线性最小化预言机在张量范数球上实现，在张量基计算机视觉任务中展现出比Adam和现有张量感知基线更好的收敛性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一阶优化器（如Adam）将每个参数块视为非结构化向量，忽略了现代机器学习模型中广泛存在的多线性权重结构。最近的工作（如Muon）表明利用矩阵结构可以改善优化动力学，但尚未扩展到更高阶的张量。

Method: Tensorion构建了一个线性最小化预言机（LMO），在精心设计的张量范数球上运行。该范数平衡了两个目标：紧密约束张量谱范数，同时保持LMO的可计算性。LMO的可计算性源于它被简化为对自适应选择的展开矩阵的操作。当限制为二阶张量（矩阵）时，Tensorion精确地恢复了Muon。

Result: 在张量基计算机视觉问题上的实验表明，Tensorion在评估设置下比基于Adam和现有张量感知的基线方法具有更好的收敛行为和更稳定的梯度更新。

Conclusion: Tensorion通过将谱范数约束优化思想从矩阵扩展到高阶张量，成功利用张量结构信息改进了优化器性能，为处理具有多线性权重结构的现代机器学习模型提供了更有效的优化方案。

Abstract: Common first-order optimizers, such as Adam, implicitly treat each parameter block as an unstructured vector, which disregards the multilinear weight structure present in many modern machine learning models. Recent work has shown that exploiting matrix structure can improve optimization dynamics. A notable example is Muon, which performs steepest descent under the spectral norm constraint. We take the next step and introduce Tensorion, a tensor-aware optimizer that extends Muon's constrained optimization perspective from matrices to higher-order tensors. Tensorion is built around a linear minimization oracle (LMO) over a tensor norm ball. The norm is carefully chosen to balance two objectives: tightly bounding the tensor spectral norm, while still keeping the LMO tractable. This LMO becomes computable because it reduces to operations on adaptively selected unfolding matrices. Notably, when restricted to order-2 tensors (i.e., matrices), Tensorion recovers Muon exactly. Experiments on tensor-based computer vision problems suggest that Tensorion can offer improved convergence behavior and more stable gradient updates compared with Adam-based and existing tensor-aware baselines in the evaluated settings.

</details>


### [244] [A Hybrid CNN-LSTM Intrusion Detection Framework for Cybersecurity in Smart Renewable Energy Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25200)
*Sajib Debnath,Remon Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合CNN-LSTM入侵检测系统，联合空间特征提取与时间序列建模，解决多步攻击检测、类别不平衡和跨环境泛化三大难题。在CICIDS2017和NSL-KDD基准测试中达到98.2%和96.1%准确率，支持27,800流/秒的实时推理吞吐量，并在资源受限的智能电子设备上实现INT8量化部署（3.1倍加速，精度损失仅0.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源智能电网的数字化（IoT传感器、AMI、SCADA）显著扩大了网络攻击面，尤其是FDI攻击（篡改状态估计）和DoS/DDoS攻击（堵塞通信信道）。现有入侵检测系统存在三大固有局限：（1）对多步攻击的时间演进过程建模不足；（2）在标准数据集极度偏斜的类别分布下可扩展性下降；（3）跨异构网络环境的泛化能力受限。

Method: 构建混合CNN-LSTM入侵检测模型：CNN模块提取空间特征，LSTM模块建模时间序列。采用七步预处理流程：缺失值填补→Min-Max归一化→独热编码→SMOTE类别平衡→互信息特征选择→因果时间序列构造（T=10）→分层划分。在CICIDS2017和NSL-KDD数据集上评估，并与LSTM、CNN、随机森林、SVM、KNN进行对比。INT8量化用于边缘部署优化。

Result: CICIDS2017上精度98.2%（LSTM 96.4%、CNN 95.2%、RF 92.7%、SVM 90.8%）；NSL-KDD上LSTM 96.1%、RF 93.5%、SVM 91.2%、KNN 89.7%。消融分析显示SMOTE平衡最关键（缺少时F1下降3.7个百分点）。实时推理：GPU 27,800流/秒，CPU FP32延迟0.082ms/样本，INT8量化实现3.1倍加速，精度损失仅0.3%。可在<128MB内存的智能电子设备上部署。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合CNN-LSTM IDS有效克服了现有方法的三大局限，在检测精度、实时性和跨场景泛化上均达到最优性能。INT8量化方案证实了在资源受限的电网边缘设备上部署的可行性，为下一代可再生能源智能电网基础设施提供了可靠的安全框架。

Abstract: The accelerated digitalization of renewable energy smart grids through IoT sensors, AMI, and SCADA systems has significantly expanded the attack surface for sophisticated cyberattacks, FDI attacks that stealthily distort state estimation and DoS/DDoS attacks that flood communication channels. Current IDS, however, exhibit three inherent limitations: inadequate modeling of the temporal progression of multi-step attacks, degraded scalability under extremely skewed class distributions of standard benchmark datasets, and restricted generalization across heterogeneous network environments. In this study, we present a Hybrid CNN-LSTM IDS that jointly exploits CNN-based spatial feature extraction and LSTM-based temporal sequence modeling, enabling the detection of instantaneous volumetric anomalies and gradually evolving low and slow-attack campaigns in real time. The model was trained using a seven-step preprocessing workflow comprising missing-value imputation, min-max normalization, one-hot encoding, SMOTE class balancing, mutual-information feature selection, causal temporal sequence construction (T=10), and stratified partitioning. LSTM (96.1%), Random Forest (93.5%), SVM (91.2%) and KNN (89.7%); in NSL-KDD, it reaches 98.2% precision versus 96.4% (LSTM), 95.2% (CNN), 92.7% (Random Forest) and 90.8% (SVM), with margins of 2-9 percentage points in all measures. An ablation analysis identified SMOTE balancing as the most influential design choice (-3.7~pp F1 without it). The model achieves a real-time inference throughput of 27,800 flows/s on GPU and 0.082 ms/sample CPU latency in FP32,, with INT8 quantization providing an additional 3.1 x speedup at 0.3% accuracy loss, confirming deployment feasibility on resource-constrained IEDs with <128MB memory and establishing a deployable deep-learning framework for securing next-generation renewable energy smart grid infrastructure.

</details>


### [245] [FDN: Interpretable Spatiotemporal Forecasting with Future Decomposition Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25201)
*Nicholas Majeske,Ariful Azad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出未来分解网络(FDN)，一种兼具可解释性和高精度的时间序列预测模型，在多个时空系统数据集上达到SOTA水平，同时大幅降低存储和运行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA时空预测方法精度虽高，但缺乏可解释性，难以理解潜在的活动模式。

Method: 提出Future Decomposition Network (FDN)，通过分类机制实现可解释预测，同时揭示目标时间序列中的潜在活动模式。

Result: 在水文、交通和能源等多个数据集上的实验表明，FDN的预测精度与SOTA方法相当，但存储和运行成本显著更低。

Conclusion: FDN在保持竞争力的同时提升了预测的可解释性和效率，为时空预测提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal systems comprise a collection of spatially distributed yet interdependent entities each generating unique dynamic signals. Highly sophisticated methods have been proposed in recent years delivering state-of-the-art (SOTA) forecasts but few have focused on interpretability. To address this, we propose the Future Decomposition Network (FDN), a novel forecast model capable of (a) providing interpretable predictions through classification (b) revealing latent activity patterns in the target time-series and (c) delivering forecasts competitive with SOTA methods at a fraction of their memory and runtime cost. We conduct comprehensive analyses on FDN for multiple datasets from hydrologic, traffic, and energy systems, demonstrating its improved accuracy and interpretability.

</details>


### [246] [ASAP: Agent-System Co-Design for Wall-Clock-Centered Auto HPO Research for ML Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25207)
*Taicheng Guo,Haomin Zhuang,Kehan Guo,Yujun Zhou,Nitesh V. Chawla,Olaf Wiest,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASAP是一个智能体-系统协同设计方案，通过LLM集成多种带归纳偏置的优化器并利用推测并行等技术，在保持优化质量的同时显著降低端到端运行时间，解决现有LLM-based HPO方法在问题多样性和实际耗时上的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用于HPO的方法存在两个局限：(i) LLM作为单一工具替换时，受自身预训练目标约束，仍只能提供一种归纳偏置，无法应对多样化问题；(ii) 以迭代次数评估性能忽略了实际运行中LLM推理和工具执行是串行叠加在模型评估之上的，迭代次数上的优势无法转化为端到端时间上的优势。

Method: ASAP采用智能体-系统协同设计：(i) 智能体层面：LLM整合多样化带归纳偏置的优化器池，每轮选择其中的候选方案；(ii) 系统层面：前缀稳定提示词实现跨轮KV缓存复用；推测并行机制通过相对误差接受测试将LLM和工具延迟隐藏在模型评估之下；自调优器在关键路径外根据执行日志自适应调整推测阈值。

Result: 在多种现代HPO任务上的大量实验表明，ASAP在优化质量和端到端运行时间上均持续优于基线方法，验证了工具集成与智能体-系统协同设计的价值。

Conclusion: ASAP通过智能体-系统协同设计有效解决了现有LLM-based HPO方法在问题多样性和实际耗时上的双重局限，证明了工具集成与系统级优化相结合的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Hyperparameter Optimization (HPO) is essential for maximizing machine learning model performance, and its core challenge is sample efficiency: finding strong configurations within a limited budget. Because every HPO tool relies on a surrogate prior that imparts its own inductive bias, individual tools struggle once problems become sufficiently diverse and drift from these priors. Motivated by the reasoning and generalization capabilities of LLMs, recent work has explored using LLMs for HPO and reports improved per-iteration performance. Yet these methods share two limitations with a common origin: they use the LLM as a single-tool replacement evaluated by iteration count. (i) Deployed in place of prior tools, the LLM is itself constrained by its pretraining objective to one family of inductive-biased proposals; this single-source setup still fails to handle the full diversity of problems. (ii) Per-iteration evaluation ignores that, in real runs, LLM inference or tool execution is paid serially on top of model evaluation every round, so iteration-count gains do not translate into end-to-end wall-clock gains. We present ASAP, an agent-system co-design that addresses both limitations. On the agent side, ASAP uses the LLM to integrate a diverse pool of inductive-biased optimizers and to select among their proposals each round. On the system side, ASAP re-architects the loop to reduce end-to-end wall-clock while preserving regret quality: a prefix-stable prompt maximizes KV-cache reuse across rounds; speculation parallelism hides the remaining LLM and tool latency under model evaluation via a relative-error accept test; and a Self-Tuner adapts the speculation threshold from execution logs off the critical path. Extensive experiments on diverse modern HPO tasks show that ASAP consistently outperforms baselines, underscoring the value of tool integration and agent-system co-design.

</details>


### [247] [Variational Inference via Entropic Transport Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25265)
*Vincent Pacelli,Akash Ratheesh,Evangelos Theodorou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出熵输运下降（ETD），一种新的粒子变分推断方法，通过熵正则化最优传输解决SVGD在高维和 multimodal 场景中的方差坍缩与模式坍塌问题，在多个任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于核排斥的粒子变分推断方法（如SVGD）在高维空间中存在方差坍缩，在多模态目标分布上存在模式坍塌，根本原因在于缺乏全局传输结构。需要一种能提供全局协调、自然保留多模态结构的粒子更新方法。

Method: 将每个粒子更新建模为熵正则化最优传输问题，通过JKO邻近方案提升到耦合空间并利用KL链式法则松弛，每个ETD迭代简化为Sinkhorn计算。该传输计划提供全局协调，引导粒子到附近高密度提案，无需评分函数，仅需未归一化目标密度的逐点评估。

Result: 在方差坍缩诊断、贝叶斯逻辑回归、神经网络和分子玻尔兹曼分布等实验中，ETD匹配或优于SVGD、AGF-SVGD和SGLD，在高维和多模态设置中提升最大。

Conclusion: ETD通过引入熵正则化最优传输框架，为粒子变分推断提供了全局传输结构，有效克服了SVGD的方差坍缩和模式坍塌问题，且无需评分函数，是一种有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Particle-based variational inference (ParVI) methods approximate an intractable target distribution by evolving an ensemble of interacting samples. Existing approaches rely predominantly on kernel-based repulsion (e.g., SVGD), which suffers from variance collapse in high dimensions and mode collapse on multimodal targets -- pathologies caused by the absence of global transport structure. We introduce entropic transport descent (ETD), a ParVI family that frames each particle update as an entropy-regularized optimal transport problem. Derived from the JKO proximal scheme by lifting to the space of couplings and relaxing via the KL chain rule, each ETD iteration reduces to a Sinkhorn computation. The resulting transport plan provides global coordination, guiding each particle to nearby high-density proposals and naturally preserving multimodal structure. ETD can operate entirely score-free, requiring only pointwise evaluations of the unnormalized target density. Experiments on variance-collapse diagnostics, Bayesian logistic regression, neural networks, and molecular Boltzmann distributions show that ETD matches or outperforms SVGD, AGF-SVGD, and SGLD, with the largest gains in high-dimensional and multimodal settings.

</details>


### [248] [Inverse Reinforcement Learning for Interpretable Keystroke Biomarkers in Parkinson's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25270)
*Navin Bondade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将逆强化学习（IRL）应用于帕金森病（PD）的击键动力学分析，通过可解释的奖励函数建模每位受试者的打字速度偏好，发现速度偏好权重与UPDRS-III严重程度显著相关（r=-0.607, p<0.001），并经过多项稳健性检验和代码审计验证。同时报告了两个未能通过混杂检验的负结果，并强调了验证过程本身作为贡献的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统击键动力学研究通常提取汇总统计特征并训练分类器区分PD患者与健康对照组，这种黑箱方法缺乏对个体打字行为背后决策机制的可解释性。作者希望利用IRL从击键时序中恢复可解释的奖励函数，从而更深入地理解PD患者的运动控制策略变化。

Method: 将每次击键建模为关于打字速度的离散选择，应用逆强化学习从击键数据中恢复每位受试者的可解释奖励函数。初始四参数模型（速度、努力、平滑度、手交替成本）因特征共线性问题（r=1.000）被修正为可识别的三参数模型。在公开的neuroQWERTY MIT-CSXPD数据集（85名受试者，42名PD患者）上进行实验，并进行了对抗性代码审计以发现实现错误。

Result: 速度偏好权重与UPDRS-III严重程度呈显著负相关（r=-0.607, p<0.001, n=42），在两个子队列中独立复现，在九种敏感性配置下保持稳定，且超越原始打字速度的增量解释力（R²从0.194增至0.338, p=0.006）。一致性权重和手交替权重未能通过混杂检验，作为负结果报告。代码审计发现两个实现错误（会话边界污染、滚动窗口数据泄露），修复后主要结果基本不变。

Conclusion: IRL能够从击键动力学中恢复具有临床意义的可解释特征，速度偏好权重是稳健的PD严重程度生物标志物。论文强调验证过程本身（包括负结果报告、代码审计、敏感性分析等）与研究发现的数值结果同等重要，特别是在该领域文献报道准确率高度异质（汇总AUC 0.85, I²=94%）的背景下。

Abstract: Keystroke dynamics have been explored extensively as a passive digital biomarker for Parkinson's disease (PD), typically by extracting summary statistics from typing timing and training a classifier to discriminate PD from healthy controls. We instead apply inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) to keystroke data, modeling each keystroke as a discrete choice over typing speed and recovering, per subject, an interpretable reward function that explains their observed timing behavior. To our knowledge this is the first application of IRL to keystroke dynamics. On the public neuroQWERTY MIT-CSXPD dataset (85 subjects, 42 with PD), an initial four-parameter reward decomposition (speed, effort, smoothness, hand-alternation cost) was found to suffer severe feature collinearity between two terms ($r=1.000$ in typical contexts); we diagnose and correct this, yielding an identifiable three-parameter model. The recovered speed-preference weight correlates with UPDRS-III severity at $r=-0.607$ ($p<0.001$, $n=42$), replicates independently across two sub-cohorts, is stable across nine sensitivity configurations, and retains a statistically significant contribution beyond raw typing speed alone (incremental $R^2$ from 0.194 to 0.338, $p=0.006$). Two other recovered weights (consistency, hand-alternation) did not survive confound checks and are reported as negative results. We document two implementation bugs found during adversarial code review (session-boundary contamination, a rolling-window data leakage) and show the headline result is materially unchanged after fixing both. We discuss this result in the context of a literature where reported accuracies vary widely between studies (pooled AUC 0.85, I^2=94% in a 2022 meta-analysis), and argue that the validation process itself, not only the correlation coefficient, is part of the contribution.

</details>


### [249] [UC-Search: Risk-Aware Test-Time Search for Delayed Constrained Time-Series Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25274)
*Xibai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UC-Search，一种模型无关的测试时搜索框架，通过可行性自动机、不确定性量化与有限搜索，将任意时间序列模型转化为能够做出带延迟、风险调整的可行决策的系统。在多个控制与库存基准上显著优于CEM、MPPI和风险随机基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列模型通常仅以预测质量评分，但实际部署系统往往需要在不确定性和严格可行性约束下做出延迟决策。现有方法（如CEM、MPPI）在可行性与风险调整方面存在不足，需要一种通用方法将任意基座模型转化为风险感知的可行决策制定者。

Method: 提出UC-Search框架：基座模型输出预测/动作分数，可行性自动机前向滚动候选轨迹，有限束搜索返回风险调整后的可行轨迹的第一个动作。具体实现包括UC-Beam（束搜索）和UC-MCTS（UCT风格蒙特卡洛树搜索）。利用认知不确定性、偶然不确定性和传播不确定性作为路径风险项。推导了一个关于短视坍缩/分离的定理，刻画搜索何时退化为单步风险贪婪、何时延迟可行集耦合能产生非短视价值。

Result: 在9个系列族、33个系列的预声明延迟控制基准上，UC-Pareto在归一化阈值下优于验证集选出的CEM（+3.1675）、MPPI（+2.3328）和风险随机（+2.5038）；计算量匹配审计仍保持正值（+2.8466/+2.7418/+2.7429）。ETT/LTSF延迟库存验证支持相同计算前沿主张。在48系列M4标准周期性盘点缺货损失库存审计中，优于最强经典基库存控制（+13556.7547）、CEM（+64900.2207）和风险随机（+52881.6042），MPPI结果系列混杂。此外报告了FI-2010、官方预测适配器、SB3/FQI控制、方向/容量/干预检查和合成机制测试作为边界或机制证据。

Conclusion: UC-Search作为一种通用测试时搜索框架，能够有效结合任意基座模型、可行性约束和不确定性量化，在延迟决策场景中显著优于现有方法。理论分析和大量实证验证表明其在控制与库存管理任务上的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Time-series models are usually scored as forecasters, yet deployed systems often require delayed decisions under uncertainty and hard feasibility constraints. UC-Search is a model-agnostic test-time wrapper: a backbone emits forecasts or action scores, a feasibility automaton rolls candidate paths forward, and bounded search returns the first action of a risk-adjusted feasible trajectory. We instantiate UC-Beam and a UCT-style UC-MCTS diagnostic, using epistemic, aleatoric, and propagated uncertainty mainly as path-risk terms. A myopic-collapse/separation theorem states when search reduces to one-step risk-greedy and when delayed feasible-set coupling can create non-myopic value. Primary evidence comes from a predeclared public $9$-family, $33$-series delayed-control suite with six held-out starts per series: UC-Pareto is positive versus validation-selected CEM, MPPI, and risk-aware random at the normalized threshold ($+3.1675/+2.3328/+2.5038$), and remains positive in a compute-matched audit ($+2.8466/+2.7418/+2.7429$). ETT/LTSF delayed-inventory validation supports the same compute-frontier claim. A 48-series raw M4 standard periodic-review lost-sales inventory audit is positive versus the strongest classic base-stock control ($+13556.7547$), CEM ($+64900.2207$), and risk-random ($+52881.6042$), while MPPI remains family-mixed. FI-2010, official-forecast adapters, SB3/FQI controls, direction/capacity/intervention checks, and synthetic mechanism tests are reported as boundary or mechanism evidence rather than broad dominance claims.

</details>


### [250] [EPTS: Elastic Post-Training Sparsity for Efficient Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25285)
*Ke Xu,Jiaqi Wan,Wenhao Hu,Han Pu,Xiaoyun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出弹性后训练稀疏化框架EPTS，通过单次优化生成支持多种稀疏度配置的弹性模型，解决传统方法需为每种稀疏度单独优化的效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练稀疏化方法局限于单一稀疏度优化，每次适配新稀疏度都需要完整的重新优化过程，这种僵化范式严重阻碍了不同硬件场景下的灵活部署。

Method: 提出EPTS框架，包含两个核心机制：1) 多稀疏层级LoRA机制，实现从低稀疏组到高稀疏组的知识继承，缓解参数重构竞争；2) 多稀疏特征混合器，通过动态融合不同稀疏粒度的特征表示，增强模型对剪枝扰动的适应性。

Result: 在LLaMA和OPT系列模型上的实验表明，EPTS在保持与SparseGPT、Wanda等最先进方法竞争性能的同时，通过单次优化实现多场景部署，显著提升效率。

Conclusion: EPTS是一种统一的多稀疏度框架，通过单次优化即可产生支持多种稀疏度配置的弹性模型，有效克服了传统PTS方法的局限性，在性能和部署效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Post-Training Sparsity (PTS) has emerged as a crucial paradigm for compressing Large Language Models to facilitate efficient deployment on resource-constrained devices. However, existing PTS methodologies are typically confined to Single-Sparsity optimization, necessitating a separate, time-consuming optimization session for each specific sparsity level. This rigid paradigm significantly hinders flexible deployment across diverse hardware scenarios, as adapting to a new sparsity requirement mandates a complete re-optimization process. To address these limitations, we propose Elastic Post-Training Sparsity (EPTS), a unified Multi-Sparsity framework that produces a single elastic model capable of maintaining robust performance across diverse sparsity configurations through a one-shot optimization process. Specifically, we design a Multi-Sparsity Hierarchy LoRA (MS-HiLoRA) mechanism that facilitates knowledge inheritance from low- to high-sparsity groups, effectively mitigating the competition for parameter reconstruction. Furthermore, we introduce a Multi-Sparsity Feature Mixer (MSFM), which significantly enhances the model's adaptability to pruning perturbations by dynamically fusing feature representations of varying sparsity granularities. Extensive experiments on LLaMA and OPT families demonstrate that EPTS achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art methods like SparseGPT and Wanda, while offering significant efficiency gains by enabling multi-scenario deployment from a single optimization. our source code is available at https://github.com/xuke225/EPTS.

</details>


### [251] [Communicability-Inspired Positional Encoding (CIPE)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25293)
*Yipeng Zhang,Zhongtian Sun,Pietro Liò,Kelin Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于通信性（communicability）的位置编码方法CIPE，用于图Transformer。CIPE通过聚合所有长度路径的节点对信息，将全局多路径连通性转化为与注意力机制兼容的相似性几何结构。通过维度对齐技术将图规模依赖的表示映射到预设维度，实验表明CIPE在七个基准上平均提升结构无关Transformer 35.5%，并能持续改进结构偏置图Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位置编码（PEs）在非欧几里得图上设计有效编码仍具挑战性，理想的位置编码应诱导出与自注意力兼容的几何结构，使内积能够反映有意义的结构相关性，而不仅仅是描述图结构。

Method: 基于通信性（communicability）构建位置编码CIPE，通信性衡量节点对间所有长度路径的聚合贡献；CIPE的内积天然恢复通信性，将全局多路径连通性转化为注意力友好的相似性几何；引入维度对齐技术，将图规模依赖的CIPE表示映射到指定维度，同时保真地保持诱导的几何结构。

Result: 在七个基准上平均提升结构无关Transformer 35.5%，优于代表性PE方法；同时持续改进结构偏置图Transformer，而竞争性PE方法通常仅带来边际收益。

Conclusion: CIPE为注意力兼容的图位置编码提供了一个原理性框架。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) are essential for Transformers. Yet designing effective PEs for non-Euclidean graphs remains challenging. Such encodings should ideally induce an Attention-Compatible Geometry for self-attention: not merely describing graph structure, but defining a geometry whose inner products reflect meaningful structural relatedness. To realize this geometry, we propose Communicability-Inspired Positional Encoding (CIPE), built from communicability, a measure between pairs of nodes that aggregates contributions from paths of all lengths. By construction, CIPE inner products recover communicability, converting global multi-path connectivity into an attention-ready similarity geometry. For practical Transformer training, we introduce dimensionality alignment, mapping graph-size-dependent CIPE representations to prescribed dimensions while faithfully preserving the induced geometry. Empirically, CIPE improves structure-agnostic Transformers by 35.5% on average across seven benchmarks, outperforming representative PEs; it also consistently improves structure-biased graph Transformers, where competing PEs often yield only marginal benefits. These results position CIPE as a principled framework for attention-compatible graph positional encodings.

</details>


### [252] [Stagnant Neuron: Towards Understanding the Plasticity Loss in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Value Factorization Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25335)
*Zhengzhu Liu,Zeming Gao,Haoyuan Qin,Jiawei Hu,Junhao Wu,Miao Zhu,Haipeng Zhang,Chennan Ma,Siqi Shen,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MARL价值分解方法在任务迁移时塑性丧失问题，提出KNIFE方法，通过将停滞神经元替换为包含冻结知识神经元、重新初始化活跃神经元和补偿神经元的复合单元，在保持已有知识的同时恢复学习能力，在SMACv2等任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MARL价值分解方法在转移到新任务实例时会逐渐丧失塑性（plasticity），难以适应新环境。现有塑性注入方法对停滞神经元（stagnant neurons）无效。

Method: 提出KNIFE方法，直接针对停滞神经元：将每个停滞神经元替换为由三个组件组成的复合单元——冻结知识神经元（保留已获取知识）、重新初始化的活跃神经元（恢复学习能力）和补偿神经元（确保组合输出与原始匹配，维持已学习的合作知识）。

Result: 在SMACv2、捕食者-猎物和矩阵博弈等任务上的大量实验表明，KNIFE显著优于最先进的塑性注入方法。

Conclusion: KNIFE通过针对性处理停滞神经元，在恢复学习能力的同时保持已有知识，有效解决了MARL价值分解方法中的塑性丧失问题。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) value factorization methods can suffer from a loss of plasticity, gradually failing to adapt when transferring to new task instances. We trace this issue to stagnant neurons, units whose gradient updates become negligibly small relative to their weights, thereby hindering learning. While existing plasticity injection methods exist, they prove ineffective for such neurons. To address this, we propose Knowledge-retentive Neuron-level PlastIcity Focusing InjEction (KNIFE), a novel method that directly targets stagnant neurons. KNIFE replaces each stagnant neuron with a composite unit comprising three specialized components: a frozen knowledge neuron to preserve acquired knowledge, a re-initialized active neuron to restore learning capacity, and a compensation neuron to ensure the combined output matches the original, thus maintaining previous learned cooperation knowledge. Extensive experiments on SMACv2, predator-prey, and matrix games demonstrate that KNIFE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art plasticity injection methods.

</details>


### [253] [Lifelong In-Context Learning with Transformers Requires Parametric Forms of Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25342)
*Luke McDermott,Robert W. Heath,Rahul Parhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将上下文学习扩展到终身学习场景是AI智能体持续学习的实用方案，并系统分析了参数化注意力机制（如线性注意力、状态空间模型、快速权重编程器等）相比非参数化注意力（如softmax注意力）的优势与局限，指出其因记忆容量有限或在线更新成本高而尚未实现真正的终身学习，并提出了一系列开放性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的二次注意力复杂度无法处理任意长序列，而终身连续学习是实现类人智能的关键障碍。作者认为将上下文学习扩展到终身设置是AI智能体持续学习的实用解决方案。

Method: 系统分类和对比了参数化注意力机制（线性注意力、状态空间模型、快速权重编程器、测试时训练层）与非参数化注意力机制（softmax注意力），分析它们在终身学习场景中的适用性。

Result: 参数化注意力可以通过在线可训练的神经网络替代不断增长的键值缓存，保持恒定内存占用，但当前仍因记忆容量有限或在线更新成本高昂而未能实现真正的终身学习。

Conclusion: 提出了引导该领域向长视界智能体发展的开放性问题和新见解。

Abstract: Lifelong continual learning remains an obstacle on the path to human-like intelligence. Modern transformers show sparks of intelligence with in-context learning. The quadratic nature of attention, however, prohibits transformers from performing this process on arbitrarily long sequences. In this work, we argue that extending in-context learning to lifelong settings is a practical solution for continual learning in AI agents. In particular, we argue that \emph{parametric forms of attention} are needed to understand a lifetime of context with transformers on a fixed hardware budget. These attention mechanisms learn the relationship between keys and their associated values at test-time with parametric regression. Our generalization of parametric approaches (linear attention, state-space models, fast weight programmers, and test-time training layers) contrasts with nonparametric counterparts like softmax attention. They replace the ever-growing key-value cache with an online-trainable neural network, maintaining a constant memory footprint. We highlight how parametric attention currently fall short of lifelong learning due to limited memory capacity or costly online updates. To address these issues, we pose a set of open questions with novel insights to guide the field toward long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [254] [Compositional Behavioral Semantics for State Abstraction in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25357)
*Yivan Zhang,Ziyan Luo,Manuel Baltieri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，用于定义和分析强化学习中的行为结构，该框架基于局部的单步系统动力学描述，以组合方式指定行为语义，并证明了行为结构可以在抽象系统与具体系统之间安全迁移，同时展示了如何从逻辑行为语义构建具有可靠性保证的量化度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 状态抽象在强化学习中至关重要，但缺乏一个通用原则来确定哪些行为结构（如值函数、不变量、对偶仿真关系、行为度量等）能在状态抽象下被保质地保留。现有研究覆盖了多种行为结构，但缺乏统一的原理性框架。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，通过局部的、单步的系统动力学描述，以组合方式指定行为语义。基于该框架，建立行为结构在抽象系统与具体系统之间安全迁移的理论结果，并展示如何从逻辑行为语义构建具有可靠性保证的量化度量。

Result: 建立了行为结构在抽象与具体系统间安全迁移的理论保证；展示了从逻辑行为语义构建量化度量并保证其可靠性；为强化学习中状态抽象下的行为推理提供了原理性基础。

Conclusion: 该统一框架为强化学习中广泛的行为结构提供了可复用的定义和证明原理，填补了状态抽象下行为结构保质性的一般性理论空白。

Abstract: State abstraction plays a key role in scaling reinforcement learning to complex but structured systems. In studying such systems, a wide range of behavioral structures have been studied in reinforcement learning, including value functions, invariants, bisimulation relations, and behavioral metrics. However, a general principle for determining what structures are provably preserved under state abstraction is still lacking. In this paper, we present a unified framework for defining and analyzing behavioral structures in reinforcement learning. Our framework provides a compositional way to specify behavioral semantics based on local, one-step descriptions of system dynamics. Using this framework, we establish results showing how behavioral structures can be safely transferred between abstract and concrete systems. We further show how to construct quantitative metrics from logical behavioral semantics with soundness guarantees. Together, these results provide a principled foundation for reasoning about behaviors under state abstraction in reinforcement learning and offer reusable definition and proof principles for a broad class of behavioral structures in reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [255] [FactorLibrary: From Polynomials to Circuits via Recursive Subgoals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25394)
*Rohan Pandey,Michael Ruofan Zeng,Weikun K. Zhang,Kaijie Jin,Naomi Morato,Archit Ganapule,Bhaumik Mehta,Jarod Alper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将有限域上多项式最小算术电路问题转化为强化学习问题，引入FactorLibrary存储可分解子表达式作为可重用子目标，训练了三种强化学习智能体，其中PPO+MCTS自顶向下智能体以91.8%的成功率找到了复杂度≤8的认证最优电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找有限域上多项式的最小算术电路是代数复杂度理论中的组合难题，现有方法难以应对快速增长的组合搜索空间。

Method: 将问题形式化为自底向上和自顶向下两个方向的强化学习问题；引入FactorLibrary存储可因子化的子表达式作为跨训练episode的可重用子目标；训练了Gumbel-PPO-MCTS自底向上智能体和PPO+MCTS、SAC两种自顶向下智能体。

Result: PPO+MCTS自顶向下智能体表现最稳定，以91.8%的成功率找到复杂度≤8的认证最优电路。

Conclusion: 强化学习结合因子库存储可重用子目标的方法在有限域多项式最小算术电路搜索任务上有效，自顶向下策略优于自底向上策略。

Abstract: Finding minimal arithmetic circuits for polynomials over finite fields is a combinatorially hard problem central to algebraic complexity theory. We formulate it as a reinforcement learning problem in two directions, bottom-up and top-down. To address the challenge of a fast-growing combinatorial search space, we introduce FactorLibrary, which stores factorizable subexpressions that serve as reusable subgoals across training episodes. We trained a bottom-up agent with Gumbel-PPO-MCTS and two top-down agents with PPO+MCTS and SAC. The PPO+MCTS top-down agent exhibited the most stable performance, finding certified optimal circuits up to complexity $8$ with a success rate of $91.8\%$.

</details>


### [256] [DFMU: Data-Frugal Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25410)
*Sajith U,Prateek Keserwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种数据节俭的机器遗忘方法（DFMU），仅需单次前向和后向传播计算模型各模块的重要性分数，基于知识保持剪枝实现快速收敛，在仅用13%数据样本的情况下比现有方法提升40%的保留准确率，并平均加快88%的处理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘领域面临的核心挑战是移除预训练元素（概念、属性、实体、类别）时必然导致模型在剩余元素上的性能下降，而现有方法依赖计算密集型重训练。本文旨在降低计算需求，实现更快的保留数据集准确率收敛，且无需对预训练模型进行大量重训练。

Method: 提出Data-Frugal Machine Unlearning (DFMU)方法，通过单次前向和后向传播计算模型中各计算块的重要性分数，该分数基于知识保持剪枝（knowledge preserving pruning）计算，能够实现更快的收敛速度，且所需数据量远少于现有方法。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上，与现有最优方法相比，DFMU仅使用13%的数据样本即可实现40%更高的保留准确率，并平均达到88%更快的遗忘处理速度。

Conclusion: DFMU方法通过知识保持剪枝的重要性评分机制，显著降低了机器遗忘的计算开销和数据需求，在保持模型性能的同时实现了高效的遗忘操作，为机器遗忘领域提供了一种轻量级且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is an emerging domain that ensures the safe removal of elements (includes concepts, attributes, entity and class) from the trained model along with least drop in model performance. The domain of machine unlearning brings its own indigenous challenges since the removal of pre-trained elements from model will always degrade the model performance on remaining elements. The existing methods basically rely on retraining for removal of elements from the pre-trained model, which is compute extensive. In this work, we propose a machine unlearning method which helps to reduce the computational requirement for faster retain-dataset accuracy convergence which also does not require extensive retraining of the pre-trained model. The proposed method, Data-Frugal Machine Unlearning (DFMU) requires only a single forward and backward pass for computing the importance score of various computational blocks of a model. The importance score computation is based on knowledge preserving pruning which helps to converge faster and requires far less data as compared to the existing methods. Experimentally, it achieves 40% more retain-accuracy with just 13% of data samples in comparison with SOTA method on various public datasets and also averages 88% faster processing time for forgetting a given class.

</details>


### [257] [Interpretable Concept-Guided Polynomial Tabular Kolmogorov-Arnold Network for EEG-Based Mild Cognitive Impairment Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25434)
*Yosef Bernardus Wirian,Qiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CPTabKAN框架，通过概念引导的表征学习、多项式交互扩展和非线性表格分类，在睡眠脑电数据上实现轻度认知障碍的早期检测，F1-score达0.9038，显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCI检测方法要么依赖手工特征丢弃神经生理学结构，要么使用深度学习牺牲可解释性，尚无统一框架同时整合生理概念编码器、跨概念交互建模和非线性表格分类。

Method: CPTabKAN包含三个核心组件：(1) 将EEG特征映射到十类生理概念表征；(2) 采用二次多项式变换暴露一阶和二阶跨概念交互；(3) 使用傅里叶参数化的TabKAN分类器学习非线性决策边界。

Result: 在SOF队列372名受试者上，CPTabKAN加权F1-score达0.9038(SD 0.034)，比GradientBoosting高5.65个百分点(p=0.043)；消融实验验证各组件独立贡献；概念重要性分析揭示PSD、多尺度熵等主导一阶权重，LZW复杂度等跨概念交互超越所有一阶分数。

Conclusion: 概念结构化、交互感知的表格学习能够产生生理学上连贯的推理，支持临床信任，为可解释的MCI脑电检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Early and scalable detection of mild cognitive impairment (MCI) remains an unresolved clinical challenge. Existing EEG-based screening approaches are constrained by handcrafted feature pipelines that discard neurophysiologically meaningful domain structure and deep learning classifiers that sacrifice interpretability for performance. No existing work unifies physiologically organized concept encoders, cross-concept interaction modeling, and nonlinear tabular classification in a sleep EEG-based MCI detection framework. This study proposes Concept-guided Polynomial-transformed Tabular learning using Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (CPTabKAN), which maps heterogeneous EEG-derived features into domain-informed concept representations, expands them via degree-2 polynomial transformation to expose first- and second-order interactions, and applies a Fourier-parameterized TabKAN classifier to learn nonlinear decision boundaries. CPTabKAN was evaluated on the Study of Osteoporotic Fractures cohort (372 subjects, overnight polysomnography), using 1,379 features organized into ten physiologically motivated concept groups. Under 10-fold cross-validation, CPTabKAN-Second Order achieved a weighted F1-score of 0.9038 (SD 0.034), outperforming GradientBoosting by 5.65 percentage points (t(9)=1.934,p=0.043, one-sided paired test), with advantages persisting under SMOTE-based balancing. Ablation analysis confirmed independent contributions from each component. Concept importance analysis revealed that power spectral density, multi-scale entropy, and Hjorth parameters dominated first-order weights, while cross-concept interactions involving Lempel-Ziv-Welch complexity, statistics, demographics, and slow oscillations exceeded all first-order scores. These results demonstrate that concept-structured, interaction-aware tabular learning surfaces physiologically coherent reasoning, supporting clinical trust.

</details>


### [258] [TopoCast: A Topological Fidelity Framework for Evaluating Transformer-Based Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25439)
*Sandeepa Weerasekara,Sandareka Wickramanayake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TopoCast框架，利用拓扑数据分析（Takens延迟嵌入+持续同调）从相空间重构角度评估时间序列预测的结构保真度，并引入局部化拓扑保真度分数（LTFS）来捕捉传统指标（如MSE）无法检测的相位偏移、频率失真等结构退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测评估过度依赖MSE等逐点误差指标，这些指标仅衡量数值精度，却忽略了预测信号的循环动态、振荡行为、相位对齐等结构特性，导致过平滑、相位偏移等结构退化的预测仍能获得较好的误差评分。

Method: (1) 利用Takens延迟嵌入重构预测序列与真实序列的相空间表示；(2) 应用持续同调提取相空间的拓扑特征，构建持久性图；(3) 从持久性图中导出四种互补的拓扑保真度度量，聚合为拓扑保真度分数（TFS）；(4) 提出主导周期重叠度量，将持久拓扑特征映射到时域，评估主导振荡模式是否发生在正确的时间点；(5) 将TFS与主导周期重叠结合，得到局部化拓扑保真度分数（LTFS）。

Result: 在5种Transformer架构和3个真实基准数据集上的实验表明，预测误差相似的模型可能表现出显著不同的结构保真度轮廓，揭示了传统评估方法忽略的失败模式。

Conclusion: TopoCast能够有效检测传统逐点误差指标无法捕捉的结构退化问题，证明了拓扑感知的预测评估具有重要价值。

Abstract: Deep learning-based models have achieved state-of-the-art performance in Time Series Forecasting (TSF), yet their evaluation remains dominated by pointwise error metrics such as Mean Squared Error (MSE), which quantify numerical accuracy but overlook structural properties of the forecast signal, including recurrent dynamics, oscillatory behavior, and phase alignment. As a result, forecasts exhibiting over-smoothing, phase shifts, or frequency distortions may achieve favorable error scores despite substantial structural degradation. To address this limitation, we propose TopoCast, a topology-driven framework for evaluating structural fidelity in TSF. TopoCast reconstructs phase-space representations of forecast and ground-truth sequences using Takens delay embedding and applies persistent homology to characterize their intrinsic dynamics. We derive four complementary topological fidelity measures from persistence diagrams and aggregate them into a Topological Fidelity Score (TFS). We further introduce dominant cycle overlap, a novel metric that maps persistent topological features to the temporal domain to assess whether dominant oscillatory patterns occur at the correct time points. Combined with TFS, this yields the Localized Topological Fidelity Score (LTFS), a phase-aware measure that captures temporal localization errors invisible to existing evaluation metrics. Experiments on five Transformer architectures across three real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate that models with similar forecasting errors can exhibit markedly different structural fidelity profiles, revealing failure modes overlooked by conventional evaluation and highlighting the value of topology-aware forecast assessment.

</details>


### [259] [The Interplay of Harness Design and Post-Training in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25447)
*Kyungmin Kim,Youngbin Choi,Seoyeon Lee,Suhyeon Jun,Dongwoo Kim,Sangdon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了工具集成LLM代理的"脚手架"（harness）设计对后训练的影响，扩展了ALFWorld以将脚手架作为可控设计维度，并支持任务与工具环境变化下的评估。实验表明，脚手架感知的后训练不仅能提升分布内性能，还能使代理鲁棒地适应分布外场景，而设计不佳的脚手架在工具环境变化下会导致性能大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理后训练方法通常将脚手架（工具暴露方式、描述及辅助信息）视为固定工程细节，忽略了其设计对后训练的影响；且后训练算法假设静态环境，无法应对部署后的任务与工具环境变化。

Method: 扩展ALFWorld以将脚手架作为可控设计维度，并支持任务与工具环境变化的评估；系统分析脚手架设计在分布内和分布外设置下对后训练的影响。

Result: 脚手架感知的后训练在分布内性能有提升，且在分布外场景下表现出鲁棒适应性；而低设计成本的脚手架在强工具环境变化下后训练性能大幅下降，凸显了脚手架感知后训练的重要性。

Conclusion: 脚手架设计是影响LLM代理后训练效果的关键因素，脚手架感知的后训练范式对于提升代理在动态环境中的鲁棒性至关重要。

Abstract: Tool-integrated LLM agents are often wrapped within a harness: the scaffolding that determines which tools are exposed, how they are described, and what auxiliary information accompanies each per-step observation. While agents are routinely post-trained, this scaffolding is typically treated as a fixed engineering detail, with design effort limited to the training-free regime. Moreover, existing post-training algorithms assume a static environment, even though tool environments and tasks often shift upon deployment. To address this gap, we extend $\texttt{ALFWorld}$ (i) to treat the harness as a controllable design dimension and (ii) to support evaluation under task and tool environment shifts. Building on this, we systematically analyze how the harness design influences post-training in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution (OOD) settings. We empirically show that harness-aware post-training not only improves in-distribution performance but also enables agents to robustly adapt to OOD settings. Under a harness with minimal design effort, post-training suffers a drastic performance drop under stronger tool environment shifts, further highlighting the importance of harness-aware post-training under such shifts.

</details>


### [260] [The Generalization Spectrum: A Chromatographic Approach to Evaluating Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25450)
*Jinghan Zhang,Zerui Cheng,Shiqi Chen,Ge Zhang,Wenhao Huang,Jiashuo Liu,Junxian He,Tianle Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“泛化频谱”(Generalization Spectrum)评估框架，通过为每个训练样本构建由近到远的测试变体，揭示算法从单个样本中学习到的知识能迁移多远，而不仅仅是最终聚合分数。在竞技编程任务上验证发现：RL比SFT更高效地将记忆转化为近迁移，ICL迁移强但依赖对应关系；局部增益不必然扩大泛化半径，某些方法甚至可能牺牲远迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统评估将学习简化为单一聚合分数，掩盖了从特定样本学到的知识能泛化到其他样本的程度这一根本问题。人类认知中的类比学习体现了这种逐样本泛化能力，但标准基准无法捕捉。

Method: 提出Generalization Spectrum框架，为每个训练示例构建按迁移距离排列的受控测试变体（精确回忆→跨语言实现迁移→跨上下文叙事重构迁移→类别匹配域内问题→非配对基线），并在竞技编程任务上通过选择-合成管线实例化该框架，对比SFT、RL、ICL三种学习范式及其变体。

Result: RL比SFT基线更高效地将记忆转化为近迁移；ICL迁移能力强但依赖样本-测试对应关系；局部增益不保证扩大泛化半径——抽象和提示主要提升局部迁移，RFT比参考SFT保留更强的远迁移尾，自蒸馏或提示辅助RL可能减少远迁移。

Conclusion: 泛化频谱揭示了传统聚合指标隐藏的逐样本泛化维度，可用于诊断学习方法在知识迁移距离上的真实表现，帮助理解不同训练范式在泛化深度上的差异与权衡。

Abstract: Traditional evaluations measure a learning algorithm's final performance on an i.i.d. test set, reducing learning to a single aggregate score. This approach obscures a fundamental question: to what extent does learning from a specific example generalize to others? Such per-sample generalization, akin to learning by analogy in human cognition, captures how far the knowledge extracted from one example can transfer, yet remains invisible to standard benchmarks. We introduce the Generalization Spectrum, an evaluation framework designed to expose this hidden dimension. For each training example, we construct a controlled suite of test variants arranged by increasing transfer distance, from exact recall to implementation transfer across languages, context transfer under complete narrative re-framing, category-matched in-domain problems, and an unpaired baseline. By tracking performance across these distances, we reveal not just whether an algorithm learns, but how far that learning extends. We instantiate this framework on competitive programming, using a selection-and-synthesis pipeline seeded with recent problems to mitigate contamination. We first compare three canonical learning paradigms under matched memorization. RL converts memorization into near-transfer more efficiently than SFT-family baselines, while ICL exhibits strong but correspondence-dependent transfer. We then use the Spectrum to diagnose within-family variants. The resulting profiles show that local gains need not expand the generalization radius: abstractions and hints mainly lift local transfer, RFT preserves a stronger far-transfer tail than reference SFT, and self-distillation or hint-assisted RL can reduce far transfer even when local transfer or optimization improves.

</details>


### [261] [Learning with a Single Rollout via Monte Carlo Pass@k Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25451)
*Fengdi Che,Yang Liu,Lei Yu,Meng Cao,Tong Che,Rupam Mahmood,Dale Schuurmans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SR-PPO方法，通过训练单次轨迹的token级信用评估器（使用Pass@k作为学习信号），避免了传统RL中多次采样导致的轨迹发散和高计算成本问题，在数学推理基准上取得稳定改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的RL方法（如GRPO）在语言模型训练中存在两个关键问题：(1) 重复采样导致轨迹发散，计算成本高昂；(2) 结果奖励过于稀疏，难以进行token级别的信用分配。本文旨在缓解这些问题。

Method: 提出SR-PPO（单轨迹PPO），核心是训练一个校准的token级信用评估器（critic）。该评估器使用每个提示的单个rollout的蒙特卡洛结果，预测前缀的Pass@k成功概率。通过增大k值，Pass@k趋近于可达性指示器，可在显式状态图中以O(|V|+|E|)时间计算，无需采样对比轨迹。

Result: SR-PPO在数学推理基准HMMT26和AIME24上展示了稳定的学习动态和一致的Pass@128成功率提升。

Conclusion: 通过Pass@k作为信用分配信号的SR-PPO，有效降低了RL训练的计算成本，同时提供了更精细的token级信用分配能力，为语言模型强化学习提供了一种高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Estimating token-level advantages in reinforcement learning (RL) for language models remains challenging because scaling up episodic experience collection is expensive. The difficulty intensifies for baseline advantage estimation methods, where repeated sampling causes trajectories to diverge into substantially different reasoning prefixes. In this context, RL algorithms such as GRPO prove limited: an outcome reward is too sparse to be attributed to specific actions like intermediate steps, and comparisons across sampled traces are non-trivial because they are heterogeneous. To mitigate both the computational cost of repeated sampling and the difficulty of credit assignment, we study single-rollout proximal policy optimization (SR-PPO) featuring token-level credit assignment in RL for language models. Instead of estimating advantages by normalizing episodic returns within the candidate group, we train a calibrated token-level credit critic using Monte Carlo outcomes from one rollout per prompt. Specifically, we use the critic to predict the Pass@k success probability at the prompt prefix, which is derived from a Pass@1 attempt. This choice yields a more selective learning signal than Pass@1: it discounts easily solved prefixes while prioritizing hard ones whose success probability remains marginal. We show that as $k$ increases, Pass@k converges to a reachability indicator, reflecting whether a prefix can lead to at least one successful continuation. In an explicit state graph, the limit ($k \rightarrow \infty$) can be computed in $O(|V|+|E|)$ time, offering a promising surrogate for direct credit assignment without the need to sample contrastive traces. As an initial validation, SR-PPO exhibits stable learning dynamics, along with consistent gains in Pass@128 success rates on mathematical reasoning benchmarks such as HMMT26 and AIME24.

</details>


### [262] [Towards Robust EEG Decoding Based on Riemannian Self-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25456)
*Shaocheng Jin,Tao Zhou,Rui Wang,Ziheng Chen,Xiaoqing Luo,Xiaojun Wu,Josef Kittler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Bures-Wasserstein度量的黎曼自注意力网络（GBWAtt），用于脑电图（EEG）解码。该方法通过可学习的广义Bures-Wasserstein度量捕捉SPD矩阵间的非线性关系，解决了现有SPD学习方法无法显式建模局部关系、对病态矩阵敏感等问题，在三个EEG基准数据集上验证了鲁棒性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SPD学习的EEG解码方法通常采用基础网络架构，无法显式捕获EEG信号之间的局部关系；且大多局限于仿射不变度量（AIM），该度量对SPD矩阵呈二次依赖且难以处理病态SPD矩阵，限制了网络性能。

Method: 提出基于Bures-Wasserstein度量（BWM）的黎曼自注意力网络，并扩展为可学习版本GBWAtt。利用BWM对SPD矩阵的线性依赖特性及其在病态条件下的优越性能，结合功率变形的广义Bures-Wasserstein度量以更细致地刻画SPD流形的几何结构。

Result: 在三个EEG基准数据集上的实验验证了所提出方法的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: GBWAtt通过引入BWM和其可学习的广义变体，克服了现有黎曼流形方法在EEG解码中的局限，为低信噪比EEG信号的高效解码提供了新思路。

Abstract: Brain-Computer Interface (BCI) based on electroencephalography (EEG) enables direct interaction between the brain and external environments and has significant applications in assistive technologies, medical rehabilitation, and entertainment. Recently, EEG decoding methods based on Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) learning have demonstrated superior performance. However, these methods typically employ basic network architectures and do not explicitly capture local relationships between EEG signals. This limitation is problematic for EEG signals due to their inherently low Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR). Moreover, most existing Riemannian manifold-based methods are restricted to specific metrics. The most widely used is the Affine-Invariant Metric (AIM). However, it has a quadratic dependency on the SPD matrices and cannot handle ill-conditioned SPD matrices, which hinders the effectiveness of networks. In contrast, the Bures-Wasserstein Metric (BWM) exhibits linear dependence on SPD matrices and demonstrates superior performance for ill conditioning. To overcome these challenges, we propose a Riemannian self-attention network based on the BWM. Additionally, the recently introduced power-deformed generalized Bures-Wasserstein metric reveals a nonlinear relationship between SPD matrices and matrix power deformation. This metric provides a more nuanced representation of the geometric structure of the SPD manifold. Consequently, we extend our model to a learnable version. For simplicity, we refer to it as GBWAtt. Experimental results on three EEG benchmarking datasets validate the robustness and effectiveness of our proposed method. The code is available at https://github.com/jissc/GBWAtt.

</details>


### [263] [Distill on a Diet: Efficient Knowledge Distillation via Learnable Data Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25488)
*Yifan Wu,Yiqi Wang,Xichen Ye,Wenjing Yan,Xiaoqiang Li,Cheng Jin,Xiangyu Yue,Weizhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IF-Beta框架，通过影响函数和可学习的Beta分布采样策略，高效地进行知识蒸馏中的数据剪枝，在减少数据和计算量的同时提升学生模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据剪枝方法不适用于知识蒸馏场景：要么需要重新训练获取训练动态带来巨大开销，要么依赖启发式规则无法捕捉KD的实际需求。

Method: 提出IF-Beta框架，核心包括：(1) 利用影响函数作为KD场景下样本影响的有效估计器；(2) 用Beta分布参数化可学习的采样策略，利用其灵活的两参数族适应不同剪枝率；(3) 双层优化：内环在教师特征空间中进行KD目标对齐的快速代理训练，外环更新策略参数以最大化蒸馏性能。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100和ImageNet上的大量实验表明，IF-Beta在各种剪枝率下持续优于其他基线方法。显著地，使用更少数据和计算的学生模型性能可超越在完整数据集上蒸馏的学生模型。

Conclusion: IF-Beta通过影响函数与可学习采样策略的结合，实现了计算高效且与KD目标对齐的数据剪枝，验证了在资源受限环境下通过数据剪枝获得更好学生模型的可行性。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) is widely used to obtain compact models for efficient inference in resource-constrained environments. Yet the computational overhead of the distillation process itself is often overlooked, raising the question of whether a better student model can be obtained with less data and less compute via data pruning. However, existing data pruning methods are not designed for KD: some introduce substantial overhead, such as obtaining training dynamics through retraining, while others rely on heuristic selection rules that fail to capture what KD actually requires, often resulting in suboptimal subsets. To address these issues, we propose IF-Beta, an efficient data pruning framework that combines influence functions with a learnable sampling policy. Empirically, we first demonstrate that influence functions can serve as an effective and efficient estimator of sample impact in KD settings, where only a pretrained teacher is available. Building on this, our sampling policy is specifically parameterized by a Beta distribution, whose highly flexible two-parameter family allows the policy to adapt to diverse pruning regimes rather than being tied to fixed heuristic forms. Next, we formulate KD pruning as optimizing this policy through a bilevel objective, where the inner loop operates in the teacher feature space with a KD-aligned objective, enabling fast proxy training, while the outer loop updates the policy parameters to maximize distillation performance. This design ensures that IF-Beta is both computationally efficient and inherently aligned with the goals of KD. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10/100 and ImageNet show that IF-Beta consistently outperforms other baselines across a wide range of pruning ratios. Remarkably, IF-Beta enables students trained on less data and less compute to surpass the performance of students distilled on the full dataset.

</details>


### [264] [Low Variance Trust Region Optimization with Independent Actors and Sequential Updates in Cooperative Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25526)
*Bang Giang Le,Viet Cuong Ta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多智能体强化学习中序贯更新导致联合优势函数方差过高的问题，本文提出一种裁剪目标函数来控制优势波动上界，实现了次线性收敛到ε-纳什均衡，并在多个基准测试中取得优于基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体独立actor序贯更新框架中，更新每个智能体策略后需重新估计联合优势函数，尽管使用了重要性采样，但更新后的优势函数存在指数级高方差问题，导致收敛不稳定。

Method: 1) 从经验与理论两个角度分析了序贯更新中优势函数的高方差问题；2) 提出一个裁剪目标函数来控制优势波动的上界；3) 基于该目标函数推导出两种新的实用算法；4) 提供了从任意策略到ε-纳什均衡的次线性收敛单调界。

Result: 在三个主流多智能体强化学习基准测试中，所提方法在多数环境下优于基线方法，同时展现出稳定的收敛特性和低方差优势估计。

Conclusion: 提出了一种通过裁剪目标函数降低序贯更新中优势函数方差的有效方法，在理论和实践上均证明了其优越性，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning assumes each agent shares the same reward function and can be trained effectively using the Trust Region framework of single-agent. Instead of relying on other agents' actions, the independent actors setting considers each agent to act based only on its local information, thus having more flexible applications. However, in the sequential update framework, it is required to re-estimate the joint advantage function after each individual agent's policy step. Despite the practical success of importance sampling, the updated advantage function suffers from exponentially high variance problems, which likely result in unstable convergence. In this work, we first analyze the high variance advantage both empirically and theoretically. To overcome this limitation, we introduce a clipping objective to control the upper bounds of the advantage fluctuation in sequential updates. With the proposed objective, we provide a monotonic bound with sub-linear convergence to $ε$-Nash Equilibria. We further derive two new practical algorithms using our clipping objective. The experiment results on three popular multi-agent reinforcement learning benchmarks show that our proposed method outperforms the tested baselines in most environments. By carefully analyzing different training settings, our proposed method is highlighted with both stable convergence properties and the desired low advantage variance estimation. For reproducibility purposes, our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/giangbang/Low-Variance-Trust-Region-MARL.

</details>


### [265] [Beyond One-Size-Fits-All: Diagnosis-Driven Online Reinforcement Learning with Offline Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25527)
*Guozheng Ma,Lu Li,Zilin Wang,Pierre-Luc Bacon,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出，在线强化学习应放弃追求通用解决方案的基准驱动范式，转向基于诊断的张力管理范式，根据部署特定证据灵活调整智能体与离线先验之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在线RL智能体日益依赖离线知识（从离线数据集到基础模型、生成式世界模型），但先验有效性随部署环境和训练过程动态变化，基准驱动范式无法提供普适最优解，导致实际部署中缺乏指导。

Method: 构建了刻画先验通过三种功能角色重塑在线优化的框架；设计控制实验展示"帮助-伤害"反转现象；跨领域（基础模型后训练、具身智能）验证；对五种实质性反驳进行讨论。

Result: 证明了在不同部署条件下，同一先验可能从帮助转向伤害，表明单一方法无法通用最优；跨领域证据支持诊断驱动方法的必要性。

Conclusion: 该领域应从追求通用解决方案转向基于诊断的张力管理，依据部署特定证据动态调整智能体与先验的关系，实现灵活且自适应的部署。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) agents increasingly depend on knowledge acquired offline to achieve practical efficiency. Originally studied in offline-to-online RL, this paradigm now spans foundation model post-training and embodied intelligence, with prior types expanding from offline datasets and pre-trained policies to increasingly diverse knowledge sources such as multimodal foundation models and generative world models. Offline priors have become central to how deep RL is developed and deployed. However, this reliance introduces a challenge that the prevailing benchmark-driven paradigm cannot resolve: because prior validity varies across deployments and shifts during training, no single approach to managing it is universally optimal, and benchmark rankings offer limited guidance for real-world deployments. Rather than pursuing universal solutions, we argue that the field should shift to diagnosis-driven tension management, in which deployment-specific evidence guides how the learner relates to its priors throughout training, enabling both flexible and adaptive deployment. We support this position with a framework characterizing how priors reshape online optimization through three functional roles, controlled experiments demonstrating help-or-hurt reversals, cross-domain evidence from foundation model post-training to embodied intelligence, and engagement with five substantive counterarguments.

</details>


### [266] [Low-Complexity Policy Tessellations in Structured Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25593)
*Fredy Pokou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究结构化MDP中最优策略的几何特性，提出基于边界的策略近似方法，通过直接学习策略区域来替代高维值函数近似，实验证明该方法在多个指标上优于传统强化学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统近似动态规划和强化学习方法通常聚焦于近似高维值函数，但忽略了最优策略本身可能具有更简单的决策几何结构。作者希望通过利用最优策略的几何特性（决策区域的简单划分）来设计更高效的策略学习方法。

Method: 提出基于边界（boundary-based）的策略近似方法，直接学习策略区域（policy regions）而非近似值函数。通过策略损失分解（policy-loss decomposition）将性能退化与动作边际（action margins）关联起来，并解释为何误差集中在无差异边界（indifference boundaries）附近。

Result: 在库存控制（inventory control）和队列准入（queue admission）实验中，该方法相比强化学习基线取得了更低的策略误差、更小的值函数差距、更快的误差衰减速度和更好的稳定性。

Conclusion: 最优策略具有比值函数更简单的几何结构（决策区域的划分），直接学习策略边界是一种高效且稳定的替代方案。

Abstract: We study optimal-policy geometry in structured Markov decision processes. While approximate dynamic programming and reinforcement learning typically approximate high-dimensional value functions, we show that optimal policies induce simpler decision tessellations. We propose boundary-based policy approximations that learn policy regions directly. A policy-loss decomposition links performance degradation to action margins and explains why errors concentrate near indifference boundaries. Inventory control and queue admission experiments show lower policy error, smaller value gaps, faster error decay, and stability than reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [267] [Learning Subset-Shared Invariances for Domain Generalization with Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25665)
*Tien-Hung Nguyen,Tien-Dat Tran,M. -Duong Nguyen,Kok-Seng Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出子集共享不变性（subset-shared invariance），通过混合专家架构实现领域子集内的选择性对齐，以替代传统全局不变性约束，在DomainBed基准上提升了域泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统域泛化方法强制所有源域共享全局不变表示，但研究发现随着域数量增加，这种约束会过度限制表示空间，丢弃了仅在部分域间可迁移的预测性因子。

Method: 采用混合专家（MoE）架构，每个专家专门对齐特定域子集；设计路由机制根据输入动态组合子集不变组件；提出训练目标以促进选择性对齐、置信且平衡的路由以及多样化的专家专业化。

Result: 在DomainBed基准上，该方法在域外泛化性能上优于全局不变性方法，且在领域异质性增加时表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 域泛化应超越全局单一不变性约束，转而通过域子集间的部分共享结构来建模不变性。

Abstract: Domain generalization (DG) aims to learn a model from one or more source domains that generalizes to an unseen target domain without accessing target data during training. A common approach enforces invariance of representations across all source domains, assuming predictive structure is globally shared. However, we demonstrate that enforcing invariance across more domains gradually restricts the feasible representation space, discarding transferable predictive factors that are not universally shared. To address this limitation, we propose subset-shared invariance, where predictive structure is assumed stable only within domain subsets. We implement this principle with a mixture-of-experts architecture, where each expert aligns the specific domains it serves and a routing mechanism composes subset-invariant components for prediction. This creates a routing-conditioned invariance, jointly learned with the representation. To facilitate effective decomposition, we develop training objectives that encourage selective alignment, confident and balanced routing, and diverse expert specialization. Experiments on DomainBed benchmarks demonstrate improved out-of-domain generalization and greater robustness under increasing domain heterogeneity. Our results suggest that DG should move beyond enforcing a single global invariance and instead model invariance through partially shared structure across domain subsets.

</details>


### [268] [Memory-Efficient Policy Libraries with Low-Rank Adaptation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25700)
*Samuel Valland Lyngset,Tor Viljen Raanaas,Gard Sveipe,Eirik Møller Nilsen,Jim Torresen,Kai Olav Ellefsen,Tobias Lømo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将参数高效微调方法LoRA应用于机器人强化学习的多任务策略库中，在几乎不影响任务成功率的前提下，实现了20-160倍的显存压缩和90-95%的存储空间节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索参数高效微调（PEFT）方法如LoRA能否从大语言模型领域迁移到机器人强化学习中，以解决多任务策略库场景下的内存和计算效率问题。

Method: 基于PPO算法，使用LoRA对基线模型进行多任务微调，构建专家策略库，并与全参数微调进行对比实验。

Result: LoRA可将内存使用量减少20-160倍，部署10-50个专业化策略时节省90-95%的存储空间，且任务成功率与全参数微调无显著差异。

Conclusion: LoRA可以有效地从NLP领域迁移到机器人RL领域，在显著降低存储和内存开销的同时，保持与全参数微调相当的任务性能。

Abstract: When fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs), there has been success in minimizing both memory usage and computation with Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT), like Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA). In this article, we have explored whether this approach is transferable to the world of robotics and Reinforcement Learning (RL), allowing learning with reduced memory usage and improved computational performance. Specifically, we focused on a version of multi-task robotics, where a library of specialist policies are created. In such a library memory efficiency is especially important. We used a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm and fine-tuned a baseline model to different tasks using LoRA. Our results demonstrate that, depending on the hyperparameters, LoRA can minimize memory usage by a factor of 20-160 compared to full fine-tuning of all layers. This implies a 90-95% storage saving when deploying a library of many (10-50) specialized policies, which can be the differentiating factor between being able to store the entire library in memory or having to use swap-memory in an applied robotics setting. At the same time, our results indicate that there is no significant difference in the success-rate between full fine-tuning and LoRA fine-tuning for the selected tasks.

</details>


### [269] [Cellular Predictions on the Move: What about Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25709)
*Natalia Vesselinova,Pauliina Ilmonen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过引入人口动态特征（潜在流量源数量及其移动性）作为额外数据，在高速路场景下使蜂窝负载预测精度提升约60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蜂窝负载预测研究主要专注于开发更强大的学习结构以提高预测精度，忽视了数据本身作为学习过程关键组成部分的重要性。作者假设，如果数据能够反映产生蜂窝负载的底层过程（而非仅依赖历史时序数据和基站周边外生信息），预测性能可获得实质性提升。

Method: 提出在传统移动数据流量历史时序基础上，额外表征人口动态特征——即潜在蜂窝流量源数量及其移动性。在罕有研究的高速路场景下进行实验验证。

Result: 仅使用人口动态数据即可使预测性能提升约60%。

Conclusion: 数据层面（特别是反映负载产生过程的人口动态信息）的改进对蜂窝负载预测具有显著价值，在高速路等动态场景下效果尤为突出。

Abstract: Mobile cellular load forecasting is native to network resource optimization and delivery of services with reliability, latency and quality guarantees. The mainstream of machine learning research in the area is focused primarily on developing powerful learning structures for improved prediction accuracy. The data used for forecasting traditionally belong to the cellular domain and at most contain exogenous information about the surroundings of the base stations. We approach the prediction task from the perspective of data as a vital component of any data learning process. We hypothesize that substantial improvements could be achieved when the data inform on the processes that create the cellular load. Specifically, we propose to characterize the population dynamics -- the potential number of cellular traffic sources and their mobility -- in addition to employing historical time series of mobile data traffic. We validate our hypothesis for the rarely examined highway scenario. Comprehensive experiments show forecasting improvements on the order of $60\%$ due to the use of these data alone.

</details>


### [270] [Black-Box Assisted Regression: Phase Transitions and Minimax Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25743)
*Yan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在仅能黑盒访问固定预训练模型 $f_0$ 且目标函数 $f^*$ 与 $f_0$ 在 $L_2(P_X)$ 上接近（未知半径 $\delta$）的非参数回归问题。给出了有限样本极小化最优刻画，揭示了在 $\delta_c(n) \asymp n^{-\beta/(2\beta+d)}$ 处的相变现象。提出安全残差估计器（Safe Residual Estimator），通过学习 $f_0$ 的修正项并利用留出验证回退机制避免负迁移，其风险达到极小化最优的领头项（加上验证选择代价）。合成数据和实际任务（CIFAR-100+CLIP、AG News+Qwen3-8B）验证了理论发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型常被用作固定黑盒预测器处理下游少样本任务，但其预测可能存在偏差，盲目信任不可靠。本文旨在严格刻画在仅能黑盒访问预训练模型的情况下，如何安全地利用其信息进行非参数回归，并避免负迁移（即性能不如直接使用黑盒预测器）。

Method: 1) 将问题建模为黑盒辅助非参数回归：有标签样本和黑盒预测器 $f_0$，目标 $f^*$ 与 $f_0$ 的 $L_2(P_X)$ 距离以未知半径 $\delta$ 为界。2) 推导有限样本极小化最优风险，揭示 $\delta_c(n)$ 处的相变。3) 提出安全残差估计器：在 $f_0$ 上学习残差修正项，残差头初始化为零（初始预测等于 $f_0$），通过留出验证选择是否回退到 $f_0$，从而避免负迁移。

Result: 极小化最优风险呈现相变：当 $\delta$ 小于临界值 $\delta_c(n)$ 时，主导风险为 $\delta^2$（受限于逼近误差）；当 $\delta$ 大于临界值时，主导风险为 $n^{-2\beta/(2\beta+d)}$（标准非参数率）。安全残差估计器的风险匹配极小化最优领头项，仅增加一个加性的验证选择代价。合成数据和CIFAR-100+CLIP、AG News+Qwen3-8B实验验证了相变现象和残差修正的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文在理论上完整刻画了黑盒辅助非参数回归的极小化最优风险与相变现象，提出的安全残差估计器在统计意义上保证了"安全"（不劣于直接使用黑盒模型），为实际中可靠地利用基础模型提供了理论指导和可操作的算法框架。

Abstract: Foundation models are often used as fixed black-box predictors for downstream tasks with limited labeled data, but their predictions may be biased and unsafe to trust blindly. We study this setting through black-box assisted nonparametric regression: a learner observes labeled samples and can query a fixed predictor $f_0$, while the target $f^*$ is close to $f_0$ in $L_2(P_X)$ up to an unknown radius $δ$. We give a finite-sample minimax characterization showing a phase transition at $δ_c(n) \asymp n^{-β/(2β+d)}$, with leading risk $\min\{δ^2, n^{-2β/(2β+d)}\}$. We then analyze a Safe Residual Estimator: it learns a correction around $f_0$, initializes the residual head at zero so the initial predictor equals $f_0$, and uses holdout selection to revert to $f_0$ when the learned correction is not supported by validation data. Here, "safe" means avoiding negative transfer, i.e., performing worse than the black-box predictor alone. The estimator matches the leading minimax term up to an additive validation-selection cost. Synthetic regression experiments verify the predicted phase transition, while CIFAR-100 with CLIP and AG News with Qwen3-8B provide practice-facing evidence that the same residual-correction tradeoff is useful beyond the formal squared-loss regression setting.

</details>


### [271] [Gradient-based inverse lithography for EUV masks via the waveguide method and a physics-informed neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25753)
*Vasiliy A. Es'kin,Egor V. Ivanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度的极紫外光刻掩模逆向光刻技术(ILT)，通过将可微波导方法和波导神经算子作为端到端物理引擎，利用自动微分恢复掩模吸收体的介电常数，在11.2nm波长下对2D和3D吸收体（TaBN, La, U）的实验验证了该方法能获得满足晶圆所需场分布的掩模结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对EUV掩模的逆向光刻技术需求，传统方法难以高效求解掩模结构以实现目标晶圆场分布，因此需要一种可端到端微分的新型计算框架。

Method: 提出一种将可微波导方法（differentiable waveguide method）和波导神经算子（WGNO）作为端到端物理引擎的框架，通过全正向衍射模型的自动微分来恢复掩模吸收体的介电常数，实现梯度驱动的逆向光刻优化。

Result: 在11.2nm波长下，针对TaBN、La、U等材料的2D和3D掩模吸收体的数值实验表明，该方法能够获得实现晶圆目标场分布的掩模结构。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于梯度的ILT方法可有效应用于EUV掩模设计，通过端到端可微框架实现掩模结构的逆向优化。

Abstract: Gradient-based inverse lithography technology~(ILT) for extreme ultraviolet~(EUV) masks is presented. A novel framework treats the differentiable waveguide method and the recently proposed waveguide neural operator~(WGNO) as end-to-end physics engines, recovering the permittivity of the absorber of the mask through automatic differentiation of the full forward diffraction model. Numerical experiments on realistic 2D and 3D absorbers of the mask (TaBN, La, U) at $λ{=}11.2$~nm show that the considered ILT methods make it possible to obtain a mask structure that achieves the desired field on the wafer.

</details>


### [272] [Bridging Spherical Black-Box Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25761)
*Johannes Ackermann,Stefano Peluchetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文统一了黑箱优化中的ES、CBO、OVI等方法，揭示其差异仅在于适应度聚合（控制锐度偏好）和共识范围（控制模态）两个设计选择，并据此提出ES-OVI和CBO-OVI混合优化器，在连续控制和语言模型合并任务中优于原始算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱优化中ES、CBO、OVI等方法虽各自被独立研究，但它们之间的理论联系尚未被充分探索，缺乏统一的框架来理解其本质差异和潜在协同效应。

Method: 1) 提出统一理论框架，将ES、CBO、OVI等方法归结为两个设计维度的差异：适应度聚合（控制对平坦/尖锐最优的偏好）和共识范围（控制单峰/多模态特性）；2) 基于此框架设计混合优化器：ES-OVI混合体可显式控制平坦极小值的偏好，CBO-OVI混合体结合参数方法的高维效率与粒子方法的多模态能力。

Result: 在标准BBO基准测试和高维运动控制任务中，混合方法在性能上优于其组成算法；ES-OVI混合体在连续控制任务中实现了性能与鲁棒性的可调节权衡；CBO-OVI混合体在有限评估预算下的语言模型合并任务中取得了有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架有效揭示了不同BBO方法的内在联系，混合优化器通过插值现有方法成功实现了性能提升和特性权衡，验证了该理论视角的实用价值。

Abstract: When gradient information is unavailable, black-box optimization (BBO) methods provide a practical alternative. While Evolution Strategies (ES), Consensus-Based Optimization (CBO), Optimization via Integration (OVI), and related methods have each been studied independently, their connections remain underexplored. We unify these approaches within a common theoretical framework, revealing that they differ primarily in two design choices: fitness aggregation (controlling sharpness preference) and consensus scope (controlling modality). Leveraging these insights, we introduce hybrid optimizers that interpolate between existing methods. Our ES-OVI hybrid allows explicit control over the preference for flat minima, enabling a trade-off between performance and robustness in continuous control tasks. Our CBO-OVI hybrids combine the higher-dimensional efficiency of parametric methods with the multimodal capabilities of particle-based approaches, achieving competitive results on language model merging under limited evaluation budgets. We validate our methods on standard BBO benchmarks and higher-dimensional locomotion tasks, demonstrating that the hybrid methods can outperform their constituent algorithms.

</details>


### [273] [OncoSynth: Synthetic data generation for treatment effect estimation in oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25762)
*Octavia-Andreea Ciora,Julian Welzel,Dennis Frauen,Maresa Schröder,Marie Brockschmidt,Harry Amad,Thomas Callender,Mihaela van der Schaar,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OncoSynth是一个基于扩散模型的因果感知生成框架，可生成保留因果关系的合成患者队列，在肺癌和乳腺癌数据上，将群体级治疗效果估计误差降低66%，患者级降低58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤学中患者级数据常受访问限制，现有合成数据生成方法未能保留协变量、治疗和结局之间的因果关系，导致治疗效果估计存在偏差。

Method: 提出OncoSynth框架，采用基于扩散的序列化方法建模协变量如何影响治疗分配以及治疗如何影响生存，实现因果感知的合成数据生成。

Result: 在大规模肺癌（N=37,128）和乳腺癌（N=17,046）队列中，OncoSynth生成了高保真合成队列，保持真实分布；群体级治疗效果误差降低66%，患者级治疗效果误差降低58%。

Conclusion: OncoSynth能够在数据共享受限的环境下，为精准肿瘤学提供可靠的治疗效果证据生成支持。

Abstract: In oncology, access to patient-level data is often restricted. Synthetic data provides an alternative for analyzing treatment effectiveness, but existing methods for synthetic data generation fail to preserve the causal relationships between covariates, treatments, and outcomes, thereby leading to biased estimates of treatment effects. Here, we introduce OncoSynth, a generative, causally-aware machine learning framework designed to produce synthetic cohorts that enable accurate estimation of population- and patient-level treatment effects. OncoSynth uses a diffusion-based sequential approach to model how covariates influence treatment assignment and how treatment affects survival. We evaluate OncoSynth using large lung (N = 37,128) and breast cancer (N = 17,046) cohorts. Our results show that OncoSynth generates high-fidelity synthetic patient cohorts that preserve real-world patient, treatment, and outcome distributions. Notably, OncoSynth improves treatment effect estimation over existing approaches, by reducing population-level treatment effect error by up to 66%, and patient-level treatment effect error by up to 58%. Thereby, OncoSynth supports reliable evidence generation for precision oncology in settings where data sharing is restricted.

</details>


### [274] [Deep Neural Networks with Ordinal Loss for Medical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25769)
*Tal Dvora,Rotem Haba,Gonen Singer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出序数交叉熵（OCE）框架，通过序数代价矩阵扩展标准交叉熵，以纳入分类间的有序结构和非对称误分类代价，在保持概率解释和优化优势的同时，实现更平滑的优化动态和更好的序数一致性，在基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学预测中目标标签具有内在序数结构，误分类代价通常非均匀且不对称（远距离类别误分类代价远大于相邻类别，高估与低估代价不同），而传统损失函数（如多类交叉熵）平等对待所有误分类，无法利用序数信息。

Method: 提出Ordinal Cross-Entropy（OCE）框架，一种与架构无关的序数数据学习方法。通过序数代价矩阵扩展标准交叉熵，在保留概率解释和优化优势的前提下，将误分类严重度纳入损失函数。对OCE梯度行为进行了理论分析。

Result: 在基准数据集上，OCE相比现有最先进的序数方法实现了更低的预测误差成本和更好的校准性能。

Conclusion: OCE是一种简单而有效的深度神经网络序数回归解决方案，具有更平滑的优化动力学和更好的序数一致性。

Abstract: In many prediction problems in medical applications, target labels exhibit an inherent ordinal structure, where class ordering reflects clinically meaningful severity levels. The cost associated with misclassification is often non-uniform and asymmetric, as errors between distant ordinal categories may have substantially more severe consequences than errors between adjacent ones, and overestimating disease severity may have different clinical implications than underestimating it.
